#include <iostream>

#include "StudentModule.h"
#include "ProjectModule.h"
#include "MatchingModule.h"
#include "GraphModule.h"
#include "TeamModule.h"
#include "DataStorage.h"

using namespace std;

int main()
{
    StudentModule studentModule;
    ProjectModule projectModule;
    GraphModule graphModule;
    MatchingModule matchingModule;
    TeamModule teamModule;
    DataStorage storage;

    /*
       Some initial skill relationships.

       These are examples only.
       New relationships can be added later.
    */

    graphModule.addSkillRelationship(
        "C", "C++"
    );

    graphModule.addSkillRelationship(
        "Python", "Django"
    );

    graphModule.addSkillRelationship(
        "Python", "Flask"
    );

    graphModule.addSkillRelationship(
        "JavaScript", "React"
    );

    graphModule.addSkillRelationship(
        "JavaScript", "Node.js"
    );

    graphModule.addSkillRelationship(
        "HTML", "HTML5"
    );

    graphModule.addSkillRelationship(
        "CSS", "Tailwind CSS"
    );

    graphModule.addSkillRelationship(
        "CSS", "Bootstrap"
    );

    graphModule.addSkillRelationship(
        "SQL", "MySQL"
    );

    graphModule.addSkillRelationship(
        "SQL", "PostgreSQL"
    );

    int choice;

    do
    {
        cout << "\n\n========================================";
        cout << "\n             SKILLMATCH";
        cout << "\n========================================";

        cout << "\n1. Register Student";
        cout << "\n2. Display Students";

        cout << "\n3. Create Project";
        cout << "\n4. Display Projects";

        cout << "\n5. Display Skill Relationships";

        cout << "\n6. Find Student Matches";

        cout << "\n7. Form Suggested Team";

        cout << "\n8. Save Data";

        cout << "\n0. Exit";

        cout << "\n\nEnter your choice: ";
        cin >> choice;

        switch(choice)
        {
            case 1:
                studentModule.addStudent();
                break;

            case 2:
                studentModule.displayAllStudents();
                break;

            case 3:
                projectModule.addProject();
                break;

            case 4:
                projectModule.displayAllProjects();
                break;

            case 5:
                graphModule.displayGraph();
                break;

            case 6:
            {
                int projectId;

                cout << "\nEnter Project ID: ";
                cin >> projectId;

                Project* project =
                    projectModule.findProjectById(
                        projectId
                    );

                if(project == NULL)
                {
                    cout << "\nProject not found.\n";
                }
                else
                {
                    matchingModule.findMatches(
                        studentModule,
                        *project,
                        graphModule
                    );
                }

                break;
            }

            case 7:
            {
                int projectId;

                cout << "\nEnter Project ID: ";
                cin >> projectId;

                Project* project =
                    projectModule.findProjectById(
                        projectId
                    );

                if(project == NULL)
                {
                    cout << "\nProject not found.\n";
                }
                else
                {
                    teamModule.formTeam(
                        studentModule,
                        *project,
                        graphModule
                    );
                }

                break;
            }

            case 8:

                storage.saveStudents(
                    studentModule,
                    "students.txt"
                );

                storage.saveProjects(
                    projectModule,
                    "projects.txt"
                );

                break;

            case 0:
                cout << "\nThank you for using SkillMatch!\n";
                break;

            default:
                cout << "\nInvalid choice.\n";
        }

    } while(choice != 0);

    return 0;
}

#ifndef STUDENTMODULE_H
#define STUDENTMODULE_H

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <string>

using namespace std;

class Student
{
private:
    int id;
    string name;
    string email;
    vector<string> skills;
    vector<string> interests;
    string availability;

public:
    Student();

    Student(int id, string name, string email,
            vector<string> skills,
            vector<string> interests,
            string availability);

    void inputStudent();
    void displayStudent() const;

    int getId() const;
    string getName() const;
    string getEmail() const;
    vector<string> getSkills() const;
    vector<string> getInterests() const;
    string getAvailability() const;

    void setSkills(vector<string> newSkills);
};

class StudentModule
{
private:
    vector<Student> students;

public:
    void addStudent();
    void displayAllStudents() const;
    Student* findStudentById(int id);

    vector<Student> getStudents() const;
};

#endif

#include "StudentModule.h"
#include <limits>
using namespace std;
Student::Student()
{
    id = 0;
    name = "";
    email = "";
    availability = "";
}

Student::Student(int id, string name, string email,
                 vector<string> skills,
                 vector<string> interests,
                 string availability)
{
    this->id = id;
    this->name = name;
    this->email = email;
    this->skills = skills;
    this->interests = interests;
    this->availability = availability;
}


void Student::inputStudent()
{
    cout << "\nEnter Student ID: ";
    cin >> id;
    cin.ignore(numeric_limits<streamsize>::max(), '\n');

    cout << "Enter Name: ";
    getline(cin, name);

    cout << "Enter Email: ";
    getline(cin, email);

    int n;

    cout << "Enter number of skills: ";
    cin >> n;
    cin.ignore(numeric_limits<streamsize>::max(), '\n');

    skills.clear();

    for(int i = 0; i < n; i++)
    {
        string skill;

        cout << "Enter skill " << i + 1 << ": ";
        getline(cin, skill);

        skills.push_back(skill);
    }

    cout << "Enter number of interests: ";
    cin >> n;
    cin.ignore(numeric_limits<streamsize>::max(), '\n');

    interests.clear();

    for(int i = 0; i < n; i++)
    {
        string interest;

        cout << "Enter interest " << i + 1 << ": ";
        getline(cin, interest);

        interests.push_back(interest);
    }

    cout << "Enter availability: ";
    getline(cin, availability);
}


void Student::displayStudent() const
{
    cout << "\n-----------------------------";
    cout << "\nStudent ID: " << id;
    cout << "\nName: " << name;
    cout << "\nEmail: " << email;

    cout << "\nSkills: ";

    for(size_t i = 0; i < skills.size(); i++)
    {
        cout << skills[i];

        if(i != skills.size() - 1)
            cout << ", ";
    }

    cout << "\nInterests: ";

    for(size_t i = 0; i < interests.size(); i++)
    {
        cout << interests[i];

        if(i != interests.size() - 1)
            cout << ", ";
    }

    cout << "\nAvailability: " << availability;
    cout << "\n-----------------------------\n";
}

int Student::getId() const
{
    return id;
}

string Student::getName() const
{
    return name;
}

string Student::getEmail() const
{
    return email;
}

vector<string> Student::getSkills() const
{
    return skills;
}

vector<string> Student::getInterests() const
{
    return interests;
}

string Student::getAvailability() const
{
    return availability;
}

void Student::setSkills(vector<string> newSkills)
{
    skills = newSkills;
}


// StudentModule

void StudentModule::addStudent()
{
    Student s;

    s.inputStudent();

    students.push_back(s);

    cout << "\nStudent added successfully!\n";
}

void StudentModule::displayAllStudents() const
{
    if(students.empty())
    {
        cout << "\nNo students registered.\n";
        return;
    }

    for(size_t i = 0; i < students.size(); i++)
    {
        students[i].displayStudent();
    }
}

Student* StudentModule::findStudentById(int id)
{
    for(size_t i = 0; i < students.size(); i++)
    {
        if(students[i].getId() == id)
        {
            return &students[i];
        }
    }

    return NULL;
}

vector<Student> StudentModule::getStudents() const
{
    return students;
}

Project::Project()
{
    projectId = 0;
    title = "";
    description = "";
    teamSize = 0;
    deadline = "";
}

void Project::inputProject()
{
    cout << "\nEnter Project ID: ";
    cin >> projectId;
    cin.ignore();

    cout << "Enter Project Title: ";
    getline(cin, title);

    cout << "Enter Project Description: ";
    getline(cin, description);

    int n;

    cout << "Enter number of required skills: ";
    cin >> n;
    cin.ignore();

    requiredSkills.clear();

    for(int i = 0; i < n; i++)
    {
        string skill;

        cout << "Enter required skill " << i + 1 << ": ";
        getline(cin, skill);

        requiredSkills.push_back(skill);
    }

    cout << "Enter required team size: ";
    cin >> teamSize;
    cin.ignore();

    cout << "Enter project deadline: ";
    getline(cin, deadline);
}

void Project::displayProject() const
{
    cout << "\n=============================";
    cout << "\nProject ID: " << projectId;
    cout << "\nTitle: " << title;
    cout << "\nDescription: " << description;

    cout << "\nRequired Skills: ";

    for(size_t i = 0; i < requiredSkills.size(); i++)
    {
        cout << requiredSkills[i];

        if(i != requiredSkills.size() - 1)
            cout << ", ";
    }

    cout << "\nTeam Size: " << teamSize;
    cout << "\nDeadline: " << deadline;
    cout << "\n=============================\n";
}

// ProjectModule

void ProjectModule::addProject()
{
    Project p;

    p.inputProject();

    projects.push_back(p);

    cout << "\nProject created successfully!\n";
}

void ProjectModule::displayAllProjects() const
{
    if(projects.empty())
    {
        cout << "\nNo projects available.\n";
        return;
    }

    for(size_t i = 0; i < projects.size(); i++)
    {
        projects[i].displayProject();
    }
}

Project* ProjectModule::findProjectById(int id)
{
    for(size_t i = 0; i < projects.size(); i++)
    {
        if(projects[i].getProjectId() == id)
        {
            return &projects[i];
        }
    }

    return NULL;
}

vector<Project> ProjectModule::getProjects() const
{
    return projects;
}

#ifndef GRAPHMODULE_H
#define GRAPHMODULE_H

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <string>

using namespace std;

class GraphModule
{
private:
    map<string, vector<string> > skillGraph;

public:
    void addSkillRelationship(string skill1, string skill2);

    bool areRelated(string skill1, string skill2) const;

    void displayGraph() const;

    vector<string> getRelatedSkills(string skill) const;
};

#endif


================================================================================
FILE: cpp/GraphModule.cpp (72 lines)
================================================================================
#include "GraphModule.h"

void GraphModule::addSkillRelationship(string skill1, string skill2)
{
    skillGraph[skill1].push_back(skill2);
    skillGraph[skill2].push_back(skill1);
}

bool GraphModule::areRelated(string skill1, string skill2) const
{
    if(skill1 == skill2)
        return true;

    map<string, vector<string> >::const_iterator it;

    it = skillGraph.find(skill1);

    if(it == skillGraph.end())
        return false;

    vector<string> related = it->second;

    for(size_t i = 0; i < related.size(); i++)
    {
        if(related[i] == skill2)
            return true;
    }

    return false;
}

vector<string> GraphModule::getRelatedSkills(string skill) const
{
    map<string, vector<string> >::const_iterator it;

    it = skillGraph.find(skill);

    if(it != skillGraph.end())
        return it->second;

    return vector<string>();
}

void GraphModule::displayGraph() const
{
    if(skillGraph.empty())
    {
        cout << "\nNo skill relationships available.\n";
        return;
    }

    cout << "\n========== SKILL RELATIONSHIP GRAPH ==========\n";

    map<string, vector<string> >::const_iterator it;

    for(it = skillGraph.begin(); it != skillGraph.end(); ++it)
    {
        cout << it->first << " -> ";

        for(size_t i = 0; i < it->second.size(); i++)
        {
            cout << it->second[i];

            if(i != it->second.size() - 1)
                cout << ", ";
        }

        cout << endl;
    }

    cout << "==============================================\n";
}


================================================================================
FILE: cpp/MatchingModule.h (55 lines)
================================================================================
#ifndef MATCHINGMODULE_H
#define MATCHINGMODULE_H

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <queue>
#include <string>

#include "StudentModule.h"
#include "ProjectModule.h"
#include "GraphModule.h"

using namespace std;

struct Match
{
    int studentId;
    string studentName;
    double score;
};

struct CompareMatch
{
    bool operator()(const Match& a, const Match& b)
    {
        return a.score < b.score;
    }
};

class MatchingModule
{
private:

    int compareSkill(string studentSkill,
                     string requiredSkill,
                     const GraphModule& graph) const;

    double calculateScore(const Student& student,
                          const Project& project,
                          const GraphModule& graph) const;

public:

    void findMatches(const StudentModule& students,
                     const Project& project,
                     const GraphModule& graph) const;

    vector<Match> getMatches(const StudentModule& students,
                             const Project& project,
                             const GraphModule& graph) const;

    void displayMatch(const Match& match) const;
};

#endif


================================================================================
FILE: cpp/MatchingModule.cpp (173 lines)
================================================================================
#include "MatchingModule.h"

int MatchingModule::compareSkill(string studentSkill,
                                 string requiredSkill,
                                 const GraphModule& graph) const
{
    // Exact match
    if(studentSkill == requiredSkill)
    {
        return 100;
    }

    // Related match
    if(graph.areRelated(studentSkill, requiredSkill))
    {
        return 75;
    }

    return 0;
}


double MatchingModule::calculateScore(const Student& student,
                                      const Project& project,
                                      const GraphModule& graph) const
{
    vector<string> studentSkills = student.getSkills();
    vector<string> requiredSkills = project.getRequiredSkills();

    if(requiredSkills.empty())
        return 0;

    double totalScore = 0;

    for(size_t i = 0; i < requiredSkills.size(); i++)
    {
        string requirement = requiredSkills[i];

        /*
           Supports OR requirements.

           Example:
           "C|Python"

           means C OR Python.
        */

        vector<string> options;

        string current = "";

        for(size_t j = 0; j <= requirement.length(); j++)
        {
            if(j == requirement.length() || requirement[j] == '|')
            {
                if(current != "")
                    options.push_back(current);

                current = "";
            }
            else
            {
                current += requirement[j];
            }
        }

        int bestScore = 0;

        for(size_t j = 0; j < options.size(); j++)
        {
            for(size_t k = 0; k < studentSkills.size(); k++)
            {
                int score = compareSkill(
                    studentSkills[k],
                    options[j],
                    graph
                );

                if(score > bestScore)
                    bestScore = score;
            }
        }

        totalScore += bestScore;
    }

    return totalScore / requiredSkills.size();
}


vector<Match> MatchingModule::getMatches(
    const StudentModule& students,
    const Project& project,
    const GraphModule& graph) const
{
    vector<Match> result;

    vector<Student> studentList = students.getStudents();

    for(size_t i = 0; i < studentList.size(); i++)
    {
        double score = calculateScore(
            studentList[i],
            project,
            graph
        );

        if(score > 0)
        {
            Match m;

            m.studentId = studentList[i].getId();
            m.studentName = studentList[i].getName();
            m.score = score;

            result.push_back(m);
        }
    }

    return result;
}


void MatchingModule::findMatches(
    const StudentModule& students,
    const Project& project,
    const GraphModule& graph) const
{
    vector<Match> matches =
        getMatches(students, project, graph);

    if(matches.empty())
    {
        cout << "\nNo matching students found.\n";
        return;
    }

    priority_queue<
        Match,
        vector<Match>,
        CompareMatch
    > pq;

    for(size_t i = 0; i < matches.size(); i++)
    {
        pq.push(matches[i]);
    }

    cout << "\n========================================";
    cout << "\n       RANKED STUDENT MATCHES";
    cout << "\n========================================\n";

    int rank = 1;

    while(!pq.empty())
    {
        Match m = pq.top();
        pq.pop();

        cout << "\nRank " << rank;
        displayMatch(m);

        rank++;
    }
}


void MatchingModule::displayMatch(const Match& match) const
{
    cout << "\nStudent ID   : " << match.studentId;
    cout << "\nStudent Name : " << match.studentName;
    cout << "\nMatch Score  : " << match.score << "%\n";
}

FILE: cpp/TeamModule.cpp (99 lines)

#include "TeamModule.h"

void TeamModule::formTeam(
    const StudentModule& students,
    const Project& project,
    const GraphModule& graph) const
{
    MatchingModule matcher;

    vector<Match> matches =
        matcher.getMatches(
            students,
            project,
            graph
        );

    if(matches.empty())
    {
        cout << "\nUnable to form team.";
        cout << "\nNo matching students available.\n";
        return;
    }

    vector<Student> allStudents =
        students.getStudents();

    vector<Student> team;

    int requiredSize =
        project.getTeamSize();

    /*
       Select students based on highest
       matching score.
    */

    for(size_t i = 0; i < matches.size(); i++)
    {
        if((int)team.size() >= requiredSize)
            break;

        for(size_t j = 0; j < allStudents.size(); j++)
        {
            if(allStudents[j].getId() ==
               matches[i].studentId)
            {
                team.push_back(allStudents[j]);
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    displayTeam(team, project);
}


void TeamModule::displayTeam(
    const vector<Student>& team,
    const Project& project) const
{
    cout << "\n======================================";
    cout << "\n          SUGGESTED TEAM";
    cout << "\n======================================";

    cout << "\nProject: "
         << project.getTitle();

    cout << "\nRequired Team Size: "
         << project.getTeamSize();

    cout << "\nSelected Members: "
         << team.size();

    for(size_t i = 0; i < team.size(); i++)
    {
        cout << "\n\nMember " << i + 1;

        cout << "\nID: "
             << team[i].getId();

        cout << "\nName: "
             << team[i].getName();

        cout << "\nSkills: ";

        vector<string> skills =
            team[i].getSkills();

        for(size_t j = 0; j < skills.size(); j++)
        {
            cout << skills[j];

            if(j != skills.size() - 1)
                cout << ", ";
        }
    }

    cout << "\n\n======================================\n";
}

#include "DataStorage.h"

void DataStorage::saveStudents(const StudentModule& students, string filename)
{
    ofstream outFile(filename.c_str());
    if(!outFile.is_open())
    {
        cout << "
Error: Unable to open " << filename << " for writing.
";
        return;
    }

    vector<Student> list = students.getStudents();
    for(size_t i = 0; i < list.size(); i++)
    {
        outFile << "Student ID: " << list[i].getId() << "
";
        outFile << "Name: " << list[i].getName() << "
";
        outFile << "Email: " << list[i].getEmail() << "
";
        outFile << "Skills: ";
        vector<string> sk = list[i].getSkills();
        for(size_t j = 0; j < sk.size(); j++)
        {
            outFile << sk[j] << (j + 1 < sk.size() ? ", " : "");
        }
        outFile << "
Interests: ";
        vector<string> intr = list[i].getInterests();
        for(size_t j = 0; j < intr.size(); j++)
        {
            outFile << intr[j] << (j + 1 < intr.size() ? ", " : "");
        }
        outFile << "
Availability: " << list[i].getAvailability() << "
";
        outFile << "-----------------------------------------
";
    }

    outFile.close();
    cout << "
Students saved successfully to " << filename << "!
";
}

void DataStorage::saveProjects(const ProjectModule& projects, string filename)
{
    ofstream outFile(filename.c_str());
    if(!outFile.is_open())
    {
        cout << "
Error: Unable to open " << filename << " for writing.
";
        return;
    }

    vector<Project> list = projects.getProjects();
    for(size_t i = 0; i < list.size(); i++)
    {
        outFile << "Project ID: " << list[i].getProjectId() << "
";
        outFile << "Title: " << list[i].getTitle() << "
";
        outFile << "Description: " << list[i].getDescription() << "
";
        outFile << "Required Skills: ";
        vector<string> req = list[i].getRequiredSkills();
        for(size_t j = 0; j < req.size(); j++)
        {
            outFile << req[j] << (j + 1 < req.size() ? ", " : "");
        }
        outFile << "
Team Size: " << list[i].getTeamSize() << "
";
        outFile << "Deadline: " << list[i].getDeadline() << "
";
        outFile << "=========================================
";
    }

    outFile.close();
    cout << "
Projects saved successfully to " << filename << "!
";
}

# SkillMatch — Student Skill Exchange & Project Team Platform

**Graphic Era Deemed to be University • Course B.Tech DSCPP-III-2026-T383**  
*Website created by ShishirTB4146*

---

## Quick Start (Run Locally on Your Computer)

1. **Install Node.js** (v18+ recommended) from [nodejs.org](https://nodejs.org/).
2. **Extract this ZIP archive** and open a terminal inside the project folder:
   ```bash
   cd SkillMatch-Source-Code
   ```
3. **Install dependencies**:
   ```bash
   npm install
   ```
4. **Start the development server**:
   ```bash
   npm run dev
   ```
5. Open **`http://localhost:3000`** in your browser.

---

## Portal Access & Admin Credentials

The Opening Home Page provides three role portals:

1. **Be a Team Member**: Register a new student account with your skills and interests (or sign in to an existing student account). Automatically matches you with all 105+ project teams using the C++ Graph & Priority Queue engine.
2. **Be a Team Leader**: Create a new project & team with full technical specifications (or sign in as an existing team leader, e.g., `shreya.yadav.cs@geu.ac.in` for Team Sigma).
3. **Admin Control Portal**:
   - **Username**: `adminsm$4146`
   - **Password**: `adminsrsad@414.`

---

## Project Structure

```text
├── index.html                  # HTML entry point & Google Fonts (Caveat, Dancing Script)
├── package.json                # Dependencies & scripts (React 19, Vite, Tailwind CSS, Lucide)
├── tsconfig.json               # TypeScript configuration
├── vite.config.ts              # Vite bundler configuration
└── src/
    ├── main.tsx                # React DOM root
    ├── index.css               # Tailwind CSS v4 & signature font utilities
    ├── App.tsx                 # Main layout, navigation routing, modals & persistent footer
    ├── context/
    │   └── AppContext.tsx      # Global state, localStorage persistence & role management
    ├── lib/
    │   ├── types.ts            # TypeScript interfaces for Students, Teams, Applications, Logs
    │   ├── initialData.ts      # 105+ project teams (Team Sigma led by Shreya Yadav) & student profiles
    │   ├── graphEngine.ts      # C++ STL Adjacency List Graph & Priority Queue matchmaking engine
    │   ├── portalAuthEngine.ts # Private account/activity vault & deep team technical specs generator
    │   ├── assessmentEngine.ts # Proctored C/C++ skill assessment & anti-cheat engine
    │   ├── learningPathEngine.ts # C++ Beginner-to-Project-Ready learning path engine
    │   └── streakEngine.ts     # Daily coding & collaboration streak tracker engine
    └── components/
        ├── HomePage.tsx                # Opening page with 3 role portals, live metrics & 105 teams
        ├── Navbar.tsx                  # Top navigation bar
        ├── TeamDetailPage.tsx          # Dedicated full-page deep project architecture & roster view
        ├── TeamExplorer.tsx            # Searchable/filterable directory of all 105+ teams
        ├── CandidateDashboard.tsx      # Student dashboard with matched teams & learning path
        ├── CandidateLearningPath.tsx   # Interactive C++ learning path visualizer
        ├── CandidateStreakTracker.tsx  # Daily activity streak & milestone tracker
        ├── TeamLeaderDashboard.tsx     # Team leader project & applicant management portal
        ├── AdminDashboard.tsx          # Supreme Admin control panel for all 105+ teams & accounts
        ├── SkillAssessment.tsx         # Proctored C/C++ assessment environment
        ├── CppVisualizer.tsx           # Interactive C++ Graph & Priority Queue algorithm visualizer
        ├── ApplyTeamModal.tsx          # Team application modal
        ├── ApplicantChatModal.tsx      # Real-time 1-on-1 leader/applicant chat modal
        ├── StudentProfileModal.tsx     # Student profile inspector
        ├── StudentRegistrationModal.tsx# Quick student registration modal
        ├── LeaderInvitationPopup.tsx   # Real-time team leader notification popup
        └── UserAvatar.tsx              # Emoji & avatar badge component
```

    plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
    resolve: {
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, '.'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
      // Do not modify—file watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',
      // Disable file watching when DISABLE_HMR is true to save CPU during agent edits.
      watch: process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true' ? null : {},

  };
});

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { AppProvider, useApp } from './context/AppContext';
import { Navbar } from './components/Navbar';
import { HomePage } from './components/HomePage';
import { TeamExplorer } from './components/TeamExplorer';
import { SkillAssessment } from './components/SkillAssessment';
import { CandidateDashboard } from './components/CandidateDashboard';
import { TeamLeaderDashboard } from './components/TeamLeaderDashboard';
import { AdminDashboard } from './components/AdminDashboard';
import { CppVisualizer } from './components/CppVisualizer';
import { TeamDetailPage } from './components/TeamDetailPage';
import { ApplicantChatModal } from './components/ApplicantChatModal';
import { ApplyTeamModal } from './components/ApplyTeamModal';
import { StudentProfileModal } from './components/StudentProfileModal';
import { StudentRegistrationModal } from './components/StudentRegistrationModal';
import { LeaderInvitationPopup } from './components/LeaderInvitationPopup';
import { SourceCodeViewerModal } from './components/SourceCodeViewerModal';
import { Team, StudentProfile } from './lib/types';
import {
  GraduationCap,
  Sparkles,
  ShieldCheck,
  Heart,
  Github,
  BookOpen,
  Feather,
  Download,
  Code2,
} from 'lucide-react';

const MainLayout: React.FC = () => {
  const { role, currentUserProfile, siteSettings } = useApp();

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<string>('home');
  const [previousTab, setPreviousTab] = useState<string>('teams');
  const [selectedTeamIdForPage, setSelectedTeamIdForPage] = useState<string>('team-sigma');

  // Modals state
  const [activeChatAppId, setActiveChatAppId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [activeApplyTeam, setActiveApplyTeam] = useState<Team | null>(null);
  const [viewingProfile, setViewingProfile] = useState<StudentProfile | null>(null);
  const [showRegisterModal, setShowRegisterModal] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [showSourceCodeModal, setShowSourceCodeModal] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const handleOpenTeamPage = (teamOrId: Team | string) => {
    const id = typeof teamOrId === 'string' ? teamOrId : teamOrId.id;
    setSelectedTeamIdForPage(id);
    if (activeTab !== 'team-detail') {
      setPreviousTab(activeTab);
    }
    setActiveTab('team-detail');
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 flex flex-col font-sans selection:bg-indigo-500 selection:text-white">
      {/* Top Navbar */}
      <Navbar
        activeTab={activeTab}
        setActiveTab={setActiveTab}
        onOpenRegister={() => setShowRegisterModal(true)}
        onOpenProfile={() => {
          if (currentUserProfile) setViewingProfile(currentUserProfile);
        }}
      />

      {/* Site-Wide Maintenance Banner */}
      {siteSettings.maintenanceMode && (
        <div className="bg-rose-600 text-white text-xs font-bold py-2 px-4 text-center">
          ⚠️ System Maintenance Mode Active: Application submissions are currently locked by the university administrator.
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Official Announcement Strip (shown only inside dashboards) */}
      {activeTab !== 'home' && siteSettings.announcementBanner && !siteSettings.maintenanceMode && (
        <div className="bg-indigo-50 border-b border-indigo-100 text-indigo-900 text-xs py-1.5 px-4 text-center font-medium">
          📢 {siteSettings.announcementBanner}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Main Content Area */}
      <main className="flex-1 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-6">
        {activeTab === 'home' && (
          <HomePage
            onNavigate={(tab) => setActiveTab(tab)}
            onOpenRegister={() => setShowRegisterModal(true)}
            onOpenApplyModal={(team) => setActiveApplyTeam(team)}
            onOpenTeamPage={handleOpenTeamPage}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === 'teams' && (
          <TeamExplorer
            onOpenApplyModal={(team) => setActiveApplyTeam(team)}
            onOpenChatModal={(appId) => setActiveChatAppId(appId)}
            onOpenTeamPage={handleOpenTeamPage}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === 'team-detail' && (
          <TeamDetailPage
            teamId={selectedTeamIdForPage}
            onBack={() => setActiveTab(previousTab || 'teams')}
            onSelectTeam={(id) => {
              setSelectedTeamIdForPage(id);
              if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
                window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
              }
            }}
            onOpenApplyModal={(team) => setActiveApplyTeam(team)}
            onOpenChatModal={(appId) => setActiveChatAppId(appId)}
            onInspectProfile={(profile) => setViewingProfile(profile)}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === 'assessment' && <SkillAssessment />}

        {activeTab === 'candidate-dashboard' && (
          <CandidateDashboard
            onOpenChat={(appId) => setActiveChatAppId(appId)}
            onOpenApplyModal={(team) => setActiveApplyTeam(team)}
            onNavigateToAssessment={() => setActiveTab('assessment')}
            onOpenTeamPage={handleOpenTeamPage}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === 'leader-dashboard' && (
          <TeamLeaderDashboard
            onOpenChat={(appId) => setActiveChatAppId(appId)}
            onOpenTeamPage={handleOpenTeamPage}
          />
        )}

        {activeTab === 'admin' && (
          <AdminDashboard onOpenTeamPage={handleOpenTeamPage} />
        )}

        {activeTab === 'cpp-visualizer' && <CppVisualizer />}
      </main>

      {/* Persistent Footer */}
      <footer className="mt-auto bg-white border-t border-slate-200 py-6 text-xs text-slate-500">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-3 flex-wrap">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold">
                SM
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="font-bold text-slate-800">
                  SkillMatch: Student Skill Exchange & Project Team Platform
                </div>
                <div className="text-slate-500 text-[11px]">
                  Graphic Era Deemed to be University • Course B.Tech DSCPP-III-2026-T383
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowSourceCodeModal(true)}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-bold text-[11px] transition-colors shadow-2xs cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Code2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>View & Copy All Modules Code</span>
              </button>
              <a
                href="/SkillMatch-Full-Source-Code.zip"
                download="SkillMatch-Full-Source-Code.zip"
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-indigo-600 text-slate-700 hover:text-white font-semibold text-[11px] border border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-600 transition-colors shadow-2xs"
              >
                <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Download Full Website Code (.zip)</span>
              </a>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col items-center md:items-end gap-1.5 text-center md:text-right">
            <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
              Private Account & Activity Storage • Visible Only to Account Creator & Admin
            </div>
            <div
              data-testid="creator-signature"
              className="inline-flex flex-col items-center md:items-end group select-none"
            >
              <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-50/90 via-violet-50/90 to-amber-50/80 border border-indigo-200/70 shadow-2xs">
                <Feather className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-indigo-600 -rotate-12 shrink-0" />
                <span className="font-signature text-lg sm:text-xl font-bold tracking-wide text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-800 via-violet-700 to-indigo-950 -rotate-1">
                  website created by ShishirTB4146
                </span>
              </div>
              <svg
                viewBox="0 0 220 10"
                className="w-44 h-2 text-indigo-500/70 -mt-0.5"
                fill="none"
                 aria-hidden="true"
              >
                <path
                  d="M4 7C58 2 142 1.5 216 6.5"
                  stroke="currentColor"
                  strokeWidth="1.6"
                  strokeLinecap="round"
                />
                <path
                  d="M35 8.5C95 5 160 5 198 8"
                  stroke="currentColor"
                  strokeWidth="0.9"
                  strokeLinecap="round"
                  strokeOpacity="0.65"
                />
              </svg>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </footer>

      {/* Complete All-Modules Source Code Explorer Modal */}
      {showSourceCodeModal && (
        <SourceCodeViewerModal onClose={() => setShowSourceCodeModal(false)} />
      )}

      {/* Live 1-on-1 Applicant Chat Modal */}
      {activeChatAppId && (
        <ApplicantChatModal
          applicationId={activeChatAppId}
          onClose={() => setActiveChatAppId(null)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Team Invitation Application Modal */}
      {activeApplyTeam && (
        <ApplyTeamModal
          team={activeApplyTeam}
          onClose={() => setActiveApplyTeam(null)}
          onSuccess={() => {
            setActiveApplyTeam(null);
            setActiveTab('candidate-dashboard');
          }}
        />
      )}

      {/* Student Profile & Reporting Modal */}
      {viewingProfile && (
        <StudentProfileModal
          profile={viewingProfile}
          onClose={() => setViewingProfile(null)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Student Registration Modal */}
      {showRegisterModal && (
        <StudentRegistrationModal
          onClose={() => setShowRegisterModal(false)}
          onRegisteredSuccess={() => {
            setShowRegisterModal(false);
            setActiveTab('candidate-dashboard');
          }}
        />
      )}

      {/* Team Leader Real-time Application Popup */}
      <LeaderInvitationPopup onOpenChat={(appId) => setActiveChatAppId(appId)} />
    </div>
  );
};

export default function App() {
  return (
    <AppProvider>
      <MainLayout />
    </AppProvider>
  );
}

interface AdminDashboardProps {

export const AdminDashboard: React.FC<AdminDashboardProps> = ({ onOpenTeamPage }) => {
  const {

  // Inline Team Editor State
  const [isEditingTeam, setIsEditingTeam] = useState(false);
  const [editTeamTitle, setEditTeamTitle] = useState('');
  const [editTeamDept, setEditTeamDept] = useState('');
  const [editTeamCategory, setEditTeamCategory] = useState('');
  const [editTeamDesc, setEditTeamDesc] = useState('');
  const [editTeamSkills, setEditTeamSkills] = useState('');
  const [editTeamTags, setEditTeamTags] = useState('');
  const [editTeamSize, setEditTeamSize] = useState(4);
  const [editTeamDeadline, setEditTeamDeadline] = useState('');

  // Add Member to Selected Team State
  const [candidateToAddId, setCandidateToAddId] = useState<string>('');
  const [candidateToAddRole, setCandidateToAddRole] = useState<'Member' | 'Leader'>('Member');
  const [memberSearchQuery, setMemberSearchQuery] = useState('');

  // Admin Directive Chat Input
  const [directiveText, setDirectiveText] = useState('');
  const [confirmDeleteTeamId, setConfirmDeleteTeamId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [confirmDeleteUserId, setConfirmDeleteUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Selected Person in Private Vault
  const [selectedVaultUserId, setSelectedVaultUserId] = useState<string>(
    students[0]?.id || ''
  );
  const [isEditingUser, setIsEditingUser] = useState(false);
  const [editUserName, setEditUserName] = useState('');
  const [editUserEmail, setEditUserEmail] = useState('');
  const [editUserStudentId, setEditUserStudentId] = useState('');
  const [editUserPassword, setEditUserPassword] = useState('');
  const [editUserRole, setEditUserRole] = useState<'TEAM_MEMBER' | 'TEAM_LEADER'>('TEAM_MEMBER');
  const [editUserDept, setEditUserDept] = useState('');
  const [editUserYear, setEditUserYear] = useState('');
  const [editUserSkills, setEditUserSkills] = useState('');
  const [editUserInterests, setEditUserInterests] = useState('');

  // Admin Login Gate state if not authenticated
  const [adminUser, setAdminUser] = useState('');
  const [adminPass, setAdminPass] = useState('');
  const [showPass, setShowPass] = useState(false);
  const [authError, setAuthError] = useState('');

  const userTeamsMap = useMemo(() => getUserTeamsMap(teams), [teams]);

  if (!isAdminAuthenticated) {
    const handleAdminAuth = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      setAuthError('');
      const ok = loginAdmin(adminUser, adminPass);
      if (ok) {
        setActiveTab('TEAMS');
        if (teams[0]) {
          setSelectedTeamId(teams[0].id);
        }
      } else {
        setAuthError('Access Denied: Invalid Admin username or password.');
      }
    };

    return (
      <div className="max-w-md mx-auto my-12 bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 via-amber-950 to-slate-900 text-white p-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/20 border border-amber-400/40 flex items-center justify-center">
              <KeyRound className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-300" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-lg font-black">Supreme Admin Security Gate</h1>
              <p className="text-xs text-amber-200">
                Authenticate to control all teams, user login credentials & website policies
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleAdminAuth} className="p-6 space-y-4 text-xs">
          {authError && (
            <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-700 font-semibold">
              {authError}
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Admin Username *</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              placeholder="Enter Admin Username"
              value={adminUser}
              onChange={(e) => setAdminUser(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-mono"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Admin Password *</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <input
                type={showPass ? 'text' : 'password'}
                required
                placeholder="Enter Admin Password"
                value={adminPass}
                onChange={(e) => setAdminPass(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-2.5 pr-10 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-mono"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowPass(!showPass)}
                className="absolute right-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700"
              >
                {showPass ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            className="w-full py-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
          >
            <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Unlock Supreme Admin Control</span>
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const pendingReports = reports.filter((r) => r.status === 'PENDING');
  const openTeamsCount = teams.filter((t) => t.vacancyOpen && t.members.length < t.teamSize).length;
  const verifiedStudentsCount = students.filter((s) => s.assessmentStatus?.cppVerified).length;
  const teamLeadersCount = students.filter(
    (s) => s.accountType === 'TEAM_LEADER' || (userTeamsMap[s.id]?.ledTeams.length || 0) > 0
  ).length;

  const filteredStudents = students.filter((s) => {
    const q = studentSearch.trim().toLowerCase();
    const userTeams = userTeamsMap[s.id];
    const teamNames = [
      ...(userTeams?.ledTeams.map((t) => t.title) || []),
      ...(userTeams?.memberTeams.map((t) => t.title) || []),
    ]
      .join(' ')
      .toLowerCase();

    const matchesSearch =
      !q ||
      s.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      s.email.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      s.studentIdNum.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      (s.password || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      s.department.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      s.skills.some((sk) => sk.toLowerCase().includes(q)) ||
      teamNames.includes(q);

    if (!matchesSearch) return false;
    if (personRoleFilter === 'TEAM_LEADER') {
      return s.accountType === 'TEAM_LEADER' || (userTeams?.ledTeams.length || 0) > 0;
    }
    if (personRoleFilter === 'TEAM_MEMBER') {
      return s.accountType !== 'TEAM_LEADER' && (userTeams?.ledTeams.length || 0) === 0;
    }
    if (personRoleFilter === 'VERIFIED') {
      return Boolean(s.assessmentStatus?.cppVerified);
    }
    if (personRoleFilter === 'BANNED') {
      return Boolean(s.isBanned);
    }
    return true;
  });

  const filteredTeams = teams.filter((t) => {
    const q = teamSearch.trim().toLowerCase();
    const matchesSearch =
      !q ||
      t.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      t.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      t.department.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      t.category.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      t.leaderName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      t.leaderEmail.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      t.requiredSkills.some((sk) => sk.toLowerCase().includes(q)) ||
      t.members.some((m) => m.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || m.email.toLowerCase().includes(q));

    if (!matchesSearch) return false;
    const isOpen = t.vacancyOpen && t.members.length < t.teamSize;
    if (teamStatusFilter === 'OPEN') return isOpen;
    if (teamStatusFilter === 'CLOSED') return !isOpen;
    return true;
  });

  const selectedTeam =
    teams.find((t) => t.id === selectedTeamId) || filteredTeams[0] || teams[0] || null;

  const selectedTeamIntel = selectedTeam
    ? getTeamFullIntelligence(selectedTeam, students, applications)
    : null;

  const selectedVaultStudent =
    students.find((s) => s.id === selectedVaultUserId) ||
    filteredStudents[0] ||
    students[0] ||
    null;

  const selectedUserActivities = selectedVaultStudent
    ? activityLogs.filter(
        (l) =>
          l.actorId === selectedVaultStudent.id ||
          l.actor.toLowerCase() === selectedVaultStudent.name.toLowerCase() ||
          l.description.toLowerCase().includes(selectedVaultStudent.name.toLowerCase())
      )
    : [];

  const handleCopy = (text: string, label: string) => {
    navigator.clipboard?.writeText(text);
    setCopiedToken(label);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedToken(''), 1800);
  };

  const startEditingTeam = (team: typeof selectedTeam) => {
    if (!team) return;
    setEditTeamTitle(team.title);
    setEditTeamDept(team.department);
    setEditTeamCategory(team.category);
    setEditTeamDesc(team.description);
    setEditTeamSkills(team.requiredSkills.join(', '));
    setEditTeamTags(team.tags.join(', '));
    setEditTeamSize(team.teamSize);
    setEditTeamDeadline(team.deadline);
    setIsEditingTeam(true);
  };

  const saveTeamEdits = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!selectedTeam) return;
    adminUpdateTeam(selectedTeam.id, {
      title: editTeamTitle.trim() || selectedTeam.title,
      department: editTeamDept.trim() || selectedTeam.department,
      category: editTeamCategory.trim() || selectedTeam.category,
      description: editTeamDesc.trim() || selectedTeam.description,
      requiredSkills: editTeamSkills
        .split(',')
        .map((s) => s.trim())
        .filter(Boolean),
      tags: editTeamTags
        .split(',')
        .map((s) => s.trim())
        .filter(Boolean),
      teamSize: Math.max(1, Number(editTeamSize) || selectedTeam.teamSize),
      deadline: editTeamDeadline || selectedTeam.deadline,
    });
    setIsEditingTeam(false);
  };

  const startEditingUser = (stu: typeof selectedVaultStudent) => {
    if (!stu) return;
    setEditUserName(stu.name);
    setEditUserEmail(stu.email);
    setEditUserStudentId(stu.studentIdNum);
    setEditUserPassword(stu.password || '');
    setEditUserRole(stu.accountType || 'TEAM_MEMBER');
    setEditUserDept(stu.department);
    setEditUserYear(stu.year);
    setEditUserSkills(stu.skills.join(', '));
    setEditUserInterests((stu.interests || []).join(', '));
    setIsEditingUser(true);
  };

  const saveUserEdits = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!selectedVaultStudent) return;
    adminUpdateStudent(selectedVaultStudent.id, {
      name: editUserName.trim() || selectedVaultStudent.name,
      email: editUserEmail.trim() || selectedVaultStudent.email,
      studentIdNum: editUserStudentId.trim() || selectedVaultStudent.studentIdNum,
      password: editUserPassword.trim() || selectedVaultStudent.password,
      accountType: editUserRole,
      department: editUserDept.trim() || selectedVaultStudent.department,
      year: editUserYear.trim() || selectedVaultStudent.year,
      skills: editUserSkills
        .split(',')
        .map((s) => s.trim())
        .filter(Boolean),
      interests: editUserInterests
        .split(',')
        .map((s) => s.trim())
        .filter(Boolean),
    });
    setIsEditingUser(false);
  };

  const inspectPersonInVault = (personId: string) => {
    setSelectedVaultUserId(personId);
    setIsEditingUser(false);
    setActiveTab('PRIVATE_VAULTS');
  };

  const inspectTeamInCommandCenter = (teamId: string) => {
    setSelectedTeamId(teamId);
    setIsEditingTeam(false);
    setActiveTab('TEAMS');
  };

  const eligibleStudentsToAdd = selectedTeam
    ? students.filter(
        (s) =>
          !selectedTeam.members.some((m) => m.id === s.id) &&
          (!memberSearchQuery.trim() ||
            s.name.toLowerCase().includes(memberSearchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
            s.email.toLowerCase().includes(memberSearchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
            s.studentIdNum.includes(memberSearchQuery))
      )
    : [];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Supreme Admin Header Banner */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 via-amber-950 to-slate-900 text-white rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-md border border-amber-900/30">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <span className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/30">
                <ShieldAlert className="w-5 h-5" />
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-300">
                Graphic Era University • Supreme Administration & Total Platform Authority
              </span>
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-white tracking-tight">
              Supreme Admin Command Center: All Teams, Login Credentials & Controls
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 mt-1 max-w-3xl leading-relaxed">
              Click any team below to inspect every single team detail, view every member&apos;s login
              credentials, override rosters, edit requirements, or inspect all {students.length} user
              accounts and private activity vaults.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowAllPasswords(!showAllPasswords)}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 border border-amber-400/40 text-xs font-bold text-amber-200 transition-colors"
            >
              {showAllPasswords ? <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
              <span>{showAllPasswords ? 'Passwords Visible' : 'Reveal All Passwords'}</span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={logoutAdmin}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-rose-600 text-xs font-bold text-white transition-colors"
            >
              <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Lock Admin
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Global KPIs */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-6 gap-3 mt-6 pt-5 border-t border-slate-800 text-xs">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('TEAMS')}
            className="text-left p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 hover:bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <div className="text-slate-400">All University Teams</div>
            <div className="text-xl font-black text-amber-400 mt-0.5">{teams.length} Teams</div>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setTeamStatusFilter('OPEN');
              setActiveTab('TEAMS');
            }}
            className="text-left p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 hover:bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <div className="text-slate-400">Open Vacancy Teams</div>
            <div className="text-xl font-black text-emerald-400 mt-0.5">{openTeamsCount} Open</div>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setPersonRoleFilter('ALL');
              setActiveTab('PRIVATE_VAULTS');
            }}
            className="text-left p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 hover:bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <div className="text-slate-400">All People & Logins</div>
            <div className="text-xl font-black text-indigo-300 mt-0.5">
              {students.length} People
            </div>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setPersonRoleFilter('TEAM_LEADER');
              setActiveTab('PRIVATE_VAULTS');
            }}
            className="text-left p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 hover:bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <div className="text-slate-400">Team Leaders</div>
            <div className="text-xl font-black text-cyan-300 mt-0.5">{teamLeadersCount} Leaders</div>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setPersonRoleFilter('VERIFIED');
              setActiveTab('STUDENTS');
            }}
            className="text-left p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 hover:bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <div className="text-slate-400">Verified C/C++ Devs</div>
            <div className="text-xl font-black text-emerald-300 mt-0.5">
              {verifiedStudentsCount} Verified
            </div>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('REPORTS')}
            className="text-left p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 hover:bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <div className="text-slate-400">Dispute Reports</div>
            <div
              className={`text-xl font-black mt-0.5 ${
                pendingReports.length > 0 ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-slate-400'
              }`}
            >
              {pendingReports.length} Pending
            </div>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Admin Navigation Tabs — TEAMS IS FIRST */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap border-b border-slate-200 gap-3 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('TEAMS')}
          className={`pb-3 px-2 flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors border-b-2 ${
            activeTab === 'TEAMS'
              ? 'border-amber-600 text-amber-700'
              : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
          }`}
        >
          <Layers className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600" />
          1. All Teams & Team Control Center ({teams.length})
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('PRIVATE_VAULTS')}
          className={`pb-3 px-2 flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors border-b-2 ${
            activeTab === 'PRIVATE_VAULTS'
              ? 'border-amber-600 text-amber-700'
              : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
          }`}
        >
          <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600" />
          2. All People Login Credentials & Activity Vault ({students.length})
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('STUDENTS')}
          className={`pb-3 px-2 flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors border-b-2 ${
            activeTab === 'STUDENTS'
              ? 'border-amber-600 text-amber-700'
              : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
          }`}
        >
          <Users className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600" />
          3. Master Login & Profile Directory ({students.length})
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('REPORTS')}
          className={`pb-3 px-2 flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors border-b-2 ${
            activeTab === 'REPORTS'
              ? 'border-amber-600 text-amber-700'
              : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
          }`}
        >
          <ShieldAlert className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600" />
          Dispute Reports ({reports.length})
          {pendingReports.length > 0 && (
            <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-rose-600 text-white text-[11px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center">
              {pendingReports.length}
            </span>
          )}
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('ACTIVITIES')}
          className={`pb-3 px-2 flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors border-b-2 ${
            activeTab === 'ACTIVITIES'
              ? 'border-amber-600 text-amber-700'
              : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
          }`}
        >
          <Activity className="w-4 h-4" />
          Global Audit Logs ({activityLogs.length})
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('ASSESSMENTS')}
          className={`pb-3 px-2 flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors border-b-2 ${
            activeTab === 'ASSESSMENTS'
              ? 'border-amber-600 text-amber-700'
              : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
          }`}
        >
          <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600" />
          Anti-Cheat Submissions ({assessmentSubmissions.length})
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('SITE_CONTROLS')}
          className={`pb-3 px-2 flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors border-b-2 ${
            activeTab === 'SITE_CONTROLS'
              ? 'border-amber-600 text-amber-700'
              : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
          }`}
        >
          <Settings className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-700" />
          Site-Wide Controls
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* =====================================================================
          TAB 1 (DEFAULT ON ADMIN LOGIN): ALL TEAMS & DEEP TEAM COMMAND CENTER
         ===================================================================== */}
      {activeTab === 'TEAMS' && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6 items-start">
          {/* LEFT COLUMN (5 cols): Searchable List of All 105+ Teams */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-5 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-4 shadow-xs space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-sm font-black text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <FolderKanban className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600" />
                  All University Teams ({filteredTeams.length} / {teams.length})
                </h2>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                  Click any team to inspect all team details, member logins & full controls
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Search & Status Filters */}
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <div className="relative">
                <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Search team title, ID, leader, member, or skill..."
                  value={teamSearch}
                  onChange={(e) => setTeamSearch(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px]">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setTeamStatusFilter('ALL')}
                  className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg font-bold transition-colors ${
                    teamStatusFilter === 'ALL'
                      ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white'
                      : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  All ({teams.length})
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setTeamStatusFilter('OPEN')}
                  className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg font-bold transition-colors ${
                    teamStatusFilter === 'OPEN'
                      ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white'
                      : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-100'
                  }`}
                >
                  Open Vacancy ({openTeamsCount})
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setTeamStatusFilter('CLOSED')}
                  className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg font-bold transition-colors ${
                    teamStatusFilter === 'CLOSED'
                      ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white'
                      : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-100'
                  }`}
                >
                  Full / Closed ({teams.length - openTeamsCount})
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Scrollable Team Selector Cards */}
            <div className="space-y-2 max-h-[760px] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              {filteredTeams.map((team) => {
                const isSelected = selectedTeam?.id === team.id;
                const isOpen = team.vacancyOpen && team.members.length < team.teamSize;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={team.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setSelectedTeamId(team.id);
                      setIsEditingTeam(false);
                      setConfirmDeleteTeamId(null);
                    }}
                    className={`w-full text-left p-3.5 rounded-xl border transition-all space-y-2 ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-amber-50/90 border-amber-500 ring-2 ring-amber-500/20 shadow-xs'
                        : 'bg-slate-50/70 border-slate-200 hover:bg-white hover:border-slate-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <span className="font-mono text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-200 text-slate-700">
                            {team.id}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-indigo-700 truncate">
                            {team.category}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="font-black text-xs text-slate-900 mt-1 line-clamp-1">
                          {team.title}
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 truncate">
                          {team.department}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <span
                        className={`shrink-0 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${
                          isOpen
                            ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200'
                            : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-200'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {team.members.length}/{team.teamSize} {isOpen ? 'Open' : 'Closed'}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] pt-1 border-t border-slate-200/70">
                      <div className="truncate text-slate-700">
                        <span className="text-slate-400">Lead:</span>{' '}
                        <span className="font-bold">{team.leaderName}</span>
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-700">
                        Inspect & Control →
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* RIGHT COLUMN (7 cols): Deep Team Inspector & Full Per-Team Admin Controls */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-7 space-y-5">
            {selectedTeam && selectedTeamIntel ? (
              <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden">
                {/* Team Banner & Primary Controls */}
                <div
                  className={`bg-gradient-to-r ${selectedTeam.bannerGradient || 'from-slate-900 to-indigo-900'} text-white p-6`}
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-start justify-between gap-4">
                    <div className="space-y-1.5">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-black/30 backdrop-blur-xs font-mono text-[11px] font-bold">
                          ID: {selectedTeam.id}
                        </span>
                        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-white/20 text-[11px] font-bold">
                          {selectedTeam.category}
                        </span>
                        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-white/15 text-[11px]">
                          Created: {selectedTeam.createdAt}
                        </span>
                        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-white/15 text-[11px]">
                          Deadline: {selectedTeam.deadline}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <h2 className="text-xl font-black tracking-tight">{selectedTeam.title}</h2>
                      <p className="text-xs text-white/90">{selectedTeam.department}</p>
                    </div>

                    {/* Quick Status & Capacity Pill */}
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-2 shrink-0">
                      <span
                        className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-black border ${
                          selectedTeam.vacancyOpen &&
                          selectedTeam.members.length < selectedTeam.teamSize
                            ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-white border-emerald-300'
                            : 'bg-rose-600 text-white border-rose-400'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {selectedTeam.vacancyOpen &&
                        selectedTeam.members.length < selectedTeam.teamSize
                          ? `VACANCY OPEN (${selectedTeamIntel.capacityMetrics.openSpots} Spots Left)`
                          : 'VACANCY CLOSED / FULL'}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white/90">
                        Roster: {selectedTeam.members.length} / {selectedTeam.teamSize} Members (
                        {selectedTeamIntel.capacityMetrics.fillPercentage}%)
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Admin Master Controls Bar for This Team */}
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mt-5 pt-4 border-t border-white/20 text-xs">
                    {onOpenTeamPage && (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onOpenTeamPage(selectedTeam)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-400 text-slate-950 hover:bg-amber-300 font-black shadow-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <FolderKanban className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        Open Full Project Page
                      </button>
                    )}

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        isEditingTeam ? setIsEditingTeam(false) : startEditingTeam(selectedTeam)
                      }
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white text-slate-900 hover:bg-amber-100 font-bold shadow-xs transition-colors"
                    >
                      <Edit3 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-600" />
                      {isEditingTeam ? 'Cancel Edit' : 'Edit Team Details'}
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => toggleTeamVacancy(selectedTeam.id)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-black/30 hover:bg-black/50 text-white font-bold border border-white/25 transition-colors"
                    >
                      {selectedTeam.vacancyOpen ? (
                        <>
                          <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Force Close Vacancy
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <Unlock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Force Open Vacancy
                        </>
                      )}
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        adminUpdateTeam(selectedTeam.id, {
                          teamSize: selectedTeam.teamSize + 1,
                          vacancyOpen: true,
                        })
                      }
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-black/30 hover:bg-black/50 text-white font-bold border border-white/25 transition-colors"
                      title="Increase Max Team Capacity"
                    >
                      +1 Slot (Max {selectedTeam.teamSize + 1})
                    </button>

                    {selectedTeam.teamSize > selectedTeam.members.length &&
                      selectedTeam.teamSize > 2 && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() =>
                            adminUpdateTeam(selectedTeam.id, {
                              teamSize: selectedTeam.teamSize - 1,
                            })
                          }
                          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-black/30 hover:bg-black/50 text-white font-bold border border-white/25 transition-colors"
                        >
                          -1 Slot
                        </button>
                      )}

                    {confirmDeleteTeamId === selectedTeam.id ? (
                      <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-rose-950/90 px-3 py-1 rounded-xl border border-rose-400">
                        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-rose-200">
                          Dissolve & delete team?
                        </span>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => {
                            const nextTeam = teams.find((t) => t.id !== selectedTeam.id);
                            adminDeleteTeam(selectedTeam.id);
                            setConfirmDeleteTeamId(null);
                            if (nextTeam) setSelectedTeamId(nextTeam.id);
                          }}
                          className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 text-white font-black text-[11px]"
                        >
                          Confirm Delete
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setConfirmDeleteTeamId(null)}
                          className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-700 text-white text-[11px]"
                        >
                          Cancel
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setConfirmDeleteTeamId(selectedTeam.id)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-600/90 hover:bg-rose-600 text-white font-bold border border-rose-400/40 transition-colors ml-auto"
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Dissolve Team
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="p-6 space-y-6 text-xs">
                  {/* Inline Team Editor Form (when active) */}
                  {isEditingTeam && (
                    <form
                      onSubmit={saveTeamEdits}
                      className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50/70 border-2 border-amber-300 space-y-3"
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                        <h3 className="text-sm font-black text-amber-950 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <Edit3 className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600" />
                          Admin Override: Edit Team Specifications
                        </h3>
                      </div>

                      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                        <div>
                          <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Team Title</label>
                          <input
                            type="text"
                            required
                            value={editTeamTitle}
                            onChange={(e) => setEditTeamTitle(e.target.value)}
                            className="w-full p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                          />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Department</label>
                          <input
                            type="text"
                            required
                            value={editTeamDept}
                            onChange={(e) => setEditTeamDept(e.target.value)}
                            className="w-full p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                          />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Category</label>
                          <input
                            type="text"
                            required
                            value={editTeamCategory}
                            onChange={(e) => setEditTeamCategory(e.target.value)}
                            className="w-full p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                          />
                        </div>
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                          <div>
                            <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                              Max Team Size
                            </label>
                            <input
                              type="number"
                              min={1}
                              max={15}
                              value={editTeamSize}
                              onChange={(e) => setEditTeamSize(Number(e.target.value))}
                              className="w-full p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                            />
                          </div>
                          <div>
                            <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Deadline</label>
                            <input
                              type="text"
                              value={editTeamDeadline}
                              onChange={(e) => setEditTeamDeadline(e.target.value)}
                              className="w-full p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                            />
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Project Description & Deliverable Scope
                        </label>
                        <textarea
                          rows={2}
                          value={editTeamDesc}
                          onChange={(e) => setEditTeamDesc(e.target.value)}
                          className="w-full p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                        <div>
                          <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                            Required Skills (comma-separated)
                          </label>
                          <input
                            type="text"
                            value={editTeamSkills}
                            onChange={(e) => setEditTeamSkills(e.target.value)}
                            className="w-full p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                          />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                            Tags (comma-separated)
                          </label>
                          <input
                            type="text"
                            value={editTeamTags}
                            onChange={(e) => setEditTeamTags(e.target.value)}
