                          {team.category}
                        </span>
                        <span
                          className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded ${
                            team.vacancyOpen && spotsLeft > 0
                              ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200'
                              : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-600'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {team.vacancyOpen && spotsLeft > 0
                            ? `${spotsLeft} Spot${spotsLeft > 1 ? 's' : ''} Open`
                            : 'Roster Full'}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <h4 className="text-sm font-black text-slate-900 group-hover:text-indigo-600 transition-colors leading-snug">
                        {team.title}
                      </h4>

                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed">
                        {team.description}
                      </p>

                      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 pt-1">
                        {team.requiredSkills.slice(0, 4).map((sk, idx) => (
                          <span
                            key={idx}
                            className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-white border border-slate-200 text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-700"
                          >
                            {sk}
                          </span>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="pt-2.5 border-t border-slate-200/80 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                      <span className="text-slate-600 truncate">
                        Lead: <strong className="text-slate-900">{team.leaderName.replace(' (Team Lead)', '')}</strong>
                      </span>
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-indigo-600 font-bold shrink-0 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform">
                        <span>Open Project Page</span>
                        <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =====================================================================
          MODAL 1: BE A TEAM MEMBER (NEW STUDENT REGISTRATION / LOGIN)
         ===================================================================== */}
      {activePortalModal === 'MEMBER' && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/70 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl max-w-2xl w-full border border-slate-200 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden my-8">
            <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-900 via-indigo-800 to-slate-900 text-white p-6 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-white/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <UserPlus className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-300" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-lg font-black">Be a Team Member Portal</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-indigo-200">
                    Register your student account to get all teams matched by your skills & interests
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActivePortalModal(null)}
                className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10"
              >
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Mode Switcher */}
            <div className="flex border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-6 pt-3 gap-4 text-xs font-bold">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setMemberMode('REGISTER');
                  setMemberError('');
                }}
                className={`pb-3 border-b-2 transition-colors ${
                  memberMode === 'REGISTER'
                    ? 'border-indigo-600 text-indigo-600'
                    : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
                }`}
              >
                New Student Registration
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setMemberMode('LOGIN');
                  setMemberError('');
                }}
                className={`pb-3 border-b-2 transition-colors ${
                  memberMode === 'LOGIN'
                    ? 'border-indigo-600 text-indigo-600'
                    : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
                }`}
              >
                Existing Member Sign In
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="p-6 max-h-[78vh] overflow-y-auto">
              {memberError && (
                <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-700 text-xs font-semibold">
                  {memberError}
                </div>
              )}

              {memberMode === 'REGISTER' ? (
                <form onSubmit={handleMemberRegisterSubmit} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
                  <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50/70 border border-indigo-100 text-indigo-950 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600 shrink-0" />
                    <span>
                      <strong>Privacy Guarantee:</strong> Your account data and every activity you
                      perform are stored privately and visible <strong>only to you and the Admin</strong>.
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  {/* Emoji Avatar Picker */}
                  <div>
                    <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      Choose Your Profile Emoji Avatar
                    </label>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                      {AVATAR_EMOJI_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
                        <button
                          key={opt.emoji}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setMemberAvatar(opt.emoji)}
                          className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-xl text-base flex items-center justify-center border transition-all ${
                            memberAvatar === opt.emoji
                              ? 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-600 ring-2 ring-indigo-500/20 scale-105'
                              : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 hover:bg-white'
                          }`}
                          title={opt.label}
                        >
                          {opt.emoji}
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Full Name *</label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        required
                        placeholder="e.g., Aarav Sharma"
                        value={memberName}
                        onChange={(e) => setMemberName(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-600"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        University Email *
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="email"
                        required
                        placeholder="e.g., aarav.cs@geu.ac.in"
                        value={memberEmail}
                        onChange={(e) => setMemberEmail(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-600"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Password *</label>
                      <input
                        type="password"
                        required
                        placeholder="Create password"
                        value={memberPassword}
                        onChange={(e) => setMemberPassword(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-600"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Student Roll / ID
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="e.g., 25101210991"
                        value={memberIdNum}
                        onChange={(e) => setMemberIdNum(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-600"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Academic Year</label>
                      <select
                        value={memberYear}
                        onChange={(e) => setMemberYear(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-600"
                      >
                        <option>1st Year (B.Tech)</option>
                        <option>2nd Year (B.Tech)</option>
                        <option>3rd Year (B.Tech)</option>
                        <option>4th Year (B.Tech)</option>
                        <option>M.Tech / Ph.D. Researcher</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Department *</label>
                      <select
                        value={memberDept}
                        onChange={(e) => setMemberDept(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-600"
                      >
                        {DEPARTMENTS.map((d) => (
                          <option key={d} value={d}>
                            {d}
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Weekly Availability
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={memberAvailability}
                        onChange={(e) => setMemberAvailability(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-600"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Technical Skills Selection */}
                  <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-2.5">
                    <label className="block font-bold text-slate-800">
                      Your Technical Skills (Used to Match Teams) *
                    </label>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                      {COMMON_SKILLS.map((sk) => {
                        const active = memberSkills.includes(sk);
                        return (
                          <button
                            key={sk}
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => toggleItemInList(memberSkills, setMemberSkills, sk)}
                            className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-semibold border transition-colors ${
                              active
                                ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white border-indigo-600'
                                : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:border-indigo-300'
                            }`}
                          >
                            {sk}
                          </button>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="Add custom skill (e.g., OpenCV, Rust)..."
                        value={customMemberSkill}
                        onChange={(e) => setCustomMemberSkill(e.target.value)}
                        className="flex-1 p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          if (
                            customMemberSkill.trim() &&
                            !memberSkills.includes(customMemberSkill.trim())
                          ) {
                            setMemberSkills([...memberSkills, customMemberSkill.trim()]);
                            setCustomMemberSkill('');
                          }
                        }}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 text-white font-semibold"
                      >
                        + Add Skill
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Project Interests Selection */}
                  <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-2.5">
                    <label className="block font-bold text-slate-800">
                      Your Project Interests & Domains (Used to Match Teams) *
                    </label>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                      {COMMON_INTERESTS.map((interest) => {
                        const active = memberInterests.includes(interest);
                        return (
                          <button
                            key={interest}
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() =>
                              toggleItemInList(memberInterests, setMemberInterests, interest)
                            }
                            className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-semibold border transition-colors ${
                              active
                                ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white border-emerald-600'
                                : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:border-emerald-300'
                            }`}
                          >
                            {interest}
                          </button>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="Add custom interest (e.g., Quantum Computing, Game Dev)..."
                        value={customMemberInterest}
                        onChange={(e) => setCustomMemberInterest(e.target.value)}
                        className="flex-1 p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          if (
                            customMemberInterest.trim() &&
                            !memberInterests.includes(customMemberInterest.trim())
                          ) {
                            setMemberInterests([...memberInterests, customMemberInterest.trim()]);
                            setCustomMemberInterest('');
                          }
                        }}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 text-white font-semibold"
                      >
                        + Add Interest
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        GitHub Profile URL (Optional)
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="url"
                        placeholder="https://github.com/username"
                        value={memberGithub}
                        onChange={(e) => setMemberGithub(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Short Bio / Objective
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="Passionate about building low-latency systems..."
                        value={memberBio}
                        onChange={(e) => setMemberBio(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setActivePortalModal(null)}
                      className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 font-semibold"
                    >
                      Cancel
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-bold shadow-sm flex items-center gap-2"
                    >
                      <span>Create Member Account & View Matched Teams</span>
                      <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              ) : (
                <form onSubmit={handleMemberLoginSubmit} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Registered University Email or Student ID *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      required
                      placeholder="e.g., shishir.dwivedi.cs@geu.ac.in or 25101210684"
                      value={memberLoginInput}
                      onChange={(e) => setMemberLoginInput(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Password</label>
                    <input
                      type="password"
                      placeholder="Enter your account password"
                      value={memberLoginPass}
                      onChange={(e) => setMemberLoginPass(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setActivePortalModal(null)}
                      className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 font-semibold"
                    >
                      Cancel
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-bold flex items-center gap-2"
                    >
                      <LogIn className="w-4 h-4" />
                      <span>Sign In to Member Dashboard</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* =====================================================================
          MODAL 2: BE A TEAM LEADER (CREATE NEW PROJECT & TEAM / LEADER LOGIN)
         ===================================================================== */}
      {activePortalModal === 'LEADER' && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/70 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl max-w-3xl w-full border border-slate-200 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden my-8">
            <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-950 via-indigo-900 to-slate-900 text-white p-6 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-white/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <Rocket className="w-5 h-5 text-violet-300" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-lg font-black">Be a Team Leader — Create New Project & Team</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-violet-200">
                    Define all project details, required team skills, and open slots to recruit members
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActivePortalModal(null)}
                className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10"
              >
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Mode Switcher */}
            <div className="flex border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-6 pt-3 gap-4 text-xs font-bold">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setLeaderMode('CREATE_PROJECT');
                  setLeaderError('');
                }}
                className={`pb-3 border-b-2 transition-colors ${
                  leaderMode === 'CREATE_PROJECT'
                    ? 'border-violet-600 text-violet-700'
                    : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
                }`}
              >
                Create New Project & Team Leader Account
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setLeaderMode('LOGIN');
                  setLeaderError('');
                }}
                className={`pb-3 border-b-2 transition-colors ${
                  leaderMode === 'LOGIN'
                    ? 'border-violet-600 text-violet-700'
                    : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
                }`}
              >
                Existing Team Leader Sign In
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="p-6 max-h-[78vh] overflow-y-auto">
              {leaderError && (
                <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-700 text-xs font-semibold">
                  {leaderError}
                </div>
              )}

              {leaderMode === 'CREATE_PROJECT' ? (
                <form onSubmit={handleLeaderCreateProjectSubmit} className="space-y-5 text-xs">
                  <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-violet-50 border border-violet-200 text-violet-950 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-violet-600 shrink-0" />
                    <span>
                      <strong>Confidential Account & Activity Storage:</strong> Your Team Leader
                      account data and project creation activity are stored privately and visible{' '}
                      <strong>only to you and the Website Admin</strong>.
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  {/* Section 1: Leader Account Details */}
                  <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-3">
                    <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span>1. Team Leader Account Details</span>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Leader Emoji Avatar
                      </label>
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                        {AVATAR_EMOJI_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
                          <button
                            key={opt.emoji}
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setLeaderAvatar(opt.emoji)}
                            className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-lg text-base flex items-center justify-center border ${
                              leaderAvatar === opt.emoji
                                ? 'bg-violet-50 border-violet-600 ring-2 ring-violet-500/20'
                                : 'bg-white border-slate-200'
                            }`}
                          >
                            {opt.emoji}
                          </button>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                      <div>
                        <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Leader Full Name *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          required
                          placeholder="e.g., Rohan Verma"
                          value={leaderName}
                          onChange={(e) => setLeaderName(e.target.value)}
                          className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Leader Email *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="email"
                          required
                          placeholder="e.g., rohan.lead@geu.ac.in"
                          value={leaderEmail}
                          onChange={(e) => setLeaderEmail(e.target.value)}
                          className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Password *</label>
                        <input
                          type="password"
                          required
                          placeholder="Create password"
                          value={leaderPassword}
                          onChange={(e) => setLeaderPassword(e.target.value)}
                          className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                      <div>
                        <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          University ID / Roll No
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          placeholder="e.g., 25101210888"
                          value={leaderIdNum}
                          onChange={(e) => setLeaderIdNum(e.target.value)}
                          className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Leader Department
                        </label>
                        <select
                          value={leaderDept}
                          onChange={(e) => {
                            setLeaderDept(e.target.value);
                            setProjDept(e.target.value);
                          }}
                          className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                        >
                          {DEPARTMENTS.map((d) => (
                            <option key={d} value={d}>
                              {d}
                            </option>
                          ))}
                        </select>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Academic Year</label>
                        <select
                          value={leaderYear}
                          onChange={(e) => setLeaderYear(e.target.value)}
                          className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                        >
                          <option>2nd Year (B.Tech)</option>
                          <option>3rd Year (B.Tech)</option>
                          <option>4th Year (B.Tech)</option>
                          <option>M.Tech / Faculty Mentor</option>
                        </select>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Section 2: All Team & Project Details */}
                  <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-3">
                    <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span>2. Project & Team Requirements Details</span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                      <div>
                        <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Project / Team Name *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          required
                          placeholder="e.g., NeuralEdge C++ Distributed Engine"
                          value={projTitle}
                          onChange={(e) => setProjTitle(e.target.value)}
                          className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Project Domain / Category *
                        </label>
                        <select
                          value={projCategory}
                          onChange={(e) => setProjCategory(e.target.value)}
                          className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                        >
                          {PROJECT_CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
                            <option key={c} value={c}>
                              {c}
                            </option>
                          ))}
                        </select>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Detailed Project Description & Deliverables *
                      </label>
                      <textarea
                        rows={3}
                        required
                        placeholder="Describe what your project builds, the problem it solves, and what responsibilities team members will have..."
                        value={projDesc}
                        onChange={(e) => setProjDesc(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                      />
                    </div>

                    {/* Required Skills Needed in the Team */}
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      <label className="block font-bold text-slate-800">
                        Required Skills Needed in the Team *
                      </label>
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                        {COMMON_SKILLS.map((sk) => {
                          const active = projRequiredSkills.includes(sk);
                          return (
                            <button
                              key={sk}
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() =>
                                toggleItemInList(projRequiredSkills, setProjRequiredSkills, sk)
                              }
                              className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-semibold border transition-colors ${
                                active
                                  ? 'bg-violet-600 text-white border-violet-600'
                                  : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:border-violet-300'
                              }`}
                            >
                              {sk}
                            </button>
                          );
                        })}
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          placeholder="Add specific required skill (e.g., CUDA, ROS2, Socket Programming)..."
                          value={customProjSkill}
                          onChange={(e) => setCustomProjSkill(e.target.value)}
                          className="flex-1 p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                        />
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => {
                            if (
                              customProjSkill.trim() &&
                              !projRequiredSkills.includes(customProjSkill.trim())
                            ) {
                              setProjRequiredSkills([
                                ...projRequiredSkills,
                                customProjSkill.trim(),
                              ]);
                              setCustomProjSkill('');
                            }
                          }}
                          className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 text-white font-semibold"
                        >
                          + Add Required Skill
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 pt-1">
                      <div>
                        <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Target Team Size (Total Slots) *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          min={2}
                          max={12}
                          value={projTeamSize}
                          onChange={(e) => setProjTeamSize(Number(e.target.value))}
                          className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Application Deadline
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="date"
                          value={projDeadline}
                          onChange={(e) => setProjDeadline(e.target.value)}
                          className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Project Tech Tags (Comma-separated)
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={projTagsInput}
                          onChange={(e) => setProjTagsInput(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="C++20, Graph, Systems"
                          className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setActivePortalModal(null)}
                      className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 font-semibold"
                    >
                      Cancel
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-700 text-white font-bold shadow-sm flex items-center gap-2"
                    >
                      <Rocket className="w-4 h-4" />
                      <span>Create Project & Open Team Leader Dashboard</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              ) : (
                <form onSubmit={handleLeaderLoginSubmit} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Team Leader Email or ID *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      required
                      placeholder="e.g., shreya.yadav.cs@geu.ac.in"
                      value={leaderLoginInput}
                      onChange={(e) => setLeaderLoginInput(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Password</label>
                    <input
                      type="password"
                      placeholder="Enter leader password"
                      value={leaderLoginPass}
                      onChange={(e) => setLeaderLoginPass(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setActivePortalModal(null)}
                      className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 font-semibold"
                    >
                      Cancel
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-700 text-white font-bold flex items-center gap-2"
                    >
                      <LogIn className="w-4 h-4" />
                      <span>Sign In to Team Leader Dashboard</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* =====================================================================
          MODAL 3: ADMIN LOGIN GATE (Username: adminsm$4146 / Password: adminsrsad@414.)
         ===================================================================== */}
      {activePortalModal === 'ADMIN' && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/75 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl max-w-md w-full border border-slate-200 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
            <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 via-amber-950 to-slate-900 text-white p-6 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/20 border border-amber-500/40 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <ShieldAlert className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-lg font-black">Supreme Admin Login</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-amber-200">
                    Authenticate to control the entire website & inspect all user activities
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActivePortalModal(null)}
                className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10"
              >
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleAdminLoginSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-4 text-xs">
              {adminError && (
                <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-700 font-semibold">
                  {adminError}
                </div>
              )}

              <div>
                <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Admin Username *</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  placeholder="Enter Admin Username"
                  value={adminUsername}
                  onChange={(e) => setAdminUsername(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-mono focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-600"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Admin Password *</label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <input
                    type={showAdminPass ? 'text' : 'password'}
                    required
                    placeholder="Enter Admin Password"
                    value={adminPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setAdminPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2.5 pr-10 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-mono focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-600"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowAdminPass(!showAdminPass)}
                    className="absolute right-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700"
                  >
                    {showAdminPass ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActivePortalModal(null)}
                  className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 font-semibold"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors"
                >
                  <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>Authenticate & Enter Admin Control</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { useApp } from '../context/AppContext';
import {
  Bell,
  MessageSquare,
  X,
  Sparkles,
  ChevronRight,
  ShieldCheck,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface LeaderInvitationPopupProps {
  onOpenChat: (applicationId: string) => void;
}

export const LeaderInvitationPopup: React.FC<LeaderInvitationPopupProps> = ({
  onOpenChat,
}) => {
  const { activeNotificationPopup, clearNotificationPopup, role } = useApp();

  if (!activeNotificationPopup) return null;

  const handleAction = () => {
    if (activeNotificationPopup.actionPayload?.applicationId) {
      onOpenChat(activeNotificationPopup.actionPayload.applicationId);
    }
    clearNotificationPopup();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-5 right-5 z-50 max-w-sm w-full bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-2xl border border-indigo-500/40 animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-5 duration-200">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
          <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-md">
            <Bell className="w-5 h-5 animate-bounce" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider px-2 py-0.2 rounded bg-indigo-500/30 text-indigo-300">
                Team Leader Notice
              </span>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Just now</span>
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-white mt-1">
              {activeNotificationPopup.title}
            </h4>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 mt-1 leading-snug">
              {activeNotificationPopup.message}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={clearNotificationPopup}
          className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white p-1"
        >
          <X className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={clearNotificationPopup}
          className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
        >
          Dismiss
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleAction}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white transition-colors shadow-xs"
        >
          <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          Open Chat & Review
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

================================================================================
FILE: src/components/SkillAssessment.tsx (657 lines)
================================================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useApp } from '../context/AppContext';
import {
  C_CPP_CHALLENGES,
  CodeChallenge,
  AssessmentEngine,
  CodeEvaluationResult,
} from '../lib/assessmentEngine';
import {
  Code2,
  ShieldAlert,
  Clock,
  Play,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertTriangle,
  RotateCcw,
  Sparkles,
  FileCheck2,
  Terminal,
  Send,
  Cpu,
  Layers,
  Award,
} from 'lucide-react';

export const SkillAssessment: React.FC = () => {
  const { currentUserProfile, submitAssessment, role } = useApp();

  const [selectedChallengeId, setSelectedChallengeId] = useState<string>('cpp-smart-vector');
  const currentChallenge: CodeChallenge =
    C_CPP_CHALLENGES.find((c) => c.id === selectedChallengeId) || C_CPP_CHALLENGES[0];

  const [code, setCode] = useState<string>(currentChallenge.starterCode);
  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState<number>(currentChallenge.timeLimitMinutes * 60);
  const [isRunning, setIsRunning] = useState<boolean>(true);

  // Anti-cheat telemetry
  const [tabSwitchCount, setTabSwitchCount] = useState<number>(0);
  const [copyPasteCount, setCopyPasteCount] = useState<number>(0);
  const [cheatAlert, setCheatAlert] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Results
  const [isEvaluating, setIsEvaluating] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [evaluationResult, setEvaluationResult] = useState<CodeEvaluationResult | null>(null);
  const [submittedSuccess, setSubmittedSuccess] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Switch challenge resets
  useEffect(() => {
    setCode(currentChallenge.starterCode);
    setTimeLeft(currentChallenge.timeLimitMinutes * 60);
    setTabSwitchCount(0);
    setCopyPasteCount(0);
    setEvaluationResult(null);
    setSubmittedSuccess(false);
    setCheatAlert(null);
    setIsRunning(true);
  }, [selectedChallengeId]);

  // Anti-cheat: Tab-switch & Visibility listener
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleVisibilityChange = () => {
      if (document.hidden) {
        setTabSwitchCount((prev) => {
          const next = prev + 1;
          setCheatAlert(`Anti-Cheat Warning: Tab switch / window blur detected (#${next}). This event is logged in your candidate audit report.`);
          return next;
        });
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibilityChange);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibilityChange);
  }, []);

  // Timer countdown
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isRunning || timeLeft <= 0) return;
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setTimeLeft((prev) => {
        if (prev <= 1) {
          setIsRunning(false);
          return 0;
        }
        return prev - 1;
      });
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [isRunning, timeLeft]);

  const formatTimer = (seconds: number) => {
    const mins = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
    const secs = seconds % 60;
    return `${mins.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
  };

  const handleCopyPaste = (e: React.ClipboardEvent) => {
    setCopyPasteCount((prev) => {
      const next = prev + 1;
      setCheatAlert(`Anti-Cheat Notice: Clipboard copy/paste activity detected (#${next}). Code originality verification active.`);
      return next;
    });
  };

  const handleReset = () => {
    if (confirm('Reset code back to the initial challenge template?')) {
      setCode(currentChallenge.starterCode);
      setEvaluationResult(null);
      setSubmittedSuccess(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRunEvaluation = () => {
    setIsEvaluating(true);
    setCheatAlert(null);

    const timeSpent = currentChallenge.timeLimitMinutes * 60 - timeLeft;

    // Run automated code evaluation engine
    setTimeout(() => {
      const result = AssessmentEngine.evaluateCode(code, currentChallenge, {
        timeSpentSeconds: timeSpent,
        tabSwitchCount,
        copyPasteCount,
      });

      setEvaluationResult(result);
      setIsEvaluating(false);

      // Submit assessment and send directly to recruiting teams
      if (currentUserProfile) {
        submitAssessment({
          candidateId: currentUserProfile.id,
          candidateName: currentUserProfile.name,
          candidateEmail: currentUserProfile.email,
          language: currentChallenge.language,
          challengeId: currentChallenge.id,
          challengeTitle: currentChallenge.title,
          submittedCode: code,
          syntaxValid: result.syntaxValid,
          testCasesPassed: result.testCasesPassed,
          totalTestCases: result.totalTestCases,
          score: result.score,
          timeSpentSeconds: timeSpent,
          tabSwitchCount,
          copyPasteCount,
          cheatSuspicion: result.cheatSuspicion,
          timeComplexity: result.timeComplexity,
          spaceComplexity: result.spaceComplexity,
          memorySafetyNotes: result.memorySafetyNotes,
          verdict: result.verdict,
          personalizedFeedback: result.personalizedFeedback,
        });

        setSubmittedSuccess(true);
      }
    }, 600);
  };

  const [showPlanModal, setShowPlanModal] = useState<boolean>(false);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header & Mode Switcher */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 bg-white p-5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
            <span className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700">
              <Code2 className="w-5 h-5" />
            </span>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
              C & C++ Developer Skill Assessment & Verification
            </h1>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
            Automated code quality audit, memory safety analyzer, test suite runner, and anti-cheat telemetry.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Challenge Selector & Architecture Blueprint Button */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setShowPlanModal(true)}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 hover:bg-indigo-100 border border-indigo-200 transition-colors"
          >
            <Layers className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-indigo-600" />
            Implementation Plan (8 Modules)
          </button>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Challenge:</label>
            <select
              value={selectedChallengeId}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedChallengeId(e.target.value)}
              className="text-xs font-medium py-2 px-3 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500/20 bg-white"
            >
              {C_CPP_CHALLENGES.map((ch) => (
                <option key={ch.id} value={ch.id}>
                  [{ch.language.toUpperCase()}] {ch.title} ({ch.difficulty})
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Interactive 8-Module Assessment Plan Modal */}
      {showPlanModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs animate-in fade-in duration-150">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-2xl w-full max-w-4xl max-h-[88vh] overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
            <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 via-indigo-950 to-slate-900 p-5 text-white flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-300">
                  Engineering Specification & Architectural Blueprint
                </span>
                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white mt-0.5">
                  C++ & C Skill Assessment Module Implementation Plan
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">
                  Comprehensive 8-pillar design specification for candidate proficiency evaluation & recruitment.
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowPlanModal(false)}
                className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white p-1 rounded-lg"
              >
                ✕
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-4 text-xs text-slate-700">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                {/* 1. Proficiency Evaluation */}
                <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 text-xs font-black flex items-center justify-center">1</span>
                    Proficiency Evaluation
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                    Evaluates candidate depth across core low-level paradigms: raw pointer arithmetic, dynamic heap allocations, RAII destructors, copy semantics, and graph adjacency structures. Candidates are classified into Beginner, Intermediate, and Advanced tiers.
                  </p>
                </div>

                {/* 2. Answer Verification */}
                <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 text-xs font-black flex items-center justify-center">2</span>
                    Answer Verification & Test Harness
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                    Automated dual-tier test runner executing both transparent sample test suites and hidden edge cases (null pointer boundaries, empty strings, capacity overflow doubling, cyclical graph paths). Verifies exact input-output compliance.
                  </p>
                </div>

                {/* 3. Eligibility Criteria */}
                <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 text-xs font-black flex items-center justify-center">3</span>
                    Eligibility Criteria Enforcement
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                    Enforces strict criteria cutoffs: <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-700">VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE (Score ≥ 75% & Low Cheat)</span> awards the Verified Developer Badge and priority matching; <span className="font-semibold text-amber-700">ELIGIBLE_WITH_CONDITIONS (50–74%)</span>; and <span className="font-semibold text-rose-700">NOT_ELIGIBLE_UNVERIFIED (&lt;50%)</span> locks critical project positions.
                  </p>
                </div>

                {/* 4. Anti-Cheat Measures */}
                <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 text-xs font-black flex items-center justify-center">4</span>
                    Anti-Cheat Telemetry System
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                    Real-time client monitoring: Page visibility and window blur listener tracking tab switches; clipboard paste event interceptors; 20-minute countdown timer; and rapid bot-submission velocity detection. Violations increment flags and deduct integrity points.
                  </p>
                </div>

                {/* 5. Automated Code Quality */}
                <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 text-xs font-black flex items-center justify-center">5</span>
                    Automated Code Quality & Memory Safety
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                    Syntax and memory leak analyzer: Verifies balance between <code className="font-mono text-emerald-700">new[]/delete[]</code> and <code className="font-mono text-emerald-700">malloc/free</code>, detects dangerous functions like <code className="font-mono text-rose-700">gets()</code>, checks boundary validation, and categorizes Big-O Time and Space complexity.
                  </p>
                </div>

                {/* 6. Personalized Feedback */}
                <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 text-xs font-black flex items-center justify-center">6</span>
                    Personalized Constructive Feedback
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                    Generates customized technical feedback highlighting algorithmic strengths, pinpointed memory hygiene vulnerabilities, defensive pointer recommendations, and actionable next steps for the developer's growth.
                  </p>
                </div>

                {/* 7. Candidate Dashboard */}
                <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 text-xs font-black flex items-center justify-center">7</span>
                    Candidate Dashboard & Scheduling Tracker
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                    Provides candidates with an all-in-one hub: 4-stage application progress and scheduling status timeline, document management (PDF resume, cover letter, verified certificates), and top algorithm-matched teams based on verified test results.
                  </p>
                </div>

                {/* 8. Reporting to Recruiters */}
                <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 text-xs font-black flex items-center justify-center">8</span>
                    Recruiting Team Dispatch Pipeline
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                    Upon assessment completion, full candidate reports—including submitted code, test case scores, anti-cheat audit logs, complexity analysis, and verification verdict—are dispatched directly to team leaders and university recruiting coordinators.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 border-t border-slate-200 flex justify-end">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowPlanModal(false)}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-lg font-bold text-xs hover:bg-indigo-700"
              >
                Close Plan
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Anti-Cheat Telemetry Banner */}
      <div className="bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-xl p-4 shadow-sm border border-slate-800 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 text-emerald-400">
            <ShieldAlert className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 flex items-center gap-2">
              Automated Integrity & Anti-Cheat System
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />
              <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold text-emerald-400">Active</span>
            </div>
            <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
              Monitoring tab switches, clipboard paste anomalies, and memory safety invariants.
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-xs">
          {/* Timer */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700">
            <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-amber-300">{formatTimer(timeLeft)}</span>
          </div>

          {/* Tab Switch Counter */}
          <div
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border text-xs font-medium ${
              tabSwitchCount > 0
                ? 'bg-rose-950/80 border-rose-800 text-rose-300'
                : 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-slate-300'
            }`}
          >
            <span>Tab Switches:</span>
            <span className="font-bold">{tabSwitchCount}</span>
          </div>

          {/* Copy Paste Counter */}
          <div
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border text-xs font-medium ${
              copyPasteCount > 0
                ? 'bg-amber-950/80 border-amber-800 text-amber-300'
                : 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-slate-300'
            }`}
          >
            <span>Paste Count:</span>
            <span className="font-bold">{copyPasteCount}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Anti-cheat Alert Toast */}
      {cheatAlert && (
        <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-300 text-amber-900 rounded-lg p-3 text-xs flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600 shrink-0" />
            <span>{cheatAlert}</span>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setCheatAlert(null)}
            className="text-amber-700 hover:text-amber-900 font-bold px-2 py-0.5"
          >
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Main Coding & Problem Workspace */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-5">
        {/* Left Column: Problem Brief & Instructions */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-5 space-y-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 shadow-xs space-y-4">
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 uppercase">
                  {currentChallenge.language.toUpperCase()} • {currentChallenge.difficulty}
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">
                  {currentChallenge.testCases.length} Test Criteria
                </span>
              </div>
              <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">{currentChallenge.title}</h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 mt-2 leading-relaxed">
                {currentChallenge.description}
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Step by Step Instructions */}
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                Engineering Requirements:
              </h3>
              <ul className="space-y-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600">
                {currentChallenge.instructions.map((inst, i) => (
                  <li key={i} className="flex items-start gap-2">
                    <span className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 text-[10px]">
                      {i + 1}
                    </span>
                    <span>{inst}</span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>

            {/* Test Cases Overview */}
            <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                Verification Test Suites:
              </h3>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {currentChallenge.testCases.map((tc) => (
                  <div
                    key={tc.id}
                    className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      <span>{tc.description}</span>
                      <span
                        className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-bold ${
                          tc.isHidden
                            ? 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700'
                            : 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {tc.isHidden ? 'Hidden Edge Case' : 'Public Test'}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-600">
                      Input: {tc.input} ➔ Expected: {tc.expectedOutput}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right Column: Interactive Code Editor */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-7 space-y-4">
          <div className="bg-slate-950 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 overflow-hidden shadow-md flex flex-col">
            {/* Editor Toolbar */}
            <div className="bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2.5 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Terminal className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
                <span className="font-mono text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200">
                  solution.{currentChallenge.language === 'cpp' ? 'cpp' : 'c'}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleReset}
                  className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded text-xs font-medium text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors flex items-center gap-1"
                >
                  <RotateCcw className="w-3 h-3" /> Reset
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Code Input Area */}
            <div className="relative p-2 bg-slate-950">
              <textarea
                value={code}
                onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value)}
                onPaste={handleCopyPaste}
                onCopy={handleCopyPaste}
                rows={16}
                spellCheck={false}
                className="w-full bg-slate-950 font-mono text-xs text-emerald-400 p-3 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500/50 rounded resize-y leading-relaxed"
                placeholder="// Write your C/C++ solution here..."
              />
            </div>

            {/* Action Bar */}
            <div className="bg-slate-900/90 px-4 py-3 border-t border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                Press Run to execute test suite and verify eligibility.
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleRunEvaluation}
                disabled={isEvaluating}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-emerald-600 text-white hover:bg-emerald-500 transition-colors shadow-sm disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {isEvaluating ? (
                  <>
                    <Cpu className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                    Auditing Code & Memory...
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Play className="w-4 h-4" />
                    Run & Submit for Verification
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Results & Personalized Recruiting Feedback Display */}
          {evaluationResult && (
            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                    <Award className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600" />
                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                      Assessment & Verification Results
                    </h3>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                    Automated feedback and eligibility report sent directly to recruiting teams and team leaders.
                  </p>
                </div>

                {/* Verdict Badge */}
                <div className="text-right">
                  <span
                    className={`inline-block px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider ${
                      evaluationResult.verdict === 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE'
                        ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-300'
                        : evaluationResult.verdict === 'ELIGIBLE_WITH_CONDITIONS'
                        ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-300'
                        : 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 border border-rose-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {evaluationResult.verdict.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
                  </span>
                  <div className="text-lg font-black text-slate-900 mt-1">
                    Score: {evaluationResult.score}%
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Metrics Grid */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3 text-xs">
                <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100">
                  <div className="text-slate-400 font-medium">Test Cases Passed</div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 mt-1">
                    {evaluationResult.testCasesPassed} / {evaluationResult.totalTestCases}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100">
                  <div className="text-slate-400 font-medium">Time Complexity</div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 mt-1">
                    {evaluationResult.timeComplexity}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100">
                  <div className="text-slate-400 font-medium">Space Complexity</div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 mt-1">
                    {evaluationResult.spaceComplexity}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100">
                  <div className="text-slate-400 font-medium">Anti-Cheat Suspicion</div>
                  <div
                    className={`text-sm font-bold mt-1 ${
                      evaluationResult.cheatSuspicion === 'LOW'
                        ? 'text-emerald-700'
                        : evaluationResult.cheatSuspicion === 'MEDIUM'
                        ? 'text-amber-700'
                        : 'text-rose-700'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {evaluationResult.cheatSuspicion} Risk
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Memory Safety Feedback */}
              {evaluationResult.memorySafetyNotes.length > 0 && (
                <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-xs text-rose-800 space-y-1">
                  <div className="font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600" />
                    Memory Safety Observations:
                  </div>
                  <ul className="list-disc list-inside text-rose-700">
                    {evaluationResult.memorySafetyNotes.map((note, i) => (
                      <li key={i}>{note}</li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Personalized Recruiting Team Feedback */}
              <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50/70 border border-indigo-200/80 space-y-2">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold text-indigo-950">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600" />
                    Personalized Candidate Feedback (Forwarded to Recruiting Team):
                  </span>
                  {submittedSuccess && (
                    <span className="text-emerald-700 text-[11px] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1">
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Forwarded
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-indigo-900/90 leading-relaxed italic">
                  "{evaluationResult.personalizedFeedback}"
                </p>
              </div>

              {submittedSuccess && (
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200 p-2.5 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
                  Your candidate profile and verified score have been updated. Team leaders will see
                  your verification status during application reviews!
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
