import React, { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  Copy,
  Check,
  Download,
  FileCode,
  Search,
  Layers,
  Cpu,
  FolderGit2,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';

export interface SourceModuleEntry {
  path: string;
  category: 'CPP_CORE' | 'TS_ENGINE' | 'REACT_COMPONENT' | 'CONFIG';
  language: string;
  lines: number;
  bytes: number;
  content: string;
}

interface SourceCodeViewerModalProps {
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const SourceCodeViewerModal: React.FC<SourceCodeViewerModalProps> = ({ onClose }) => {
  const [modules, setModules] = useState<SourceModuleEntry[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [selectedPath, setSelectedPath] = useState<string>('');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');
  const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState<
    'ALL' | 'CPP_CORE' | 'TS_ENGINE' | 'REACT_COMPONENT' | 'CONFIG'
  >('ALL');
  const [copiedSingle, setCopiedSingle] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [copiedAll, setCopiedAll] = useState<boolean>(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/SkillMatch-All-Modules.json')
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data: SourceModuleEntry[]) => {
        setModules(data);
        if (data.length > 0) {
          setSelectedPath(data[0].path);
        }
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        setLoading(false);
      });
  }, []);

  const filteredModules = useMemo(() => {
    return modules.filter((m) => {
      if (categoryFilter !== 'ALL' && m.category !== categoryFilter) return false;
      if (
        searchQuery.trim() &&
        !m.path.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase())
      ) {
        return false;
      }
      return true;
    });
  }, [modules, categoryFilter, searchQuery]);

  const activeModule = useMemo(() => {
    return (
      modules.find((m) => m.path === selectedPath) ||
      filteredModules[0] ||
      null
    );
  }, [modules, selectedPath, filteredModules]);

  const handleCopyCurrent = async () => {
    if (!activeModule) return;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(activeModule.content);
      setCopiedSingle(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopiedSingle(false), 2000);
    } catch {
      // Fallback
    }
  };

  const handleCopyAllModules = async () => {
    if (modules.length === 0) return;
    const combined = modules
      .map(
        (m) =>
          `// ============================================================================\n// FILE: ${m.path} (${m.lines} lines)\n// ============================================================================\n\n${m.content}`
      )
      .join('\n\n\n');
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(combined);
      setCopiedAll(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopiedAll(false), 2500);
    } catch {
      // Fallback
    }
  };

  const totalLines = useMemo(
    () => modules.reduce((acc, m) => acc + m.lines, 0),
    [modules]
  );

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-950/80 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-2 sm:p-4">
      <div className="bg-slate-900 text-white border border-slate-700 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-7xl h-[90vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        {/* Top Bar */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 via-indigo-950 to-slate-900 px-4 sm:px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-800 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600/30 border border-indigo-400/40 flex items-center justify-center text-indigo-300">
              <FolderGit2 className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-white">
                  Complete Website Source Code & All Modules Explorer
                </h2>
                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30">
                  {modules.length} Files • {totalLines.toLocaleString()} Lines
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">
                Browse, copy, or download every single C++ module, TypeScript engine, React component, and config file.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleCopyAllModules}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white font-bold text-xs transition-colors shadow-xs cursor-pointer"
            >
              {copiedAll ? (
                <>
                  <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-300" />
                  <span>Copied All {modules.length} Files!</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Copy Entire Website Code</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>

            <a
              href="/SkillMatch-Complete-All-Modules-Code.txt"
              download="SkillMatch-Complete-All-Modules-Code.txt"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 font-bold text-xs border border-slate-700 transition-colors"
            >
              <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-indigo-400" />
              <span>All Code in 1 File (.txt)</span>
            </a>

            <a
              href="/SkillMatch-Full-Source-Code.zip"
              download="SkillMatch-Full-Source-Code.zip"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold text-xs transition-colors shadow-xs"
            >
              <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Download Full Project (.zip)</span>
            </a>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              aria-label="Close source code viewer"
              className="p-2 rounded-xl text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors"
            >
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Category Filter & Search Strip */}
        <div className="bg-slate-900/90 px-4 sm:px-6 py-2.5 border-b border-slate-800 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
            {(
              [
                { id: 'ALL', label: `All Files (${modules.length})` },
                {
                  id: 'CPP_CORE',
                  label: `C++ Core Modules (${modules.filter((m) => m.category === 'CPP_CORE').length})`,
                },
                {
                  id: 'TS_ENGINE',
                  label: `TypeScript Engines & Data (${modules.filter((m) => m.category === 'TS_ENGINE').length})`,
                },
                {
                  id: 'REACT_COMPONENT',
                  label: `React Components & Views (${modules.filter((m) => m.category === 'REACT_COMPONENT').length})`,
                },
                {
                  id: 'CONFIG',
                  label: `Config & Entry (${modules.filter((m) => m.category === 'CONFIG').length})`,
                },
              ] as const
            ).map((tab) => (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setCategoryFilter(tab.id)}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                  categoryFilter === tab.id
                    ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                    : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700'
                }`}
              >
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="relative w-full sm:w-64">
            <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Filter module or file name..."
              className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white placeholder-slate-400 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Main Body: Sidebar + Code Viewer */}
        {loading ? (
          <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-400 text-sm">
            Loading all website modules and source files...
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col md:flex-row overflow-hidden">
            {/* Left File List */}
            <div className="w-full md:w-80 border-b md:border-b-0 md:border-r border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 overflow-y-auto p-2 space-y-1 max-h-48 md:max-h-none">
              {filteredModules.map((mod) => {
                const isSelected = activeModule?.path === mod.path;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={mod.path}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSelectedPath(mod.path)}
                    className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs flex items-center justify-between gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold shadow-xs'
                        : 'text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-800/80'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                      {mod.category === 'CPP_CORE' ? (
                        <Cpu className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 text-emerald-400" />
                      ) : mod.category === 'TS_ENGINE' ? (
                        <Layers className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 text-amber-400" />
                      ) : (
                        <FileCode className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 text-indigo-400" />
                      )}
                      <span className="truncate font-mono">{mod.path}</span>
                    </div>
                    <span
                      className={`text-[10px] shrink-0 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'bg-indigo-700 text-indigo-100'
                          : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {mod.lines}L
                    </span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            {/* Right Code Panel */}
            <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden bg-slate-950">
              {activeModule ? (
                <>
                  <div className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-900 border-b border-slate-800 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="font-mono font-bold text-indigo-300">
                        {activeModule.path}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-slate-500">•</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-400">
                        {activeModule.lines} lines ({(activeModule.bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB)
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={handleCopyCurrent}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-indigo-600 text-slate-200 hover:text-white font-bold text-xs border border-slate-700 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      {copiedSingle ? (
                        <>
                          <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                          <span>Copied {activeModule.path}!</span>
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          <span>Copy This Module Code</span>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <pre className="flex-1 p-4 sm:p-6 overflow-auto text-xs font-mono text-emerald-300 leading-relaxed selection:bg-indigo-500 selection:text-white">
                    <code>{activeModule.content}</code>
                  </pre>
                </>
              ) : (
                <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-500 text-xs">
                  No matching module found.
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useApp } from '../context/AppContext';
import { StudentProfile } from '../lib/types';
import {
  X,
  FileText,
  ShieldCheck,
  ShieldAlert,
  Ban,
  Github,
  Linkedin,
  Award,
  ExternalLink,
  Flag,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertTriangle,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { UserAvatar, AVATAR_EMOJI_OPTIONS } from './UserAvatar';

interface StudentProfileModalProps {
  profile: StudentProfile;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const StudentProfileModal: React.FC<StudentProfileModalProps> = ({
  profile,
  onClose,
}) => {
  const {
    currentUserProfile,
    fileReport,
    blockUser,
    reports,
    role,
    adminDeleteUser,
    updateStudentProfile,
  } = useApp();

  const [showReportForm, setShowReportForm] = useState(false);
  const [reportReason, setReportReason] = useState('Fraudulent credentials or fake GitHub');
  const [reportDetails, setReportDetails] = useState('');
  const [reportedSuccess, setReportedSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [isBlocked, setIsBlocked] = useState(
    currentUserProfile?.blockedUsers?.includes(profile.id) || false
  );

  // Check if current user already reported this profile
  const myExistingReport = reports.find(
    (r) => r.reportedUserId === profile.id && r.reporterId === currentUserProfile?.id
  );

  const handleReportSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!reportReason.trim()) return;

    fileReport(profile.id, profile.name, reportReason, reportDetails);
    setReportedSuccess(true);
    setShowReportForm(false);
  };

  const handleBlockToggle = () => {
    blockUser(profile.id);
    setIsBlocked(true);
  };

  const isSelf = currentUserProfile?.id === profile.id;
  const activeProfile = isSelf && currentUserProfile ? currentUserProfile : profile;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-2xl w-full max-w-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh] animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
        {/* Header Banner */}
        <div className="relative bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 via-indigo-950 to-slate-900 p-6 text-white">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-slate-400 hover:text-white p-1 rounded-lg"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>

          <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
            <UserAvatar
              name={activeProfile.name}
              avatar={activeProfile.avatar}
              size="xl"
              isVerified={activeProfile.assessmentStatus?.cppVerified}
              showBadge
            />
            <div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">{activeProfile.name}</h2>
                <span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-mono bg-indigo-500/30 text-indigo-200 border border-indigo-400/30">
                  ID: {activeProfile.studentIdNum}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-indigo-200 mt-1">
                {activeProfile.department} • {activeProfile.year}
              </p>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-300 mt-0.5">{activeProfile.email}</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {isSelf && currentUserProfile && (
            <div className="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-slate-800 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-indigo-200">
                Choose Profile Emoji / Avatar:
              </span>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1">
                {AVATAR_EMOJI_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
                  <button
                    key={opt.emoji}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      updateStudentProfile({
                        ...currentUserProfile,
                        avatar: opt.emoji,
                      })
                    }
                    title={opt.label}
                    className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-sm transition-all ${
                      (currentUserProfile.avatar || '👨‍💻') === opt.emoji
                        ? 'bg-indigo-500 text-white ring-2 ring-white/60 scale-105'
                        : 'bg-slate-800/80 hover:bg-slate-700'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {opt.emoji}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Content Body */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-5 text-xs text-slate-700">
          {/* Bio */}
          <div>
            <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-wider text-[11px] mb-1">
              About & Experience
            </h4>
            <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-100">
              {profile.bio || 'Student at Graphic Era University seeking collaborative engineering projects.'}
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* C/C++ Assessment Badge */}
          <div>
            <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-wider text-[11px] mb-2">
              Skill Verification Assessment
            </h4>
            {profile.assessmentStatus?.cppVerified ? (
              <div className="p-3 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600" />
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-bold text-emerald-900">
                      Verified C/C++ Developer ({profile.assessmentStatus.score}%)
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-[11px] text-emerald-700">
                      Anti-Cheat Cleared (0 flags) • {profile.assessmentStatus.feedbackSummary || 'Algorithms & RAII verified'}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-200 text-emerald-900">
                  VERIFIED
                </span>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-slate-500">
                Candidate has not completed the automated C/C++ skill verification test yet.
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Technical Skills & Interests */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-wider text-[11px] mb-2">
                Skills & Technologies
              </h4>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {profile.skills.map((s) => (
                  <span
                    key={s}
                    className="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-slate-100 text-slate-800 font-medium border border-slate-200"
                  >
                    {s}
                  </span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-wider text-[11px] mb-2">
                Interests & Availability
              </h4>
              <div className="text-slate-600 space-y-1">
                <div>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Commitment:</span>{' '}
                  {profile.availability || 'Flexible'}
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Interests:</span>{' '}
                  {profile.interests.join(', ') || 'Systems programming, Algorithms'}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* External Links & Portfolio */}
          <div>
            <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-wider text-[11px] mb-2">
              Portfolio & External Profiles
            </h4>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {profile.githubUrl && (
                <a
                  href={profile.githubUrl}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noreferrer"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 text-white font-medium hover:bg-slate-800"
                >
                  <Github className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> View GitHub
                </a>
              )}
              {profile.linkedinUrl && (
                <a
                  href={profile.linkedinUrl}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noreferrer"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-blue-600 text-white font-medium hover:bg-blue-700"
                >
                  <Linkedin className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> LinkedIn Profile
                </a>
              )}
              {profile.resumeFileName && (
                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 font-medium border border-indigo-200">
                  <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Resume: {profile.resumeFileName}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Reporting & Moderation Notice (Only visible to Reporter and Admin) */}
          {!isSelf && (
            <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-200 space-y-3">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-900">Safety & Profile Trust</h4>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                    If this profile exhibits fake claims or impersonation, report it directly to University Admin.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleBlockToggle}
                    className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 border ${
                      isBlocked
                        ? 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 border-slate-300'
                        : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 border-slate-200'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Ban className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    {isBlocked ? 'Blocked' : 'Block User'}
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowReportForm(!showReportForm)}
                    className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-semibold text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 flex items-center gap-1"
                  >
                    <Flag className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    Report Profile
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Status of report filed by current user */}
              {(myExistingReport || reportedSuccess) && (
                <div className="p-3 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg text-amber-900 text-xs">
                  <div className="font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <ShieldAlert className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600" />
                    Confidential Report Filed
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-800 mt-0.5">
                    Your report about this account is visible ONLY to you and the system administrator.
                    The admin will review the credentials and take appropriate moderation action.
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Report Input Form */}
              {showReportForm && (
                <form
                  onSubmit={handleReportSubmit}
                  className="p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl space-y-3"
                >
                  <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-xs">File Confidential Report to Admin</div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1">
                      Violation Category:
                    </label>
                    <select
                      value={reportReason}
                      onChange={(e) => setReportReason(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full p-2 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg text-xs bg-white"
                    >
                      <option value="Fraudulent credentials or fake GitHub">
                        Fraudulent credentials or fake GitHub link
                      </option>
                      <option value="Plagiarized project portfolio / fake experience">
                        Plagiarized project portfolio / fake experience
                      </option>
                      <option value="Inaccurate or false skill claims">
                        Inaccurate or false skill claims
                      </option>
                      <option value="Impersonation or abusive behavior">
                        Impersonation or abusive behavior
                      </option>
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1">
                      Supporting Details for Admin:
                    </label>
                    <textarea
                      value={reportDetails}
                      onChange={(e) => setReportDetails(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Explain what was incorrect or suspicious about this account..."
                      rows={2}
                      className="w-full p-2 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg text-xs bg-white"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-1">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setShowReportForm(false)}
                      className="px-3 py-1.5 text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-800"
                    >
                      Cancel
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      className="px-4 py-1.5 bg-rose-600 text-white rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-rose-700"
                    >
                      Submit Report
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Admin Supreme Action */}
          {role === 'ADMIN' && (
            <div className="pt-4 border-t border-rose-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-rose-50/50 p-3 rounded-xl">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-rose-900">
                Administrator Authority:
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  if (confirm(`Permanently purge and delete profile for ${profile.name}?`)) {
                    adminDeleteUser(profile.id);
                    onClose();
                  }
                }}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-rose-600 text-white rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-rose-700 text-xs flex items-center gap-1"
              >
                <Ban className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                Delete Profile Permanently
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useApp } from '../context/AppContext';
import { AVATAR_EMOJI_OPTIONS, UserAvatar } from './UserAvatar';
import {
  X,
  UserPlus,
  Mail,
  Lock,
  Upload,
  CheckCircle2,
  FileText,
  Sparkles,
  GraduationCap,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface StudentRegistrationModalProps {
  onClose: () => void;
  onRegisteredSuccess: () => void;
}

export const StudentRegistrationModal: React.FC<StudentRegistrationModalProps> = ({
  onClose,
  onRegisteredSuccess,
}) => {
  const { registerStudent } = useApp();

  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [selectedAvatar, setSelectedAvatar] = useState('👨‍💻');
  const [studentIdNum, setStudentIdNum] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [department, setDepartment] = useState('Computer Science & Engineering');
  const [year, setYear] = useState('3rd Year (B.Tech)');
  const [skillsInput, setSkillsInput] = useState('C++, Data Structures, Algorithms, JavaScript');
  const [interestsInput, setInterestsInput] = useState('Distributed Systems, Competitive Programming');
  const [availability, setAvailability] = useState('15 hrs/week (Evenings)');
  const [bio, setBio] = useState('Passionate software engineering student eager to build high performance projects.');
  const [githubUrl, setGithubUrl] = useState('https://github.com/my-profile');
  const [linkedinUrl, setLinkedinUrl] = useState('https://linkedin.com/in/my-profile');
  const [resumeFileName, setResumeFileName] = useState('Official_Resume.pdf');
  const [coverLetterText, setCoverLetterText] = useState('I am eager to contribute my algorithmic and programming expertise to the project team.');

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!name.trim() || !email.trim()) return;

    const skills = skillsInput
      .split(',')
      .map((s) => s.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);

    const interests = interestsInput
      .split(',')
      .map((s) => s.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);

    registerStudent({
      name: name.trim(),
      studentIdNum: studentIdNum.trim() || `2510${Math.floor(1000000 + Math.random() * 9000000)}`,
      email: email.trim(),
      department,
      year,
      avatar: selectedAvatar,
      bio: bio.trim(),
      skills,
      interests,
      availability,
      githubUrl: githubUrl.trim(),
      linkedinUrl: linkedinUrl.trim(),
      resumeFileName: resumeFileName.trim(),
      coverLetterFileName: 'Cover_Letter.pdf',
      coverLetterText: coverLetterText.trim(),
      password,
    });

    onRegisteredSuccess();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-2xl w-full max-w-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh] animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 via-indigo-950 to-slate-900 p-6 text-white flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-sm">
              <UserPlus className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-indigo-300">
                  <GraduationCap className="w-3.5 h-3.5 inline mr-1" /> Graphic Era University
                </span>
              </div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Student Registration & Profile Setup</h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">
                Register credentials to manage PDF documents, attempt C/C++ skill assessments, and match with 100+ teams.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white p-1 rounded-lg"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Form Body */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-4 text-xs">
          {/* Emoji / Avatar Picker */}
          <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <UserAvatar name={name || 'New Student'} avatar={selectedAvatar} size="lg" />
              <div>
                <div className="font-bold text-slate-800">Choose Profile Emoji / Avatar</div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                  Select an expressive emoji avatar for your student profile.
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
              {AVATAR_EMOJI_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
                <button
                  key={opt.emoji}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelectedAvatar(opt.emoji)}
                  title={opt.label}
                  className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-base transition-all border ${
                    selectedAvatar === opt.emoji
                      ? 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-600 ring-2 ring-indigo-500/20 scale-105'
                      : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300'
                  }`}
                >
                  {opt.emoji}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Credentials */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">Full Name *</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={name}
                onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. Shishir Dwivedi"
                className="w-full p-2.5 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500/20"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">University Student ID</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={studentIdNum}
                onChange={(e) => setStudentIdNum(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. 25101210684"
                className="w-full p-2.5 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500/20"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">University Email *</label>
              <input
                type="email"
                required
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="student.2510@gehu.ac.in"
                className="w-full p-2.5 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500/20"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">Password *</label>
              <input
                type="password"
                required
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="••••••••"
                className="w-full p-2.5 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500/20"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Academic Info */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">Department</label>
              <select
                value={department}
                onChange={(e) => setDepartment(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-2.5 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg bg-white"
              >
                <option value="Computer Science & Engineering">Computer Science & Engineering</option>
                <option value="Artificial Intelligence & Machine Learning">Artificial Intelligence & Machine Learning</option>
                <option value="Cybersecurity & Digital Forensics">Cybersecurity & Digital Forensics</option>
                <option value="Data Science & Big Data">Data Science & Big Data</option>
                <option value="Robotics, IoT & Embedded Systems">Robotics, IoT & Embedded Systems</option>
                <option value="Cloud Computing & DevOps">Cloud Computing & DevOps</option>
                <option value="Web3 & Blockchain Engineering">Web3 & Blockchain Engineering</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">Current Year / Cohort</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={year}
                onChange={(e) => setYear(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. 3rd Year (B.Tech DSCPP-III)"
                className="w-full p-2.5 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Skills and Availability */}
          <div className="space-y-3 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                Technical Skills (Comma-separated for C++ matching engine):
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={skillsInput}
                onChange={(e) => setSkillsInput(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="C++, C, Data Structures, Python, React, Tailwind CSS"
                className="w-full p-2.5 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">Interests / Focus</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={interestsInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setInterestsInput(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Algorithms, Systems, Web Development"
                  className="w-full p-2.5 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">Availability</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={availability}
                  onChange={(e) => setAvailability(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. 15 hrs/week (Evenings)"
                  className="w-full p-2.5 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Documents & Links */}
          <div className="space-y-3 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">GitHub Profile URL</label>
                <input
                  type="url"
                  value={githubUrl}
                  onChange={(e) => setGithubUrl(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="https://github.com/username"
                  className="w-full p-2.5 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">LinkedIn Profile URL</label>
                <input
                  type="url"
                  value={linkedinUrl}
                  onChange={(e) => setLinkedinUrl(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="https://linkedin.com/in/username"
                  className="w-full p-2.5 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">Resume Document (PDF Format)</label>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={resumeFileName}
                  onChange={(e) => setResumeFileName(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="My_Resume.pdf"
                  className="flex-1 p-2.5 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg font-mono text-[11px]"
                />
                <span className="px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 font-semibold border border-indigo-200 flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                  <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> PDF Attached
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">Cover Letter Statement</label>
              <textarea
                rows={2}
                value={coverLetterText}
                onChange={(e) => setCoverLetterText(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Brief intro for team leaders..."
                className="w-full p-2.5 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Action Bar */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-4 border-t border-slate-200">
            <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
              Instant login after submission with automatic team suggestions.
            </span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onClose}
                className="px-4 py-2 text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-800 font-medium"
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
              <button
                type="submit"
                className="px-5 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-lg font-bold shadow-md transition-colors"
              >
                Register & Find Teams
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
