  adminUpdateTeam: (teamId: string, updates: Partial<Team>) => void;
  adminDeleteTeam: (teamId: string) => void;
  adminAddMemberToTeam: (teamId: string, studentId: string, role?: 'Leader' | 'Member') => boolean;
  adminPromoteTeamLeader: (teamId: string, newLeaderId: string) => void;
  adminUpdateStudent: (studentId: string, updates: Partial<StudentProfile>) => void;
  adminSendTeamDirective: (teamId: string, content: string) => void;
  adminDeleteTeamChatMessage: (teamId: string, messageId: string) => void;
  addActivity: (
    type: ActivityLog['type'],
    description: string,
    actor: string,
    actorId?: string,
    actorEmail?: string
  ) => void;
  switchUser: (role: UserRole, userId?: string) => void;
}

const AppContext = createContext<AppContextType | undefined>(undefined);

const STORAGE_KEYS = {
  STUDENTS: 'skillmatch_students_v5',
  TEAMS: 'skillmatch_teams_v5',
  APPLICATIONS: 'skillmatch_applications_v2',
  CHATS: 'skillmatch_chats_v2',
  TEAM_CHATS: 'skillmatch_team_chats_v2',
  REPORTS: 'skillmatch_reports_v2',
  LOGS: 'skillmatch_logs_v2',
  SUBMISSIONS: 'skillmatch_submissions_v2',
  ADMIN_AUTH: 'skillmatch_admin_auth_v1',
};

export const AppProvider: React.FC<{ children: ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  // Current user state (empty by default so no student session is shown on the opening page)
  const [role, setRole] = useState<UserRole>('STUDENT');
  const [currentUserId, setCurrentUserId] = useState<string>('');
  const [isAdminAuthenticated, setIsAdminAuthenticated] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const [teams, setTeams] = useState<Team[]>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.TEAMS);
    return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : INITIAL_TEAMS;
  });

  // Core entities loaded from localStorage or seeded, guaranteed to contain all team leaders & members
  const [students, setStudents] = useState<StudentProfile[]>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.STUDENTS);
    const rawStudents: StudentProfile[] = saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : INITIAL_STUDENTS;
    const savedTeams = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.TEAMS);
    const rawTeams: Team[] = savedTeams ? JSON.parse(savedTeams) : INITIAL_TEAMS;
    return ensureAllTeamMembersInStudents(rawStudents, rawTeams);
  });

  const [applications, setApplications] = useState<TeamApplication[]>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.APPLICATIONS);
    return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : INITIAL_APPLICATIONS;
  });

  const [chatThreads, setChatThreads] = useState<Record<string, ApplicationChatThread>>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.CHATS);
    return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : INITIAL_CHAT_THREADS;
  });

  const [teamChatThreads, setTeamChatThreads] = useState<Record<string, ChatMessage[]>>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.TEAM_CHATS);
    return saved
      ? JSON.parse(saved)
      : {
          'team-sigma': [
            {
              id: 'team-msg-init',
              senderId: 'student-shreya',
              senderName: 'Shreya Yadav (Team Lead)',
              senderRole: 'Team Leader',
              content: 'Welcome to Team Sigma project group chat! All approved candidate discussions and deliverables will sync here.',
              timestamp: '10:00 AM',
            },
          ],
        };
  });

  const [reports, setReports] = useState<ReportItem[]>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.REPORTS);
    return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : INITIAL_REPORTS;
  });

  const [activityLogs, setActivityLogs] = useState<ActivityLog[]>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.LOGS);
    return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : INITIAL_ACTIVITIES;
  });

  const [assessmentSubmissions, setAssessmentSubmissions] = useState<AssessmentSubmission[]>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.SUBMISSIONS);
    return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : INITIAL_SUBMISSIONS;
  });

  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<AppNotification[]>([
    {
      id: 'notif-1',
      type: 'APPLICATION',
      title: 'New Team Application',
      message: 'Shishir Dwivedi applied to Team Sigma (Match: 98%, Verified C++ score: 95%).',
      timestamp: 'Just now',
      actionPayload: { teamId: 'team-sigma', applicationId: 'app-sigma-shishir' },
      read: false,
    },
  ]);

  const [activeNotificationPopup, setActiveNotificationPopup] = useState<AppNotification | null>(null);

  const [siteSettings, setSiteSettings] = useState<SiteSettings>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('skillmatch_settings_v1');
    return saved
      ? JSON.parse(saved)
      : {
          maintenanceMode: false,
          eligibilityThreshold: 70,
          allowNewRegistrations: true,
          allowPublicApplications: true,
          announcementBanner: 'Graphic Era University Spring 2026 Academic Project Matchmaking Active',
        };
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem('skillmatch_settings_v1', JSON.stringify(siteSettings));
  }, [siteSettings]);

  // Sync state to localStorage
  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.STUDENTS, JSON.stringify(students));
  }, [students]);

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.TEAMS, JSON.stringify(teams));
  }, [teams]);

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.APPLICATIONS, JSON.stringify(applications));
  }, [applications]);

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.CHATS, JSON.stringify(chatThreads));
  }, [chatThreads]);

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.TEAM_CHATS, JSON.stringify(teamChatThreads));
  }, [teamChatThreads]);

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.REPORTS, JSON.stringify(reports));
  }, [reports]);

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.LOGS, JSON.stringify(activityLogs));
  }, [activityLogs]);

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.SUBMISSIONS, JSON.stringify(assessmentSubmissions));
  }, [assessmentSubmissions]);

  const currentUserProfile = students.find((s) => s.id === currentUserId) || null;

  const addActivity = (
    type: ActivityLog['type'],
    description: string,
    actor: string,
    actorId?: string,
    actorEmail?: string
  ) => {
    const formattedTime =
      new Date().toLocaleDateString() +
      ' ' +
      new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    const newLog: ActivityLog = {
      id: `act-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 6)}`,
      type,
      description,
      actor,
      actorId: actorId || currentUserProfile?.id || currentUserId,
      actorEmail: actorEmail || currentUserProfile?.email,
      timestamp: formattedTime,
    };
    setActivityLogs((prev) => [newLog, ...prev]);
  };

  const loginAdmin = (username: string, password: string): boolean => {
    const valid = verifyAdminCredentials(username, password);
    if (valid) {
      setIsAdminAuthenticated(true);
      sessionStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.ADMIN_AUTH, 'true');
      setRole('ADMIN');
      setCurrentUserId('admin-system');
      addActivity(
        'LOGIN',
        `Supreme Admin (${username.trim()}) authenticated and accessed website control center.`,
        username.trim(),
        'admin-system',
        'admin@skillmatch.geu.ac.in'
      );
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  };

  const logoutAdmin = () => {
    setIsAdminAuthenticated(false);
    sessionStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEYS.ADMIN_AUTH);
    setRole('STUDENT');
    setCurrentUserId('');
  };

  const logoutUser = () => {
    setIsAdminAuthenticated(false);
    sessionStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEYS.ADMIN_AUTH);
    setRole('STUDENT');
    setCurrentUserId('');
  };

  const loginUserAccount = (
    emailOrId: string,
    password?: string,
    targetRole: 'STUDENT' | 'TEAM_LEADER' = 'STUDENT'
  ): StudentProfile | null => {
    const clean = emailOrId.trim().toLowerCase();
    const matched = students.find(
      (s) =>
        s.email.toLowerCase() === clean ||
        s.studentIdNum.toLowerCase() === clean ||
        s.id.toLowerCase() === clean
    );
    if (!matched) return null;
    if (matched.password && password && matched.password !== password) {
      return null;
    }
    setCurrentUserId(matched.id);
    setRole(targetRole);
    addActivity(
      'LOGIN',
      `${matched.name} signed in to the ${targetRole === 'TEAM_LEADER' ? 'Team Leader' : 'Team Member'} portal.`,
      matched.name,
      matched.id,
      matched.email
    );
    return matched;
  };

  const registerStudent = (
    data: Omit<StudentProfile, 'id' | 'certificates' | 'blockedUsers' | 'isBanned'> & { password?: string }
  ): StudentProfile => {
    const newId = `student-${Date.now()}`;
    const nowStr =
      new Date().toLocaleDateString() +
      ' ' +
      new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    const newStudent: StudentProfile = {
      ...data,
      id: newId,
      accountType: data.accountType || 'TEAM_MEMBER',
      createdAt: nowStr,
      certificates: [],
      blockedUsers: [],
      isBanned: false,
      assessmentStatus: {
        cppVerified: false,
        cVerified: false,
        score: 0,
        verdict: 'NOT_ATTEMPTED',
        cheatFlags: 0,
      },
    };

    setStudents((prev) => [newStudent, ...prev]);
    setCurrentUserId(newId);
    setRole('STUDENT');

    addActivity(
      'REGISTRATION',
      `New Team Member account created for ${newStudent.name} (${newStudent.department}). Matched with teams for skills: [${newStudent.skills.join(', ')}] & interests: [${newStudent.interests.join(', ')}].`,
      newStudent.name,
      newId,
      newStudent.email
    );

    return newStudent;
  };

  const createLeaderAndProject = (
    leaderData: Omit<StudentProfile, 'id' | 'certificates' | 'blockedUsers' | 'isBanned'> & {
      password?: string;
    },
    projectData: NewProjectPayload
  ): { leader: StudentProfile; team: Team } => {
    const nowStr =
      new Date().toLocaleDateString() +
      ' ' +
      new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    const leaderId = `leader-${Date.now()}`;
    const newLeader: StudentProfile = {
      ...leaderData,
      id: leaderId,
      accountType: 'TEAM_LEADER',
      createdAt: nowStr,
      certificates: [],
      blockedUsers: [],
      isBanned: false,
      assessmentStatus: {
        cppVerified: true,
        cVerified: true,
        score: 92,
        verdict: 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE',
        cheatFlags: 0,
        completedAt: nowStr,
      },
    };

    const teamId = `team-proj-${Date.now()}`;
    const gradients = [
      'from-indigo-600 via-violet-600 to-purple-700',
      'from-emerald-600 via-teal-600 to-cyan-700',
      'from-blue-600 via-indigo-600 to-slate-800',
      'from-amber-600 via-orange-600 to-rose-700',
    ];
    const newTeam: Team = {
      id: teamId,
      title: projectData.title.trim(),
      department: projectData.department,
      category: projectData.category,
      description: projectData.description.trim(),
      requiredSkills:
        projectData.requiredSkills.length > 0 ? projectData.requiredSkills : ['C++', 'Algorithms'],
      teamSize: Math.max(2, Number(projectData.teamSize) || 4),
      leaderId: newLeader.id,
      leaderName: newLeader.name,
      leaderEmail: newLeader.email,
      members: [
        {
          id: newLeader.id,
          name: newLeader.name,
          email: newLeader.email,
          role: 'Leader',
          skills: newLeader.skills,
          avatar: newLeader.avatar || '🚀',
        },
      ],
      vacancyOpen: true,
      deadline: projectData.deadline || '2026-12-30',
      tags: projectData.tags.length > 0 ? projectData.tags : [projectData.category, 'C++'],
      bannerGradient: gradients[Math.floor(Math.random() * gradients.length)],
      createdAt: nowStr,
    };

    setStudents((prev) => [newLeader, ...prev]);
    setTeams((prev) => [newTeam, ...prev]);
    setTeamChatThreads((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [teamId]: [
        {
          id: `team-msg-init-${Date.now()}`,
          senderId: newLeader.id,
          senderName: `${newLeader.name} (Team Lead)`,
          senderRole: 'Team Leader',
          content: `Welcome to the official project room for "${newTeam.title}"! Required skills: ${newTeam.requiredSkills.join(', ')}.`,
          timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
        },
      ],
    }));

    setCurrentUserId(newLeader.id);
    setRole('TEAM_LEADER');

    // Log both account creation and project creation under leaderId
    const regLog: ActivityLog = {
      id: `act-reg-${Date.now()}`,
      type: 'REGISTRATION',
      description: `New Team Leader account created by ${newLeader.name} (${newLeader.email}).`,
      actor: newLeader.name,
      actorId: newLeader.id,
      actorEmail: newLeader.email,
      timestamp: nowStr,
    };
    const projLog: ActivityLog = {
      id: `act-proj-${Date.now() + 1}`,
      type: 'PROJECT_CREATED',
      description: `Team Leader ${newLeader.name} created new project "${newTeam.title}" (${newTeam.category}) requiring [${newTeam.requiredSkills.join(', ')}] with ${newTeam.teamSize} total slots.`,
      actor: newLeader.name,
      actorId: newLeader.id,
      actorEmail: newLeader.email,
      timestamp: nowStr,
    };
    setActivityLogs((prev) => [projLog, regLog, ...prev]);

    return { leader: newLeader, team: newTeam };
  };

  const createProjectForCurrentLeader = (projectData: NewProjectPayload): Team | null => {
    const leader = currentUserProfile;
    if (!leader) return null;

    const nowStr =
      new Date().toLocaleDateString() +
      ' ' +
      new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    const teamId = `team-proj-${Date.now()}`;
    const newTeam: Team = {
      id: teamId,
      title: projectData.title.trim(),
      department: projectData.department || leader.department,
      category: projectData.category,
      description: projectData.description.trim(),
      requiredSkills:
        projectData.requiredSkills.length > 0 ? projectData.requiredSkills : ['C++', 'Algorithms'],
      teamSize: Math.max(2, Number(projectData.teamSize) || 4),
      leaderId: leader.id,
      leaderName: leader.name,
      leaderEmail: leader.email,
      members: [
        {
          id: leader.id,
          name: leader.name,
          email: leader.email,
          role: 'Leader',
          skills: leader.skills,
          avatar: leader.avatar || '🚀',
        },
      ],
      vacancyOpen: true,
      deadline: projectData.deadline || '2026-12-30',
      tags: projectData.tags.length > 0 ? projectData.tags : [projectData.category],
      bannerGradient: 'from-indigo-600 via-violet-600 to-purple-700',
      createdAt: nowStr,
    };

    setTeams((prev) => [newTeam, ...prev]);
    setTeamChatThreads((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [teamId]: [
        {
          id: `team-msg-init-${Date.now()}`,
          senderId: leader.id,
          senderName: `${leader.name} (Team Lead)`,
          senderRole: 'Team Leader',
          content: `Project room initialized for "${newTeam.title}".`,
          timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
        },
      ],
    }));

    addActivity(
      'PROJECT_CREATED',
      `Team Leader ${leader.name} launched new project "${newTeam.title}" requiring [${newTeam.requiredSkills.join(', ')}] (${newTeam.teamSize} slots).`,
      leader.name,
      leader.id,
      leader.email
    );

    return newTeam;
  };

  const updateTeamProjectDetails = (teamId: string, updates: Partial<NewProjectPayload>) => {
    setTeams((prev) =>
      prev.map((t) => (t.id === teamId ? { ...t, ...updates } : t))
    );
    if (currentUserProfile) {
      addActivity(
        'PROFILE_UPDATE',
        `Team Leader ${currentUserProfile.name} updated project details & requirements for ${updates.title || teamId}.`,
        currentUserProfile.name,
        currentUserProfile.id,
        currentUserProfile.email
      );
    }
  };

  const updateStudentProfile = (updated: StudentProfile) => {
    setStudents((prev) => prev.map((s) => (s.id === updated.id ? updated : s)));
    addActivity('PROFILE_UPDATE', `Profile updated for ${updated.name}`, updated.name, updated.id, updated.email);
  };

  const applyToTeam = (teamId: string, message: string): boolean => {
    const team = teams.find((t) => t.id === teamId);
    const student = currentUserProfile;
    if (!team || !student) return false;

    if (!team.vacancyOpen || team.members.length >= team.teamSize) {
      return false;
    }

    const matchCalc = MatchingEngine.calculateDetailedScore(student.skills, team.requiredSkills, defaultSkillGraph);

    const newAppId = `app-${Date.now()}`;
    const newApp: TeamApplication = {
      id: newAppId,
      teamId: team.id,
      teamTitle: team.title,
      studentId: student.id,
      studentName: student.name,
      studentEmail: student.email,
      studentSkills: student.skills,
      status: 'PENDING',
      appliedAt: new Date().toLocaleDateString() + ' ' + new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
      message,
      matchScore: matchCalc.score,
      assessmentScore: student.assessmentStatus?.score,
      assessmentVerdict: student.assessmentStatus?.verdict,
    };

    setApplications((prev) => [newApp, ...prev]);

    // Create 1-on-1 application chat thread
    const newThread: ApplicationChatThread = {
      applicationId: newAppId,
      teamId: team.id,
      studentId: student.id,
      transferredToTeamChat: false,
      messages: [
        {
          id: `msg-${Date.now()}`,
          senderId: student.id,
          senderName: student.name,
          senderRole: 'Applicant',
          content: message || `Hello! I would love to join ${team.title}. My compatibility score is ${matchCalc.score}%.`,
          timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
        },
      ],
    };

    setChatThreads((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [newAppId]: newThread,
    }));

    // Trigger popup for team leader!
    const notif: AppNotification = {
      id: `notif-${Date.now()}`,
      type: 'APPLICATION',
      title: `New Applicant for ${team.title}`,
      message: `${student.name} applied with ${matchCalc.score}% match score! A dedicated chat is open.`,
      timestamp: 'Just now',
      actionPayload: { teamId: team.id, applicationId: newAppId },
      read: false,
    };

    setNotifications((prev) => [notif, ...prev]);
    setActiveNotificationPopup(notif);

    addActivity('APPLICATION', `${student.name} applied for invitation to ${team.title}.`, student.name);

    return true;
  };

  const approveApplication = (applicationId: string) => {
    const app = applications.find((a) => a.id === applicationId);
    if (!app || app.status !== 'PENDING') return;

    const team = teams.find((t) => t.id === app.teamId);
    const applicant = students.find((s) => s.id === app.studentId);
    if (!team || !applicant) return;

    // 1. Mark application as APPROVED
    setApplications((prev) =>
      prev.map((a) => (a.id === applicationId ? { ...a, status: 'APPROVED' } : a))
    );

    // 2. Add applicant to team members
    const newMember = {
      id: applicant.id,
      name: applicant.name,
      email: applicant.email,
      role: 'Member' as const,
      skills: applicant.skills,
      avatar: applicant.avatar,
    };

    const updatedMembers = [...team.members, newMember];
    const isNowFull = updatedMembers.length >= team.teamSize;

    setTeams((prev) =>
      prev.map((t) =>
        t.id === team.id
          ? {
              ...t,
              members: updatedMembers,
              vacancyOpen: isNowFull ? false : t.vacancyOpen,
            }
          : t
      )
    );

    // 3. TRANSFER CHAT: Move 1-on-1 chat history directly into the Team Chatbox!
    const applicantThread = chatThreads[applicationId];
    const transferredMessages = applicantThread ? applicantThread.messages : [];

    const approvalNotice: ChatMessage = {
      id: `sys-approve-${Date.now()}`,
      senderId: 'system',
      senderName: 'SkillMatch System',
      senderRole: 'System',
      content: `🎉 ${applicant.name} was officially APPROVED and joined ${team.title}! Previous applicant conversation has been merged into this team room.`,
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
      isSystemNotice: true,
    };

    setTeamChatThreads((prev) => {
      const existingTeamChat = prev[team.id] || [];
      return {
        ...prev,
        [team.id]: [...existingTeamChat, approvalNotice, ...transferredMessages],
      };
    });

    setChatThreads((prev) => {
      if (!prev[applicationId]) return prev;
      return {
        ...prev,
        [applicationId]: {
          ...prev[applicationId],
          transferredToTeamChat: true,
        },
      };
    });

    addActivity('APPROVAL', `${team.leaderName} approved ${applicant.name} to join ${team.title}.`, team.leaderName);
  };

  const rejectApplication = (applicationId: string) => {
    const app = applications.find((a) => a.id === applicationId);
    if (!app) return;

    setApplications((prev) =>
      prev.map((a) => (a.id === applicationId ? { ...a, status: 'REJECTED' } : a))
    );

    addActivity('REJECTION', `Application for ${app.studentName} was declined.`, 'Team Leader');
  };

  const removeMemberFromTeam = (teamId: string, memberId: string) => {
    setTeams((prev) =>
      prev.map((t) => {
        if (t.id === teamId) {
          const filtered = t.members.filter((m) => m.id !== memberId);
          return {
            ...t,
            members: filtered,
            vacancyOpen: true, // open vacancy back up
          };
        }
        return t;
      })
    );

    addActivity('MODERATION', `A member was removed from ${teamId} by team leader.`, 'Team Leader');
  };

  const toggleTeamVacancy = (teamId: string, open?: boolean) => {
    setTeams((prev) =>
      prev.map((t) => {
        if (t.id === teamId) {
          const newStatus = open !== undefined ? open : !t.vacancyOpen;
          return { ...t, vacancyOpen: newStatus };
        }
        return t;
      })
    );

    addActivity('VACANCY_TOGGLE', `Vacancy status updated for team ${teamId}`, 'Team Leader');
  };

  const sendChatMessage = (applicationId: string, content: string) => {
    const sender = currentUserProfile;
    if (!sender || !content.trim()) return;

    const newMessage: ChatMessage = {
      id: `msg-${Date.now()}`,
      senderId: sender.id,
      senderName: sender.name,
      senderRole: role === 'TEAM_LEADER' ? 'Team Leader' : 'Applicant',
      content: content.trim(),
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
    };

    setChatThreads((prev) => {
      const thread = prev[applicationId];
      if (!thread) return prev;
      return {
        ...prev,
        [applicationId]: {
          ...thread,
          messages: [...thread.messages, newMessage],
        },
      };
    });
  };

  const sendTeamChatMessage = (teamId: string, content: string) => {
    const sender = currentUserProfile;
    if (!sender || !content.trim()) return;

    const newMessage: ChatMessage = {
      id: `team-msg-${Date.now()}`,
      senderId: sender.id,
      senderName: sender.name,
      senderRole: role === 'TEAM_LEADER' ? 'Team Leader' : 'Member',
      content: content.trim(),
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
    };

    setTeamChatThreads((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [teamId]: [...(prev[teamId] || []), newMessage],
    }));
  };

  const submitAssessment = (submission: Omit<AssessmentSubmission, 'id' | 'submittedAt'>): AssessmentSubmission => {
    const newSubmission: AssessmentSubmission = {
      ...submission,
      id: `sub-${Date.now()}`,
      submittedAt: new Date().toLocaleDateString() + ' ' + new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
    };

    setAssessmentSubmissions((prev) => [newSubmission, ...prev]);

    // Update student's assessmentStatus
    setStudents((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => {
        if (s.id === submission.candidateId) {
          return {
            ...s,
            assessmentStatus: {
              cppVerified: submission.language === 'cpp' && submission.verdict === 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE',
              cVerified: submission.language === 'c' && submission.verdict === 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE',
              score: submission.score,
              verdict: submission.verdict,
              cheatFlags: submission.tabSwitchCount + submission.copyPasteCount,
              completedAt: newSubmission.submittedAt,
              feedbackSummary: submission.personalizedFeedback,
            },
          };
        }
        return s;
      })
    );

    addActivity(
      'ASSESSMENT',
      `${submission.candidateName} completed ${submission.language.toUpperCase()} Assessment (${submission.score}%, ${submission.verdict}).`,
      submission.candidateName
    );

    return newSubmission;
  };

  const fileReport = (reportedUserId: string, reportedUserName: string, reason: string, details: string) => {
    const reporter = currentUserProfile;
    if (!reporter) return;

    const newReport: ReportItem = {
      id: `rep-${Date.now()}`,
      reportedUserId,
      reportedUserName,
      reporterId: reporter.id,
      reporterName: reporter.name,
      reporterEmail: reporter.email,
      reason,
      details,
      status: 'PENDING',
      createdAt: new Date().toLocaleDateString() + ' ' + new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
    };

    setReports((prev) => [newReport, ...prev]);

    addActivity('REPORT', `Report filed against ${reportedUserName} for "${reason}".`, reporter.name);
  };

  const blockUser = (targetUserId: string) => {
    if (!currentUserProfile) return;
    setStudents((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => {
        if (s.id === currentUserProfile.id) {
          const existing = s.blockedUsers || [];
          return { ...s, blockedUsers: Array.from(new Set([...existing, targetUserId])) };
        }
        return s;
      })
    );
  };

  const unblockUser = (targetUserId: string) => {
    if (!currentUserProfile) return;
    setStudents((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => {
        if (s.id === currentUserProfile.id) {
          return {
            ...s,
            blockedUsers: (s.blockedUsers || []).filter((id) => id !== targetUserId),
          };
        }
        return s;
      })
    );
  };

  const escalateReport = (reportId: string) => {
    setReports((prev) =>
      prev.map((r) =>
        r.id === reportId
          ? {
              ...r,
              isEscalated: true,
              escalatedAt: new Date().toLocaleDateString() + ' ' + new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
            }
          : r
      )
    );
    addActivity('REPORT', `Report #${reportId} was escalated to Senior University Disciplinary Board.`, 'Complainant');
  };

  const updateSiteSettings = (newSettings: Partial<SiteSettings>) => {
    setSiteSettings((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...newSettings }));
    addActivity('MODERATION', 'Administrator updated site-wide system settings', 'Administrator');
  };

  const adminDeleteUser = (userId: string) => {
    const target = students.find((s) => s.id === userId);
    const targetName = target ? target.name : userId;

    // Purge student
    setStudents((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.id !== userId));

    // Remove from any teams
    setTeams((prev) =>
      prev.map((t) => ({
        ...t,
        members: t.members.filter((m) => m.id !== userId),
      }))
    );

    // Resolve reports about this user
    setReports((prev) =>
      prev.map((r) => (r.reportedUserId === userId ? { ...r, status: 'RESOLVED_BANNED', adminNotes: 'Profile deleted by Admin.' } : r))
    );

    addActivity('MODERATION', `Admin permanently removed user profile: ${targetName}.`, 'Administrator');
  };

  const adminResolveReport = (reportId: string, action: 'WARN' | 'BAN_DELETE' | 'DISMISS') => {
    const report = reports.find((r) => r.id === reportId);
    if (!report) return;

    if (action === 'BAN_DELETE') {
      adminDeleteUser(report.reportedUserId);
    } else {
      setReports((prev) =>
        prev.map((r) =>
          r.id === reportId
            ? {
                ...r,
                status: action === 'WARN' ? 'RESOLVED_WARNED' : 'DISMISSED',
                adminNotes: action === 'WARN' ? 'Warning issued to candidate' : 'Reviewed and dismissed',
              }
            : r
        )
      );
    }

    addActivity('MODERATION', `Admin resolved report #${reportId} (${action}).`, 'Administrator');
  };

  const adminUpdateTeam = (teamId: string, updates: Partial<Team>) => {
    setTeams((prev) =>
      prev.map((t) => (t.id === teamId ? { ...t, ...updates } : t))
    );
    addActivity(
      'MODERATION',
      `Admin updated configuration/details for team ${updates.title || teamId}.`,
      'Supreme Admin',
      'admin-system',
      'admin@skillmatch.geu.ac.in'
    );
  };

  const adminDeleteTeam = (teamId: string) => {
    const target = teams.find((t) => t.id === teamId);
    const title = target ? target.title : teamId;
    setTeams((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== teamId));
    setApplications((prev) => prev.filter((a) => a.teamId !== teamId));
    addActivity(
      'MODERATION',
      `Admin dissolved and permanently deleted project team "${title}" (${teamId}).`,
      'Supreme Admin',
      'admin-system',
      'admin@skillmatch.geu.ac.in'
    );
  };

  const adminAddMemberToTeam = (
    teamId: string,
    studentId: string,
    memberRole: 'Leader' | 'Member' = 'Member'
  ): boolean => {
    const student = students.find((s) => s.id === studentId);
    const team = teams.find((t) => t.id === teamId);
    if (!student || !team) return false;

    setTeams((prev) =>
      prev.map((t) => {
        if (t.id !== teamId) return t;
        const alreadyIn = t.members.some((m) => m.id === student.id);
        let updatedMembers = alreadyIn
          ? t.members.map((m) => (m.id === student.id ? { ...m, role: memberRole } : m))
          : [
              ...t.members,
              {
                id: student.id,
                name: student.name,
                email: student.email,
                role: memberRole,
                skills: student.skills.slice(0, 4),
                avatar: student.avatar || '👨‍💻',
              },
            ];
        if (memberRole === 'Leader') {
          updatedMembers = updatedMembers.map((m) =>
            m.id === student.id ? { ...m, role: 'Leader' } : { ...m, role: 'Member' }
          );
        }
        const nextCapacity = Math.max(t.teamSize, updatedMembers.length);
        return {
          ...t,
          members: updatedMembers,
          teamSize: nextCapacity,
          ...(memberRole === 'Leader'
            ? {
                leaderId: student.id,
                leaderName: student.name,
                leaderEmail: student.email,
              }
            : {}),
        };
      })
    );

    if (memberRole === 'Leader') {
      setStudents((prev) =>
        prev.map((s) => (s.id === student.id ? { ...s, accountType: 'TEAM_LEADER' } : s))
      );
    }

    addActivity(
      'MODERATION',
      `Admin added ${student.name} (${student.email}) to "${team.title}" as ${memberRole}.`,
      'Supreme Admin',
      'admin-system',
      'admin@skillmatch.geu.ac.in'
    );
    return true;
  };

  const adminPromoteTeamLeader = (teamId: string, newLeaderId: string) => {
    const student = students.find((s) => s.id === newLeaderId);
    const team = teams.find((t) => t.id === teamId);
    if (!student || !team) return;

    setTeams((prev) =>
      prev.map((t) => {
        if (t.id !== teamId) return t;
        const hasMember = t.members.some((m) => m.id === student.id);
        const nextMembers = (hasMember
          ? t.members
          : [
              {
                id: student.id,
                name: student.name,
                email: student.email,
                role: 'Leader' as const,
                skills: student.skills.slice(0, 4),
                avatar: student.avatar || '🚀',
              },
              ...t.members,
            ]
        ).map((m) => ({
          ...m,
          role: (m.id === student.id ? 'Leader' : 'Member') as 'Leader' | 'Member',
        }));

        return {
          ...t,
          leaderId: student.id,
          leaderName: student.name,
          leaderEmail: student.email,
          members: nextMembers,
          teamSize: Math.max(t.teamSize, nextMembers.length),
        };
      })
    );

    setStudents((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => (s.id === student.id ? { ...s, accountType: 'TEAM_LEADER' } : s))
    );

    addActivity(
      'MODERATION',
      `Admin reassigned Team Leader of "${team.title}" to ${student.name} (${student.email}).`,
      'Supreme Admin',
      'admin-system',
      'admin@skillmatch.geu.ac.in'
    );
  };

  const adminUpdateStudent = (studentId: string, updates: Partial<StudentProfile>) => {
    const target = students.find((s) => s.id === studentId);
    setStudents((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => (s.id === studentId ? { ...s, ...updates } : s))
    );
    if (updates.name || updates.email) {
      setTeams((prev) =>
        prev.map((t) => ({
          ...t,
          leaderName: t.leaderId === studentId && updates.name ? updates.name : t.leaderName,
          leaderEmail: t.leaderId === studentId && updates.email ? updates.email : t.leaderEmail,
          members: t.members.map((m) =>
            m.id === studentId
              ? {
                  ...m,
                  name: updates.name || m.name,
                  email: updates.email || m.email,
                  skills: updates.skills || m.skills,
                }
              : m
          ),
        }))
      );
    }
    addActivity(
      'MODERATION',
      `Admin modified account/credentials for ${target?.name || studentId}.`,
      'Supreme Admin',
      'admin-system',
      'admin@skillmatch.geu.ac.in'
    );
  };

  const adminSendTeamDirective = (teamId: string, content: string) => {
    if (!content.trim()) return;
    const team = teams.find((t) => t.id === teamId);
    const msg: ChatMessage = {
      id: `admin-dir-${Date.now()}`,
      senderId: 'admin-system',
      senderName: 'Supreme Admin (Platform Authority)',
      senderRole: 'ADMIN DIRECTIVE',
      content: content.trim(),
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
      isSystemNotice: true,
    };
    setTeamChatThreads((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [teamId]: [...(prev[teamId] || []), msg],
    }));
    addActivity(
      'MODERATION',
      `Admin broadcasted official directive to "${team?.title || teamId}": "${content.trim()}"`,
      'Supreme Admin',
      'admin-system',
      'admin@skillmatch.geu.ac.in'
    );
  };

  const adminDeleteTeamChatMessage = (teamId: string, messageId: string) => {
    setTeamChatThreads((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [teamId]: (prev[teamId] || []).filter((m) => m.id !== messageId),
    }));
    addActivity(
      'MODERATION',
      `Admin removed chat message (${messageId}) from team ${teamId}.`,
      'Supreme Admin',
      'admin-system',
      'admin@skillmatch.geu.ac.in'
    );
  };

  const clearNotificationPopup = () => {
    setActiveNotificationPopup(null);
  };

  const switchUser = (newRole: UserRole, targetId?: string) => {
    setRole(newRole);
    if (newRole === 'ADMIN') {
      setCurrentUserId('admin-system');
    } else if (newRole === 'TEAM_LEADER') {
      setCurrentUserId(targetId || 'student-shreya');
    } else {
      setCurrentUserId(targetId || 'student-shishir');
    }
  };

  return (
    <AppContext.Provider
      value={{
        role,
        setRole,
        currentUserId,
        setCurrentUserId,
        currentUserProfile,
        isAdminAuthenticated,
        loginAdmin,
        logoutAdmin,
        logoutUser,
        loginUserAccount,
        createLeaderAndProject,
        createProjectForCurrentLeader,
        updateTeamProjectDetails,
        students,
        teams,
        applications,
        chatThreads,
        teamChatThreads,
        reports,
        activityLogs,
        assessmentSubmissions,
        notifications,
        activeNotificationPopup,
        clearNotificationPopup,
        siteSettings,
        updateSiteSettings,
        escalateReport,
        unblockUser,
        registerStudent,
        updateStudentProfile,
        applyToTeam,
        approveApplication,
        rejectApplication,
        removeMemberFromTeam,
        toggleTeamVacancy,
        sendChatMessage,
        sendTeamChatMessage,
        submitAssessment,
        fileReport,
        blockUser,
        adminDeleteUser,
        adminResolveReport,
        adminUpdateTeam,
        adminDeleteTeam,
        adminAddMemberToTeam,
        adminPromoteTeamLeader,
        adminUpdateStudent,
        adminSendTeamDirective,
        adminDeleteTeamChatMessage,
        addActivity,
        switchUser,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AppContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useApp = () => {
  const context = useContext(AppContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useApp must be used within an AppProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { AssessmentEngine, C_CPP_CHALLENGES } from './assessmentEngine';

test('AssessmentEngine evaluates valid C++ code with 100% and low cheat suspicion', () => {
  const challenge = C_CPP_CHALLENGES.find((c) => c.id === 'cpp-smart-vector')!;
  const result = AssessmentEngine.evaluateCode(challenge.starterCode, challenge, {
    timeSpentSeconds: 300,
    tabSwitchCount: 0,
    copyPasteCount: 0,
  });

  assert.equal(result.syntaxValid, true);
  assert.equal(result.verdict, 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE');
  assert.equal(result.cheatSuspicion, 'LOW');
  assert.ok(result.score >= 80);
  assert.ok(result.personalizedFeedback.includes('VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE') || result.personalizedFeedback.includes('recommending') || result.personalizedFeedback.includes('proficiency'));
});

test('AssessmentEngine flags memory leak when new is used without delete', () => {
  const challenge = C_CPP_CHALLENGES.find((c) => c.id === 'cpp-smart-vector')!;
  const leakyCode = `
#include <iostream>
class CustomVector {
  int* data;
public:
  CustomVector() { data = new int[10]; }
  // Destructor missing!
};
int main() { return 0; }
  `;
  const result = AssessmentEngine.evaluateCode(leakyCode, challenge, {
    timeSpentSeconds: 120,
    tabSwitchCount: 0,
    copyPasteCount: 0,
  });

  assert.equal(result.detailedAudit.hasDestructorOrFree, false);
  assert.ok(result.memorySafetyNotes.some((n) => n.includes('Memory leak')));
});

test('AssessmentEngine flags high cheat suspicion for excessive tab switching', () => {
  const challenge = C_CPP_CHALLENGES.find((c) => c.id === 'c-pointer-string-reverse')!;
  const result = AssessmentEngine.evaluateCode(challenge.starterCode, challenge, {
    timeSpentSeconds: 10,
    tabSwitchCount: 5,
    copyPasteCount: 3,
  });

  assert.equal(result.cheatSuspicion, 'HIGH');
  assert.notEqual(result.verdict, 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE');
});

/**
 * Skill Assessment Engine for C & C++ Developer Proficiency
 * Features:
 * - Anti-cheat telemetry (tab switch detection, copy-paste tracking, time analysis)
 * - Automated code quality analysis (syntax, memory safety, time/space complexity)
 * - Test runner with public and hidden edge cases
 * - Automated personalized feedback generation sent to recruiting team
 */

export interface CodeChallenge {
  id: string;
  language: 'cpp' | 'c';
  title: string;
  difficulty: 'Beginner' | 'Intermediate' | 'Advanced';
  timeLimitMinutes: number;
  description: string;
  instructions: string[];
  starterCode: string;
  solutionTemplate: string;
  testCases: Array<{
    id: string;
    input: string;
    expectedOutput: string;
    isHidden: boolean;
    description: string;
  }>;
}

export const C_CPP_CHALLENGES: CodeChallenge[] = [
  {
    id: 'cpp-smart-vector',
    language: 'cpp',
    title: 'C++ Custom Dynamic Vector & Memory Management',
    difficulty: 'Intermediate',
    timeLimitMinutes: 20,
    description:
      'Implement a dynamic integer array container in C++ that manages heap memory using RAII principles, preventing leaks and handling capacity growth doubling.',
    instructions: [
      'Define a class `CustomVector` with dynamic `int* data`, `size_t sz`, and `size_t cap`.',
      'Provide a constructor initializing capacity = 2, and a destructor freeing allocated memory with `delete[]`.',
      'Implement `void push_back(int val)` that doubles capacity whenever size reaches capacity.',
      'Implement `int at(int index)` with boundary validation, returning -1 for out-of-bounds.',
      'Implement `size_t size()` and `size_t capacity()`.',
    ],
    starterCode: `// C++ Skill Assessment - RAII Vector & Memory Management
#include <iostream>
#include <stdexcept>

class CustomVector {
private:
    int* data;
    size_t sz;
    size_t cap;

public:
    // TODO: Implement constructor
    CustomVector() {
        cap = 2;
        sz = 0;
        data = new int[cap];
    }

    // TODO: Implement destructor to prevent memory leaks
    ~CustomVector() {
        delete[] data;
    }

    void push_back(int val) {
        if (sz >= cap) {
            cap *= 2;
            int* nextData = new int[cap];
            for (size_t i = 0; i < sz; ++i) {
                nextData[i] = data[i];
            }
            delete[] data;
            data = nextData;
        }
        data[sz++] = val;
    }

    int at(int index) const {
        if (index < 0 || (size_t)index >= sz) {
            return -1;
        }
        return data[index];
    }

    size_t size() const { return sz; }
    size_t capacity() const { return cap; }
};

int main() {
    CustomVector vec;
    vec.push_back(10);
    vec.push_back(20);
    vec.push_back(30);
    std::cout << "Size: " << vec.size() << ", Cap: " << vec.capacity() << std::endl;
    return 0;
}`,
    solutionTemplate: `class CustomVector`,
    testCases: [
      {
        id: 'tc-1',
        input: 'push_back(10), push_back(20), size()',
        expectedOutput: '2',
        isHidden: false,
        description: 'Basic insertion and size check',
      },
      {
        id: 'tc-2',
        input: 'push_back 3 elements, capacity()',
        expectedOutput: '4',
        isHidden: false,
        description: 'Capacity doubling on overflow (2 -> 4)',
      },
      {
        id: 'tc-3',
        input: 'at(5) on 3-element vector',
        expectedOutput: '-1',
        isHidden: true,
        description: 'Out-of-bounds boundary safety test',
      },
      {
        id: 'tc-4',
        input: 'Destructor deallocation check',
        expectedOutput: 'NO_LEAKS',
        isHidden: true,
        description: 'Heap memory verification (delete[] called)',
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'c-pointer-string-reverse',
    language: 'c',
    title: 'C In-Place String & Pointer Reversal',
    difficulty: 'Intermediate',
    timeLimitMinutes: 15,
    description:
      'Write an efficient in-place string reversal function in C using raw pointer arithmetic (`char* left`, `char* right`). Zero auxiliary memory allocation allowed (O(1) space).',
    instructions: [
      'Implement `void reverse_string(char* str)` in pure C.',
      'Check for NULL pointer gracefully.',
      'Use pointer arithmetic to find the end of the string without calling `strlen` if possible.',
      'Swap characters until pointers cross.',
      'Do not allocate extra heap or stack buffers.',
    ],
    starterCode: `// C Skill Assessment - Pointer Arithmetic
#include <stdio.h>

void reverse_string(char* str) {
    if (str == NULL) return;
    
    char* start = str;
    char* end = str;
    
    // Advance end pointer to null terminator
    while (*end != '\\0') {
        end++;
    }
    end--; // Step back from '\\0'
    
    // In-place swap using raw pointers
    while (start < end) {
        char temp = *start;
        *start = *end;
        *end = temp;
        start++;
        end--;
    }
}

int main() {
    char greeting[] = "SkillMatch";
    reverse_string(greeting);
    printf("Reversed: %s\\n", greeting);
    return 0;
}`,
    solutionTemplate: `void reverse_string(char* str)`,
    testCases: [
      {
        id: 'tc-1',
        input: '"SkillMatch"',
        expectedOutput: '"hctaMllikS"',
        isHidden: false,
        description: 'Standard mixed-case ASCII string',
      },
      {
        id: 'tc-2',
        input: '"" (empty string)',
        expectedOutput: '""',
        isHidden: false,
        description: 'Empty string boundary test',
      },
      {
        id: 'tc-3',
        input: 'NULL pointer passed',
        expectedOutput: 'GRACEFUL_EXIT',
        isHidden: true,
        description: 'Null pointer defensive dereference safeguard',
      },
      {
        id: 'tc-4',
        input: '"A" (single character)',
        expectedOutput: '"A"',
        isHidden: true,
        description: 'Single character identity test',
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'cpp-graph-bfs',
    language: 'cpp',
    title: 'C++ Graph Adjacency & Skill BFS Search',
    difficulty: 'Advanced',
    timeLimitMinutes: 25,
    description:
      'Implement the core graph traversal engine similar to the project GraphModule: Given an undirected graph of skills, check if skill A is reachable from skill B within K degrees of separation.',
    instructions: [
      'Use `std::unordered_map<std::string, std::vector<std::string>>` or `std::map`.',
      'Implement `bool areRelatedK(const std::string& u, const std::string& v, int maxDegrees)`.',
      'Use a Breadth-First Search (BFS) with `std::queue` to guarantee the shortest distance.',
      'Keep track of visited nodes using `std::unordered_set` to avoid infinite cyclic loops.',
    ],
    starterCode: `// C++ Skill Assessment - Graph BFS Traversal
#include <iostream>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <queue>

class SkillGraph {
private:
    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::vector<std::string>> adj;

public:
    void addEdge(const std::string& u, const std::string& v) {
        adj[u].push_back(v);
        adj[v].push_back(u);
    }

    bool areRelated(const std::string& start, const std::string& target, int maxDegrees) {
        if (start == target) return true;
        if (adj.find(start) == adj.end() || adj.find(target) == adj.end()) return false;

        std::unordered_set<std::string> visited;
        std::queue<std::pair<std::string, int>> q;

        visited.insert(start);
        q.push({start, 0});

        while (!q.empty()) {
            auto [curr, dist] = q.front();
            q.pop();

            if (curr == target) return true;
            if (dist >= maxDegrees) continue;

            for (const auto& neighbor : adj[curr]) {
                if (visited.find(neighbor) == visited.end()) {
                    visited.insert(neighbor);
                    q.push({neighbor, dist + 1});
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
};

int main() {
    SkillGraph g;
    g.addEdge("C", "C++");
    g.addEdge("C++", "Data Structures");
    std::cout << "Related in 2 steps: " << g.areRelated("C", "Data Structures", 2) << std::endl;
    return 0;
}`,
    solutionTemplate: `bool areRelated`,
    testCases: [
      {
        id: 'tc-1',
        input: 'C to C++ in 1 step',
        expectedOutput: 'true',
        isHidden: false,
        description: 'Direct edge connection',
      },
      {
        id: 'tc-2',
        input: 'C to Data Structures in 2 steps',
        expectedOutput: 'true',
        isHidden: false,
        description: '2-hop transitive connection',
      },
      {
        id: 'tc-3',
        input: 'C to Data Structures with maxDegrees = 1',
        expectedOutput: 'false',
        isHidden: true,
        description: 'Degree limit cutoff test',
      },
      {
        id: 'tc-4',
        input: 'Unconnected component query',
        expectedOutput: 'false',
        isHidden: true,
        description: 'Disconnected graph partition test',
      },
    ],
  },
];

export interface CodeEvaluationResult {
  syntaxValid: boolean;
  score: number;
  testCasesPassed: number;
  totalTestCases: number;
  timeComplexity: string;
  spaceComplexity: string;
  memorySafetyNotes: string[];
  cheatSuspicion: 'LOW' | 'MEDIUM' | 'HIGH';
  verdict: 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE' | 'ELIGIBLE_WITH_CONDITIONS' | 'NOT_ELIGIBLE_UNVERIFIED';
  personalizedFeedback: string;
  detailedAudit: {
    hasDestructorOrFree: boolean;
    hasPointerArithmetic: boolean;
    boundaryChecksPresent: boolean;
    hasBufferOverflowRisk: boolean;
  };
}

export class AssessmentEngine {
  public static evaluateCode(
    code: string,
    challenge: CodeChallenge,
    metrics: {
      timeSpentSeconds: number;
      tabSwitchCount: number;
      copyPasteCount: number;
    }
  ): CodeEvaluationResult {
    const trimmedCode = code.trim();

    // 1. Basic Syntax & Structure Check
    const hasInclude = /#include\s*<[a-z_.]+>/i.test(trimmedCode);
    const hasMainOrFunc =
      new RegExp(challenge.solutionTemplate, 'i').test(trimmedCode) ||
      trimmedCode.includes('main()');
    const openBraces = (trimmedCode.match(/{/g) || []).length;
    const closeBraces = (trimmedCode.match(/}/g) || []).length;
    const balancedBraces = openBraces > 0 && openBraces === closeBraces;

    const syntaxValid = hasInclude && hasMainOrFunc && balancedBraces;

    // 2. Memory Safety & Pointer Verification
    const memorySafetyNotes: string[] = [];
    const hasMalloc = /\bmalloc\s*\(/i.test(trimmedCode);
    const hasFree = /\bfree\s*\(/i.test(trimmedCode);
    const hasNew = /\bnew\b/i.test(trimmedCode);
    const hasDelete = /\bdelete\b/i.test(trimmedCode);
    const hasGets = /\bgets\s*\(/i.test(trimmedCode);
    const hasNullCheck = /\b(NULL|nullptr|!str|==\s*0)\b/i.test(trimmedCode);

    let hasDestructorOrFree = true;
    if (hasMalloc && !hasFree) {
      hasDestructorOrFree = false;
      memorySafetyNotes.push('Memory leak detected: `malloc` is used without corresponding `free`.');
    }
    if (hasNew && !hasDelete) {
      hasDestructorOrFree = false;
      memorySafetyNotes.push('Memory leak detected: `new` is allocated without `delete[]` in destructor.');
    }
    if (hasGets) {
      memorySafetyNotes.push('Security flaw: Insecure `gets()` call detected. Risk of buffer overflow.');
    }
    if (!hasNullCheck && challenge.language === 'c') {
      memorySafetyNotes.push('Defensive coding warning: Missing explicit NULL pointer validation.');
    }

    const hasPointerArithmetic = /\*|\->|&/i.test(trimmedCode);
    const boundaryChecksPresent = />=|<=|<|>|==/i.test(trimmedCode);
    const hasBufferOverflowRisk = hasGets || (!boundaryChecksPresent && trimmedCode.includes('['));

    // 3. Complexity Heuristics
    let timeComplexity = 'O(N)';
    let spaceComplexity = 'O(1)';

    const forCount = (trimmedCode.match(/\bfor\s*\(/g) || []).length;
    const whileCount = (trimmedCode.match(/\bwhile\s*\(/g) || []).length;
    const loopCount = forCount + whileCount;

    if (challenge.id === 'cpp-smart-vector') {
      timeComplexity = 'Amortized O(1) push_back, O(1) random access';
      spaceComplexity = 'O(N) dynamic storage';
    } else if (challenge.id === 'c-pointer-string-reverse') {
      timeComplexity = 'O(N) two-pointer pass';
      spaceComplexity = 'O(1) auxiliary in-place swap';
    } else if (challenge.id === 'cpp-graph-bfs') {
      timeComplexity = 'O(V + E) BFS traversal';
      spaceComplexity = 'O(V) visited set & queue';
    } else {
      if (loopCount >= 2 && trimmedCode.includes('for') && trimmedCode.includes('while')) {
        timeComplexity = 'O(N^2)';
      }
    }

    // 4. Anti-cheat Suspicion
    let cheatSuspicion: 'LOW' | 'MEDIUM' | 'HIGH' = 'LOW';
    if (metrics.tabSwitchCount >= 4 || metrics.copyPasteCount >= 3) {
      cheatSuspicion = 'HIGH';
    } else if (metrics.tabSwitchCount >= 2 || metrics.copyPasteCount >= 1) {
      cheatSuspicion = 'MEDIUM';
    }

    if (metrics.timeSpentSeconds < 2 && metrics.copyPasteCount >= 1) {
      cheatSuspicion = 'HIGH';
    }

    // 5. Test cases scoring simulation
    const totalTestCases = challenge.testCases.length;
    let testCasesPassed = 0;

    if (syntaxValid && !hasBufferOverflowRisk) {
      testCasesPassed = totalTestCases;
      // Subtract if missing destructor in custom vector
      if (challenge.id === 'cpp-smart-vector' && !hasDelete) {
        testCasesPassed -= 1; // Leaks test fails
      }
      // Subtract if missing null check in C
      if (challenge.id === 'c-pointer-string-reverse' && !hasNullCheck) {
        testCasesPassed -= 1; // Null pointer check fails
      }
    } else if (syntaxValid) {
      testCasesPassed = Math.max(1, totalTestCases - 2);
    } else {
      testCasesPassed = 0;
    }

    // Base score calculation
    let calculatedScore = Math.round((testCasesPassed / totalTestCases) * 70);
    if (syntaxValid) calculatedScore += 15;
    if (hasDestructorOrFree && !hasBufferOverflowRisk) calculatedScore += 15;

    // Deduct penalty for suspicious anti-cheat flags
    if (cheatSuspicion === 'HIGH') {
      calculatedScore = Math.max(0, calculatedScore - 25);
    } else if (cheatSuspicion === 'MEDIUM') {
      calculatedScore = Math.max(0, calculatedScore - 10);
    }

    // Verdict determination
    let verdict: 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE' | 'ELIGIBLE_WITH_CONDITIONS' | 'NOT_ELIGIBLE_UNVERIFIED';
    if (calculatedScore >= 75 && cheatSuspicion === 'LOW') {
      verdict = 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE';
    } else if (calculatedScore >= 50 && cheatSuspicion !== 'HIGH') {
      verdict = 'ELIGIBLE_WITH_CONDITIONS';
    } else {
      verdict = 'NOT_ELIGIBLE_UNVERIFIED';
    }

    // 6. Generate Personalized Feedback for Candidate and Recruiting Team
    let feedback = '';
    if (verdict === 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE') {
      feedback = `Candidate demonstrates strong proficiency in ${challenge.language.toUpperCase()} systems programming. Test execution passed ${testCasesPassed}/${totalTestCases} criteria with clean memory deallocation and optimal ${timeComplexity} efficiency. High integrity rating with zero suspicious cheat indicators. Highly recommended for project teams requiring rigorous ${challenge.language.toUpperCase()} core skills.`;
    } else if (verdict === 'ELIGIBLE_WITH_CONDITIONS') {
      feedback = `Candidate demonstrated working knowledge of ${challenge.language.toUpperCase()} algorithms with a score of ${calculatedScore}%. Note: Minor edge cases or memory hygiene optimizations were flagged (${memorySafetyNotes.join('; ') || 'defensive pointer checking'}). Recommended for teams with technical mentorship or after a brief follow-up technical interview.`;
    } else {
      feedback = `Candidate did not meet the verification criteria (Score: ${calculatedScore}%). Issues identified: ${memorySafetyNotes.length > 0 ? memorySafetyNotes.join(', ') : 'Syntax/test-case failures or excessive anti-cheat flags (' + metrics.tabSwitchCount + ' tab switches)'}. Candidate should strengthen pointer mechanics and retake the assessment.`;
    }

    return {
      syntaxValid,
      score: calculatedScore,
      testCasesPassed,
      totalTestCases,
      timeComplexity,
      spaceComplexity,
      memorySafetyNotes,
      cheatSuspicion,
      verdict,
      personalizedFeedback: feedback,
      detailedAudit: {
        hasDestructorOrFree,
        hasPointerArithmetic,
        boundaryChecksPresent,
        hasBufferOverflowRisk,
      },
    };
  }
}

FILE: src/lib/graphEngine.test.ts (61 lines)

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { SkillGraphEngine, MatchingEngine } from './graphEngine';

test('SkillGraphEngine adds relationships and checks relatedness', () => {
  const graph = new SkillGraphEngine();
  
  // Directly seeded
  assert.equal(graph.areRelated('C', 'C++'), true);
  assert.equal(graph.areRelated('C++', 'C'), true);
  assert.equal(graph.areRelated('Python', 'Django'), true);
  
  // Custom addition
  graph.addSkillRelationship('Rust', 'Systems Programming');
  assert.equal(graph.areRelated('Rust', 'Systems Programming'), true);
  assert.equal(graph.areRelated('Rust', 'HTML'), false);
});

test('MatchingEngine compares exact and related skills correctly', () => {
  const graph = new SkillGraphEngine();
  
  // Exact = 100
  assert.equal(MatchingEngine.compareSkill('C++', 'C++', graph), 100);
  assert.equal(MatchingEngine.compareSkill('c++', 'C++', graph), 100);
  
  // Related = 75 (C related to C++)
  assert.equal(MatchingEngine.compareSkill('C', 'C++', graph), 75);
  
  // Unrelated = 0
  assert.equal(MatchingEngine.compareSkill('Ruby', 'C++', graph), 0);
});

test('MatchingEngine handles OR requirements like C|Python and calculates averages', () => {
  const graph = new SkillGraphEngine();
  const studentSkills = ['C', 'Tailwind CSS'];
  
  // Requirement: "C++|Python" -> student knows C which is related to C++ (75 score)
  // Requirement: "CSS" -> student knows Tailwind CSS which is related to CSS (75 score)
  const result = MatchingEngine.calculateDetailedScore(
    studentSkills,
    ['C++|Python', 'CSS'],
    graph
  );
  
  assert.equal(result.score, 75);
  assert.equal(result.breakdown.length, 2);
  assert.equal(result.breakdown[0].matchType, 'related');
});

test('MatchingEngine scores 100 for exact matches across all requirements', () => {
  const graph = new SkillGraphEngine();
  const studentSkills = ['C++', 'React', 'Data Structures'];
  
  const score = MatchingEngine.calculateScore(
    studentSkills,
    ['C++', 'React'],
    graph
  );
  
  assert.equal(score, 100);
});

/**
 * SkillGraph and Matching Engine
 * Direct TypeScript port and extension of C++ modules:
 * GraphModule.h / GraphModule.cpp
 * MatchingModule.h / MatchingModule.cpp
 * TeamModule.h / TeamModule.cpp
 */

export class SkillGraphEngine {
  private adjacencyList: Map<string, Set<string>> = new Map();

  constructor() {
    this.seedDefaultRelationships();
  }

  // Exact counterpart to GraphModule::addSkillRelationship
  public addSkillRelationship(skill1: string, skill2: string): void {
    const s1 = skill1.trim().toLowerCase();
    const s2 = skill2.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!this.adjacencyList.has(s1)) {
      this.adjacencyList.set(s1, new Set());
    }
    if (!this.adjacencyList.has(s2)) {
      this.adjacencyList.set(s2, new Set());
    }

    this.adjacencyList.get(s1)!.add(s2);
    this.adjacencyList.get(s2)!.add(s1);
  }

  // Exact counterpart to GraphModule::areRelated
  public areRelated(skill1: string, skill2: string): boolean {
    const s1 = skill1.trim().toLowerCase();
    const s2 = skill2.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (s1 === s2) return true;

    const neighbors = this.adjacencyList.get(s1);
    if (!neighbors) return false;

    return neighbors.has(s2);
  }

  // Exact counterpart to GraphModule::getRelatedSkills
  public getRelatedSkills(skill: string): string[] {
    const s = skill.trim().toLowerCase();
    const set = this.adjacencyList.get(s);
    return set ? Array.from(set) : [];
  }

  public getAllRelationships(): Array<{ skill: string; related: string[] }> {
    const result: Array<{ skill: string; related: string[] }> = [];
    for (const [skill, relatedSet] of this.adjacencyList.entries()) {
      result.push({
        skill,
        related: Array.from(relatedSet),
      });
    }
    return result;
  }

  // Default skill relationships from main.cpp plus real university project domains
  private seedDefaultRelationships(): void {
    // Relationships from main.cpp:
    this.addSkillRelationship('C', 'C++');
    this.addSkillRelationship('Python', 'Django');
    this.addSkillRelationship('Python', 'Flask');
    this.addSkillRelationship('JavaScript', 'React');
    this.addSkillRelationship('JavaScript', 'Node.js');
    this.addSkillRelationship('HTML', 'HTML5');
    this.addSkillRelationship('CSS', 'Tailwind CSS');
    this.addSkillRelationship('CSS', 'Bootstrap');
    this.addSkillRelationship('SQL', 'MySQL');
    this.addSkillRelationship('SQL', 'PostgreSQL');

    // Extended domains for 100+ university teams:
    this.addSkillRelationship('C++', 'Data Structures');
    this.addSkillRelationship('C++', 'Algorithms');
    this.addSkillRelationship('C', 'Embedded Systems');
    this.addSkillRelationship('C', 'Operating Systems');
    this.addSkillRelationship('TypeScript', 'JavaScript');
    this.addSkillRelationship('React', 'Next.js');
    this.addSkillRelationship('Python', 'PyTorch');
    this.addSkillRelationship('Python', 'TensorFlow');
    this.addSkillRelationship('PyTorch', 'Deep Learning');
    this.addSkillRelationship('TensorFlow', 'Machine Learning');
    this.addSkillRelationship('Machine Learning', 'Data Science');
    this.addSkillRelationship('Docker', 'Kubernetes');
    this.addSkillRelationship('Docker', 'DevOps');
    this.addSkillRelationship('AWS', 'Cloud Computing');
    this.addSkillRelationship('GCP', 'Cloud Computing');
    this.addSkillRelationship('Cryptography', 'Cybersecurity');
    this.addSkillRelationship('Penetration Testing', 'Cybersecurity');
    this.addSkillRelationship('Solidity', 'Smart Contracts');
    this.addSkillRelationship('Blockchain', 'Solidity');
    this.addSkillRelationship('ROS', 'Robotics');
    this.addSkillRelationship('Arduino', 'IoT');
    this.addSkillRelationship('Computer Vision', 'OpenCV');
    this.addSkillRelationship('Natural Language Processing', 'LLMs');
  }
}

// Global singleton instance for consistency
export const defaultSkillGraph = new SkillGraphEngine();

export interface StudentMatchScore {
  studentId: string;
  studentName: string;
  studentSkills: string[];
  score: number; // 0 to 100
  breakdown: Array<{
    required: string;
    matchedSkill: string | null;
    score: number;
    matchType: 'exact' | 'related' | 'none';
  }>;
}

/**
 * Exact counterpart to MatchingModule in C++:
 * compareSkill: exact -> 100, related -> 75, none -> 0
 * calculateScore: handles "SkillA|SkillB" OR expressions, averages requirement scores
 */
export class MatchingEngine {
  public static compareSkill(
    studentSkill: string,
    requiredSkill: string,
    graph: SkillGraphEngine = defaultSkillGraph
  ): number {
    const s1 = studentSkill.trim().toLowerCase();
    const s2 = requiredSkill.trim().toLowerCase();

    // Exact match
    if (s1 === s2) {
      return 100;
    }

    // Related match
    if (graph.areRelated(s1, s2)) {
      return 75;
    }

    return 0;
  }

  public static calculateDetailedScore(
    studentSkills: string[],
    requiredSkills: string[],
    graph: SkillGraphEngine = defaultSkillGraph
  ): {
    score: number;
    breakdown: Array<{
      required: string;
      matchedSkill: string | null;
      score: number;
      matchType: 'exact' | 'related' | 'none';
    }>;
  } {
    if (!requiredSkills || requiredSkills.length === 0) {
      return { score: 100, breakdown: [] };
    }

    let totalScore = 0;
    const breakdown: Array<{
      required: string;
      matchedSkill: string | null;
      score: number;
      matchType: 'exact' | 'related' | 'none';
    }> = [];

    for (const requirement of requiredSkills) {
      // Split OR requirements (e.g., "C|Python")
      const options = requirement.split('|').map((opt) => opt.trim());
      let bestScore = 0;
      let bestMatchedSkill: string | null = null;
      let bestMatchType: 'exact' | 'related' | 'none' = 'none';

      for (const option of options) {
        for (const sSkill of studentSkills) {
          const s = this.compareSkill(sSkill, option, graph);
          if (s > bestScore) {
            bestScore = s;
            bestMatchedSkill = sSkill;
            bestMatchType = s === 100 ? 'exact' : 'related';
          }
        }
      }

      totalScore += bestScore;
      breakdown.push({
        required: requirement,
        matchedSkill: bestMatchedSkill,
        score: bestScore,
        matchType: bestMatchType,
      });
    }

    const finalScore = Math.round(totalScore / requiredSkills.length);
    return {
      score: finalScore,
      breakdown,
    };
  }

  public static calculateScore(
    studentSkills: string[],
    requiredSkills: string[],
    graph: SkillGraphEngine = defaultSkillGraph
  ): number {
    return this.calculateDetailedScore(studentSkills, requiredSkills, graph).score;
  }
}

  for (let i = 0; i < 104; i++) {
    const teamNumber = i + 2;
    const deptIdx = i % departments.length;
    const dept = departments[deptIdx];
    const skillSet = dept.skillSets[i % dept.skillSets.length];
    const description = dept.descriptions[i % dept.descriptions.length];
    const gradient = gradients[i % gradients.length];
    const baseTitle = teamTitles[i % teamTitles.length];
    const title = `${baseTitle} (#${teamNumber})`;

    // Assign a unique leader for every team (use namedLeaders for the first 9, then generate unique leaders)
    const leader =
      i < namedLeaders.length
        ? namedLeaders[i]
        : (() => {
            const lf = LEADER_FIRST_NAMES[i % LEADER_FIRST_NAMES.length];
            const ll = LEADER_LAST_NAMES[Math.floor(i / LEADER_FIRST_NAMES.length) % LEADER_LAST_NAMES.length];
            const fullName = `${lf} ${ll}`;
            return {
              id: `lead-team-${teamNumber}`,
              name: fullName,
              email: `${lf.toLowerCase()}.${ll.toLowerCase()}.lead${teamNumber}@gehu.ac.in`,
            };
          })();

    // Randomize member counts: some open (size 3-5 with 1-2 vacancy), some filled/closed
    const capacity = 3 + (i % 3); // 3, 4, or 5
    const isClosed = i % 7 === 0; // ~15% closed vacancy
    const filledCount = isClosed ? capacity : Math.max(1, capacity - (1 + (i % 2)));

    const cleanSkillsList = skillSet.map((s) => s.split('|')[0]);
    const primarySkill = cleanSkillsList[0] || 'C++';
    const secondarySkill = cleanSkillsList[1] || 'Algorithms';

    const members: TeamMember[] = [
      {
        id: leader.id,
        name: leader.name,
        email: leader.email,
        role: 'Leader',
        skills: [primarySkill, secondarySkill],
        avatar: MEMBER_AVATARS[i % MEMBER_AVATARS.length],
      },
    ];

    for (let m = 1; m < filledCount; m++) {
      const fName = MEMBER_FIRST_NAMES[(i * 3 + m * 5) % MEMBER_FIRST_NAMES.length];
      const lName = MEMBER_LAST_NAMES[(i * 7 + m * 3) % MEMBER_LAST_NAMES.length];
      const formattedFirst = fName.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + fName.slice(1);
      const fullName = `${formattedFirst} ${lName}`;
      const cleanEmailPrefix = `${formattedFirst.toLowerCase()}.${lName.toLowerCase()}.${teamNumber}${m}`;
      const memberSkills = [
        skillSet[m % skillSet.length].split('|')[0],
        skillSet[(m + 1) % skillSet.length].split('|')[0],
      ];

      members.push({
        id: `member-${i}-${m}`,
        name: fullName,
        email: `${cleanEmailPrefix}@gehu.ac.in`,
        role: 'Member',
        skills: Array.from(new Set(memberSkills)),
        avatar: MEMBER_AVATARS[(i + m) % MEMBER_AVATARS.length],
      });
    }

    const openSlotsCount = Math.max(0, capacity - members.length);
    const deadlineDate = `2026-11-${15 + (i % 15)}`;
    const courseCode = `B.Tech ${dept.category.split(' ')[0].toUpperCase()}-2026-T${300 + teamNumber}`;

    const roleResponsibilities = [
      ...members.map((m, idx) => ({
        roleTitle:
          idx === 0
            ? `Team Leader & System Architect (${primarySkill})`
            : idx === 1
            ? `Core ${m.skills[0] || primarySkill} Implementation Engineer`
            : `Testing, Integration & ${m.skills[m.skills.length - 1] || secondarySkill} Specialist`,
        assignedTo: m.name,
        memberId: m.id,
        status: 'FILLED' as const,
        responsibilities:
          idx === 0
            ? `Directs technical architecture for ${baseTitle}, coordinates sprint deliverables, and leads ${primarySkill} core module design.`
            : `Develops and benchmarks ${m.skills.join(' & ')} pipelines, unit test suites, and integration interfaces for ${baseTitle}.`,
        skills: m.skills,
      })),
      ...Array.from({ length: openSlotsCount }).map((_, slotIdx) => ({
        roleTitle: `Open Specialist Slot #${slotIdx + 1}: ${skillSet[(members.length + slotIdx) % skillSet.length]}`,
        assignedTo: 'Open Vacancy — Accepting Student Applications',
        status: 'OPEN_VACANCY' as const,
        responsibilities: `Contribute to ${baseTitle} module development using ${skillSet.join(', ')}, participate in code reviews, and co-author phase deliverables.`,
        skills: skillSet,
      })),
    ];

    teams.push({
      id: `team-${teamNumber}`,
      title,
      department: dept.name,
      category: dept.category,
      description,
      deepDescription: [
        `${title} is a specialized university engineering and research initiative hosted under the Department of ${dept.name} (${dept.category}) at Graphic Era Deemed to be University. Led by ${leader.name}, the team is focused on ${description.charAt(0).toLowerCase() + description.slice(1)}`,
        `The system architecture combines high-efficiency ${skillSet.join(', ')} modules with rigorous benchmarking, automated testing, and modular API boundaries. Each subsystem is engineered to handle real-world concurrency, deterministic memory footprints, and fault-tolerant execution across campus laboratory clusters.`,
        `Students joining ${title} collaborate through structured bi-weekly engineering sprints, code reviews, and faculty milestone evaluations under course code ${courseCode}, culminating in a production-grade prototype and technical research demonstration.`,
      ],
      problemStatement: `Conventional implementations in ${dept.category} suffer from high latency, poor scalability, and fragmented tooling when dealing with ${description.toLowerCase().replace(/\.$/, '')}. ${baseTitle} addresses this gap by engineering a tightly integrated pipeline using ${skillSet.join(' and ')}.`,
      objectives: [
        `Architect and implement the core ${baseTitle} engine utilizing ${primarySkill} and ${secondarySkill} with deterministic performance guarantees.`,
        `Achieve modular interoperability across ${skillSet.join(', ')} components with comprehensive automated test coverage.`,
        `Benchmark throughput, memory safety, and latency against baseline academic datasets in the ${dept.name} lab.`,
        `Deliver complete technical documentation, source repository, and live faculty demonstration before ${deadlineDate}.`,
      ],
      architectureModules: [
        {
          name: `Module 1: Core ${primarySkill} Processing Kernel`,
          role: `Led by ${leader.name} (Team Lead)`,
          tech: `${primarySkill}, ${secondarySkill}, Systems Core`,
          description: `Implements the foundational data structures, state machine, and core execution loop powering ${baseTitle}.`,
        },
        {
          name: `Module 2: ${dept.category} Pipeline & Middleware`,
          role: members[1] ? `Engineered by ${members[1].name}` : 'Assigned to Core Team Roster',
          tech: skillSet.join(', '),
          description: `Handles data ingestion, transformation, and high-speed communication between internal subsystems.`,
        },
        {
          name: 'Module 3: Telemetry, Diagnostics & Interface Layer',
          role: members[2] ? `Engineered by ${members[2].name}` : 'Collaborative Team Module',
          tech: `${cleanSkillsList[cleanSkillsList.length - 1] || primarySkill}, Real-time Metrics`,
          description: `Provides interactive monitoring, diagnostic logs, and visual verification of ${baseTitle} outputs.`,
        },
        {
          name: 'Module 4: Stress Benchmarking & Verification Suite',
          role: openSlotsCount > 0 ? 'Open Vacancy / Shared Sprint Module' : 'Team QA & Verification',
          tech: `Automated Testing, Profiling, ${primarySkill}`,
          description: `Validates edge-case resilience, memory safety, and end-to-end accuracy under synthetic and real workloads.`,
        },
      ],
      milestones: [
        {
          phase: 'Phase I',
          title: 'Problem Formulation & System Architecture Design',
          timeline: 'Oct 18 – Oct 27, 2026',
          status: 'COMPLETED',
          deliverable: `Approved architecture specification and module interface contracts for ${baseTitle}.`,
        },
        {
          phase: 'Phase II',
          title: `Core ${primarySkill} Module Implementation`,
          timeline: 'Oct 28 – Nov 06, 2026',
          status: 'COMPLETED',
          deliverable: `Working prototype of the core ${primarySkill} & ${secondarySkill} processing pipeline.`,
        },
        {
          phase: 'Phase III',
          title: 'Subsystem Integration & Performance Profiling',
          timeline: 'Nov 07 – Nov 14, 2026',
          status: 'IN_PROGRESS',
          deliverable: `End-to-end integration of ${skillSet.join(', ')} modules and latency benchmarking.`,
        },
        {
          phase: 'Phase IV',
          title: 'Final Defense, Documentation & Deployment',
          timeline: `Nov 15 – ${deadlineDate}`,
          status: 'UPCOMING',
          deliverable: 'Final project report, source code release, and live university panel evaluation.',
        },
      ],
      techStackBreakdown: [
        { category: 'Primary Languages & Core', items: skillSet.map((s) => s.replace('|', ' / ')) },
        { category: 'Domain & Category Stack', items: [dept.category, dept.name, 'Linux / POSIX Env'] },
        { category: 'Engineering Workflow', items: ['Git Version Control', 'Automated Unit Tests', 'Peer Code Review', 'PBL Milestone Tracking'] },
      ],
      roleResponsibilities,
      expectedOutcomes: [
        `Production-ready ${baseTitle} prototype verified on university benchmark workloads.`,
        `Complete modular codebase in ${skillSet.join(', ')} with reproducible build scripts.`,
        `Technical research paper and live demonstration for ${dept.name} faculty evaluation.`,
      ],
      repositoryUrl: `https://github.com/gehu-projects-2026/team-${teamNumber}-${baseTitle.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')}`,
      documentationUrl: `https://gehu.ac.in/projects/2026/team-${teamNumber}/specification`,
      academicCourseCode: courseCode,
      requiredSkills: skillSet,
      teamSize: capacity,
      leaderId: leader.id,
      leaderName: leader.name,
      leaderEmail: leader.email,
      members,
      vacancyOpen: !isClosed,
      deadline: deadlineDate,
      tags: [dept.category.split(' ')[0], primarySkill, isClosed ? 'Closed' : 'Active Vacancy'],
      bannerGradient: gradient,
      createdAt: '2026-10-18',
    });
  }

  return teams;
}

export const INITIAL_TEAMS: Team[] = generateInitialTeams();

/**
 * Builds the complete university directory of StudentProfiles so EVERY person
 * (all flagship students, all 10 team leaders, and every member across all 105 teams)
 * has a complete profile, login credentials (email, studentIdNum, password),
 * skills, interests, and verification status visible to Admin.
 */
export function buildAllInitialStudents(teams: Team[]): StudentProfile[] {
  const byId = new Map<string, StudentProfile>();
  for (const stu of BASE_STUDENTS) {
    byId.set(stu.id, stu);
  }

  let seq = 100;
  for (const team of teams) {
    for (const member of team.members) {
      if (byId.has(member.id)) continue;
      seq += 1;
      const isLeader = member.role === 'Leader' || member.id.startsWith('lead-');
      const studentIdNum = isLeader ? `25101100${seq}` : `25101900${seq}`;
      const firstToken = member.name.split(' ')[0].replace(/[^a-zA-Z]/g, '') || 'User';
      const deterministicPassword = `${firstToken}@${seq}#gehu`;
      const cleanSkills =
        member.skills && member.skills.length > 0
          ? member.skills.map((s) => s.split('|')[0])
          : ['C++', 'Algorithms'];
      const hasCppOrC = cleanSkills.some(
        (s) => s.toLowerCase() === 'c++' || s.toLowerCase() === 'c'
      );
      const score = isLeader ? 88 + (seq % 11) : 76 + (seq % 20);

      byId.set(member.id, {
        id: member.id,
        studentIdNum,
        name: member.name,
        email: member.email,
        password: deterministicPassword,
        accountType: isLeader ? 'TEAM_LEADER' : 'TEAM_MEMBER',
        createdAt: `2026-10-${10 + (seq % 18)} ${10 + (seq % 8)}:${10 + (seq % 45)}`,
        phone: `+91 98${String(10000000 + seq * 137).slice(0, 8)}`,
        department: team.department,
        year: isLeader
          ? '3rd Year (Project Lead)'
          : ['2nd Year', '3rd Year', '4th Year'][seq % 3],
        avatar: member.avatar || MEMBER_AVATARS[seq % MEMBER_AVATARS.length],
        bio: isLeader
          ? `Team Leader for ${team.title} in ${team.department}. Coordinating architecture, deliverables, and member sprint tasks.`
          : `Active project member in ${team.title} (${team.department}). Focused on ${cleanSkills.join(' & ')} implementation.`,
        skills: Array.from(new Set([...cleanSkills, 'C++'])),
        interests: [team.category, ...team.tags.slice(0, 2)],
        availability: `${12 + (seq % 10)} hrs/week`,
        githubUrl: `https://github.com/${firstToken.toLowerCase()}-${seq}`,
        certificates: [],
        assessmentStatus: {
          cppVerified: isLeader || hasCppOrC || seq % 2 === 0,
          cVerified: hasCppOrC,
          score,
          verdict: score >= 75 ? 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE' : 'ELIGIBLE_WITH_CONDITIONS',
          cheatFlags: 0,
          completedAt: `2026-10-${12 + (seq % 15)} 15:20`,
          feedbackSummary: `Verified proficiency for ${team.title} (${score}% score).`,
        },
        blockedUsers: [],
        isBanned: false,
      });
    }
  }

  return Array.from(byId.values());
}

export const INITIAL_STUDENTS: StudentProfile[] = buildAllInitialStudents(INITIAL_TEAMS);
