    submittedCode: `#include <iostream>
#include <stdexcept>

class CustomVector {
private:
    int* data;
    size_t sz;
    size_t cap;

public:
    CustomVector() {
        cap = 2;
        sz = 0;
        data = new int[cap];
    }

    ~CustomVector() {
        delete[] data;
    }

    void push_back(int val) {
        if (sz >= cap) {
            cap *= 2;
            int* nextData = new int[cap];
            for (size_t i = 0; i < sz; ++i) {
                nextData[i] = data[i];
            }
            delete[] data;
            data = nextData;
        }
        data[sz++] = val;
    }

    int at(int index) const {
        if (index < 0 || (size_t)index >= sz) {
            return -1;
        }
        return data[index];
    }

    size_t size() const { return sz; }
    size_t capacity() const { return cap; }
};`,
    syntaxValid: true,
    testCasesPassed: 4,
    totalTestCases: 4,
    score: 95,
    timeSpentSeconds: 340,
    tabSwitchCount: 0,
    copyPasteCount: 0,
    cheatSuspicion: 'LOW',
    timeComplexity: 'Amortized O(1) push_back, O(1) random access',
    spaceComplexity: 'O(N) dynamic storage',
    memorySafetyNotes: ['RAII destructor correctly deallocates buffer with delete[]'],

Test execution passed 4/4 criteria with clean memory deallocation and optimal Amortized O(1) push_back, O(1) random access efficiency. High integrity rating with zero suspicious cheat indicators. Highly recommended for project teams requiring rigorous CPP core skills.',

  },

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { LearningPathEngine } from './learningPathEngine';
import { StudentProfile, AssessmentSubmission } from './types';

test('LearningPathEngine generates high priority memory safety topic when leak is detected in submission', () => {
  const profile: StudentProfile = {
    id: 'test-student',
    studentIdNum: '12345',
    name: 'Test Student',
    email: 'test@gehu.ac.in',
    department: 'CSE',
    year: '3rd Year',
    avatar: '',
    bio: '',
    skills: ['C++'],
    interests: [],
    availability: '10 hrs',
    certificates: [],
    assessmentStatus: {
      cppVerified: false,
      cVerified: false,
      score: 60,
      verdict: 'ELIGIBLE_WITH_CONDITIONS',
      cheatFlags: 0,
    },
  };

  const submissions: AssessmentSubmission[] = [
    {
      id: 'sub-test',
      candidateId: 'test-student',
      candidateName: 'Test Student',
      candidateEmail: 'test@gehu.ac.in',
      language: 'cpp',
      challengeId: 'cpp-smart-vector',
      challengeTitle: 'C++ Custom Dynamic Vector',
      submittedCode: '...',
      syntaxValid: true,
      testCasesPassed: 2,
      totalTestCases: 4,
      score: 60,
      timeSpentSeconds: 120,
      tabSwitchCount: 0,
      copyPasteCount: 0,
      cheatSuspicion: 'LOW',
      timeComplexity: 'O(N)',
      spaceComplexity: 'O(N)',
      memorySafetyNotes: ['Memory leak detected: new allocated without delete[] in destructor.'],
      verdict: 'ELIGIBLE_WITH_CONDITIONS',
      personalizedFeedback: 'Need to implement proper destructor deallocation.',
      submittedAt: '2026-10-31',
    },
  ];

  const result = LearningPathEngine.generateLearningPath(profile, submissions);

  assert.ok(result.topics.length >= 3);
  const memoryTopic = result.topics.find((t) => t.category === 'Memory Safety');
  assert.ok(memoryTopic);
  assert.equal(memoryTopic.priority, 'HIGH');
  assert.ok(memoryTopic.diagnosedIssue.includes('assessment'));
  assert.ok(memoryTopic.tutorials.some((tut) => tut.url.includes('cppreference.com')));
  assert.ok(memoryTopic.codeComparison.fixedSnippet.includes('delete[]'));
});

test('LearningPathEngine.calculateRoadmapProgress computes 5-stage visualizer from beginner to collaboration ready', () => {
  const beginnerProfile: StudentProfile = {
    id: 'student-beginner',
    studentIdNum: '10001',
    name: 'Beginner Student',
    email: 'beginner@gehu.ac.in',
    department: 'CSE',
    year: '1st Year',
    avatar: '',
    bio: '',
    skills: ['Python'],
    interests: [],
    availability: '8 hrs',
    certificates: [],
    assessmentStatus: {
      cppVerified: false,
      cVerified: false,
      score: 0,
      verdict: 'NOT_ATTEMPTED',
      cheatFlags: 0,
    },
  };

  const beginnerRoadmap = LearningPathEngine.calculateRoadmapProgress(beginnerProfile, [], [], []);
  assert.equal(beginnerRoadmap.stages.length, 5);
  assert.equal(beginnerRoadmap.overallReadinessPercent, 0);
  assert.equal(beginnerRoadmap.currentStageId, 'stage-1-foundations');
  assert.equal(beginnerRoadmap.stages[0].status, 'IN_PROGRESS');

  // Toggle beginner C++ topics as mastered and check advancement
  const midRoadmap = LearningPathEngine.calculateRoadmapProgress(
    { ...beginnerProfile, skills: ['C++'] },
    [],
    ['topic-pointers-nullsafety', 'topic-buffer-safety'],
    []
  );
  assert.equal(midRoadmap.stages[0].status, 'MASTERED');
  assert.equal(midRoadmap.stages[0].progressPercent, 100);
  assert.equal(midRoadmap.currentStageId, 'stage-2-memory-raii');

  // Verify advanced collaboration readiness when all stages are completed
  const advancedProfile: StudentProfile = {
    ...beginnerProfile,
    id: 'student-advanced',
    skills: ['C++', 'Data Structures', 'STL'],
    resumeFileName: 'Resume.pdf',
    githubUrl: 'https://github.com/student/cpp-graph',
    assessmentStatus: {
      cppVerified: true,
      cVerified: true,
      score: 95,
      verdict: 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE',
      cheatFlags: 0,
    },
  };

  const advancedRoadmap = LearningPathEngine.calculateRoadmapProgress(
    advancedProfile,
    [],
    ['topic-pointers-nullsafety', 'topic-buffer-safety', 'topic-raii-memory', 'topic-graph-bfs'],
    [
      {
        id: 'app-1',
        teamId: 'team-1',
        teamTitle: 'Core Systems',
        studentId: 'student-advanced',
        studentName: 'Advanced Student',
        studentEmail: 'advanced@gehu.ac.in',
        studentSkills: ['C++'],
        status: 'APPROVED',
        appliedAt: '2026-10-30',
        message: 'Ready to collaborate',
        matchScore: 96,
      },
    ]
  );

  assert.equal(advancedRoadmap.overallReadinessPercent, 100);
  assert.equal(advancedRoadmap.completedStagesCount, 5);
  assert.ok(advancedRoadmap.readinessTierTitle.includes('Advanced Project Collaboration Ready'));
});



================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/learningPathEngine.ts (644 lines)
================================================================================
/**
 * Learning Path Engine
 * Analyzes assessment feedback and candidate submissions to generate
 * personalized C/C++ learning paths, tutorials, and documentation links.
 */

import { AssessmentSubmission, StudentProfile, TeamApplication } from './types';

export interface LearningTopic {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  category: 'Memory Safety' | 'Pointer Mechanics' | 'Algorithms & Complexity' | 'Modern C++ / STL' | 'Defensive Coding';
  priority: 'HIGH' | 'MEDIUM' | 'RECOMMENDED';
  diagnosedIssue: string;
  description: string;
  tutorials: Array<{
    title: string;
    source: string;
    url: string;
    type: 'Documentation' | 'Interactive Tutorial' | 'Official Guide';
    readTime: string;
  }>;
  codeComparison: {
    vulnerableSnippet: string;
    vulnerableLabel: string;
    fixedSnippet: string;
    fixedLabel: string;
    explanation: string;
  };
}

export interface RoadmapCheckpoint {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  detail: string;
  completed: boolean;
  linkedTopicId?: string;
  actionType?: 'TOGGLE_TOPIC' | 'TAKE_ASSESSMENT' | 'BROWSE_TEAMS';
}

export interface RoadmapStage {
  id: string;
  stepNumber: number;
  title: string;
  shortTitle: string;
  tierLabel: string;
  subtitle: string;
  cppModuleRef: string;
  status: 'MASTERED' | 'IN_PROGRESS' | 'UNLOCKED' | 'LOCKED';
  progressPercent: number;
  checkpoints: RoadmapCheckpoint[];
  keyConcepts: string[];
  nextActionText: string;
}

export interface RoadmapProgress {
  overallReadinessPercent: number;
  readinessTierTitle: string;
  readinessSummary: string;
  completedStagesCount: number;
  totalStagesCount: number;
  currentStageId: string;
  stages: RoadmapStage[];
}

export class LearningPathEngine {
  public static generateLearningPath(
    profile: StudentProfile,
    submissions: AssessmentSubmission[]
  ): {
    headline: string;
    overallRecommendation: string;
    topics: LearningTopic[];
  } {
    const candidateSubmissions = submissions.filter((s) => s.candidateId === profile.id);
    const latestSubmission = candidateSubmissions.length > 0 ? candidateSubmissions[0] : null;

    const topics: LearningTopic[] = [];

    // Analyze feedback & audit notes from submission or profile
    const hasMemoryLeakIssue = latestSubmission?.memorySafetyNotes.some((n) =>
      n.toLowerCase().includes('leak') || n.toLowerCase().includes('delete') || n.toLowerCase().includes('free')
    );
    const hasBufferOverflowIssue = latestSubmission?.memorySafetyNotes.some((n) =>
      n.toLowerCase().includes('buffer') || n.toLowerCase().includes('gets') || n.toLowerCase().includes('overflow')
    );
    const hasNullCheckIssue = latestSubmission?.memorySafetyNotes.some((n) =>
      n.toLowerCase().includes('null') || n.toLowerCase().includes('defensive')
    );
    const hasComplexityIssue = latestSubmission?.timeComplexity.includes('O(N^2)');
    const score = latestSubmission ? latestSubmission.score : profile.assessmentStatus?.score || 0;

    // Topic 1: RAII & Heap Memory Management (Always high yield in C++)
    topics.push({
      id: 'topic-raii-memory',
      title: 'RAII & Dynamic Heap Deallocation (`delete[]` / `free`)',
      category: 'Memory Safety',
      priority: hasMemoryLeakIssue || score < 90 ? 'HIGH' : 'RECOMMENDED',
      diagnosedIssue: hasMemoryLeakIssue
        ? 'Identified during assessment: Dynamic allocations were not paired with corresponding deallocators in the destructor.'
        : 'Essential for Graphic Era University C++ project teams: Zero memory leak invariant under exceptions and capacity scaling.',
      description:
        'In C++, resource management must be tied to object lifetime (RAII). When allocating arrays via `new[]`, you must invoke `delete[]` in the class destructor to prevent heap leaks.',
      tutorials: [
        {
          title: 'cppreference: Resource Acquisition Is Initialization (RAII)',
          source: 'cppreference.com',
          url: 'https://en.cppreference.com/w/cpp/language/raii',
          type: 'Documentation',
          readTime: '6 min',
        },
        {
          title: 'C++ Core Guidelines: Resource Management & Destructors',
          source: 'isocpp.github.io',
          url: 'https://isocpp.github.io/CppCoreGuidelines/CppCoreGuidelines#Rr-raii',
          type: 'Official Guide',
          readTime: '10 min',
        },
        {
          title: 'Valgrind Memcheck: Detecting Heap Memory Leaks in C/C++',
          source: 'valgrind.org',
          url: 'https://valgrind.org/docs/manual/mc-manual.html',
          type: 'Interactive Tutorial',
          readTime: '8 min',
        },
      ],
      codeComparison: {
        vulnerableLabel: 'Vulnerable: Leaky Dynamic Array Destructor',
        vulnerableSnippet: `class CustomVector {
    int* data;
    size_t sz, cap;
public:
    CustomVector(size_t c = 2) : sz(0), cap(c) {
        data = new int[cap];
    }
    // BUG: Missing destructor! Memory leaks when object goes out of scope.
};`,
        fixedLabel: 'Fixed: Idiomatic RAII with Destructor',
        fixedSnippet: `class CustomVector {
    int* data;
    size_t sz, cap;
public:
    CustomVector(size_t c = 2) : sz(0), cap(c) {
        data = new int[cap];
    }
    // FIX: Deallocate heap buffer with delete[]
    ~CustomVector() {
        delete[] data;
    }
};`,
        explanation:
          'Failing to declare `~CustomVector()` leaks the heap buffer upon scope exit. Modern C++ also prefers `std::unique_ptr<int[]>` for automated memory safety.',
      },
    });

    // Topic 2: Raw Pointer Arithmetic & Defensive Null Checks
    topics.push({
      id: 'topic-pointers-nullsafety',
      title: 'Raw Pointer Mechanics & Defensive NULL/nullptr Validation',
      category: 'Pointer Mechanics',
      priority: hasNullCheckIssue || score < 85 ? 'HIGH' : 'MEDIUM',
      diagnosedIssue: hasNullCheckIssue
        ? 'Identified during assessment: Defensive null checks were missing before dereferencing raw pointers.'
        : 'Critical for C systems programming: Safeguarding against segmentation faults and dangling references.',
      description:
        'In pure C and low-level C++, raw pointers do not carry boundary or validity metadata. Every public API function must check for `NULL` / `nullptr` before dereferencing.',
      tutorials: [
        {
          title: 'Learn-C.org: Pointers and Pointer Arithmetic in C',
          source: 'learn-c.org',
          url: 'https://www.learn-c.org/en/Pointers',
          type: 'Interactive Tutorial',
          readTime: '7 min',
        },
        {
          title: 'cppreference: nullptr and Pointer Safety Best Practices',
          source: 'cppreference.com',
          url: 'https://en.cppreference.com/w/cpp/language/nullptr',
          type: 'Documentation',
          readTime: '5 min',
        },
      ],
      codeComparison: {
        vulnerableLabel: 'Vulnerable: Insecure Pointer Dereference',
        vulnerableSnippet: `void reverse_string(char* str) {
    char* end = str;
    // CRASH: Undefined behavior / segfault if str is NULL!
    while (*end != '\\0') end++;
}`,
        fixedLabel: 'Fixed: Defensive Null Check & In-place Swap',
        fixedSnippet: `void reverse_string(char* str) {
    if (str == NULL) return; // Guard clause
    
    char* start = str;
    char* end = str;
    while (*end != '\\0') end++;
    end--; // Step back from '\\0'
    
    while (start < end) {
        char tmp = *start;
        *start++ = *end--;
    }
}`,
        explanation:
          'Adding a defensive guard clause `if (str == NULL) return;` prevents catastrophic segmentation faults across operating system threads.',
      },
    });

    // Topic 3: Graph Traversal & Shortest Path (PBL SkillMatch Core)
    topics.push({
      id: 'topic-graph-bfs',
      title: 'Graph Adjacency Lists & BFS Shortest Path Traversal',
      category: 'Algorithms & Complexity',
      priority: 'RECOMMENDED',
      diagnosedIssue:
        'Directly applicable to Team Sigma\'s GraphModule: Matching skills across K degrees of separation using BFS queues.',
      description:
        'Using `std::unordered_map<std::string, std::vector<std::string>>` and `std::queue` guarantees optimal $O(V + E)$ shortest-distance queries without cyclic infinite loops.',
      tutorials: [
        {
          title: 'GeeksforGeeks: Breadth First Search (BFS) for a Graph in C++',
          source: 'geeksforgeeks.org',
          url: 'https://www.geeksforgeeks.org/breadth-first-search-or-bfs-for-a-graph/',
          type: 'Interactive Tutorial',
          readTime: '8 min',
        },
        {
          title: 'cppreference: std::queue container adapter documentation',
          source: 'cppreference.com',
          url: 'https://en.cppreference.com/w/cpp/container/queue',
          type: 'Documentation',
          readTime: '4 min',
        },
      ],
      codeComparison: {
        vulnerableLabel: 'Vulnerable: Unvisited Graph Traversal (Cycle Danger)',
        vulnerableSnippet: `void traverse(string node) {
    // BUG: Missing visited set! Cyclic graphs cause stack overflow.
    for (auto& neighbor : adj[node]) {
        traverse(neighbor);
    }
}`,
        fixedLabel: 'Fixed: BFS with std::unordered_set Visited Guard',
        fixedSnippet: `bool areRelated(string start, string target, int maxK) {
    unordered_set<string> visited;
    queue<pair<string, int>> q;
    q.push({start, 0});
    visited.insert(start);

    while (!q.empty()) {
        auto [curr, dist] = q.front(); q.pop();
        if (curr == target) return true;
        if (dist >= maxK) continue;

        for (const auto& next : adj[curr]) {
            if (visited.insert(next).second) {
                q.push({next, dist + 1});
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}`,
        explanation:
          '`visited.insert(next).second` atomically checks and inserts visited nodes in $O(1)$, completely preventing cycle re-entry.',
      },
    });

    // Topic 4: Defensive Coding & Buffer Overflow Mitigation
    topics.push({
      id: 'topic-buffer-safety',
      title: 'Buffer Bounds Safety & Elimination of Unsafe C Functions',
      category: 'Defensive Coding',
      priority: hasBufferOverflowIssue ? 'HIGH' : 'MEDIUM',
      diagnosedIssue: hasBufferOverflowIssue
        ? 'Identified during assessment: Insecure memory copy or unchecked boundary buffer operations.'
        : 'Essential for university cybersecurity and low-level systems engineering.',
      description:
        'Avoid depreciated C library functions like `gets()` or unchecked array bounds. Use bounded operations (`fgets`, `snprintf`, `std::string_view`, bounds-checking `vector::at()`).',
      tutorials: [
        {
          title: 'SEI CERT C Coding Standard: Eliminate Buffer Overflows (STR31-C)',
          source: 'wiki.sei.cmu.edu',
          url: 'https://wiki.sei.cmu.edu/confluence/display/c/STR31-C.+Guarantee+that+storage+for+strings+has+sufficient+space+for+character+data+and+the+null+terminator',
          type: 'Official Guide',
          readTime: '9 min',
        },
        {
          title: 'cppreference: std::string_view and Memory Safety',
          source: 'cppreference.com',
          url: 'https://en.cppreference.com/w/cpp/string/basic_string_view',
          type: 'Documentation',
          readTime: '6 min',
        },
      ],
      codeComparison: {
        vulnerableLabel: 'Vulnerable: Insecure gets() and Buffer Overflow',
        vulnerableSnippet: `void read_input() {
    char buffer[64];
    // DANGEROUS: gets() does not check buffer boundary! Vulnerable to exploits.
    gets(buffer);
}`,
        fixedLabel: 'Fixed: Safe fgets() with Bounded Buffer Capacity',
        fixedSnippet: `void read_input() {
    char buffer[64];
    // SAFE: Reads at most sizeof(buffer)-1 characters
    if (fgets(buffer, sizeof(buffer), stdin) != NULL) {
        // Securely bounded
    }
}`,
        explanation:
          'Using `fgets()` ensures the input cannot exceed the allocated array capacity, preventing memory corruption and exploits.',
      },
    });

    const headline =
      score >= 90
        ? 'Advanced C/C++ Systems Mastery & High-Performance Optimizations'
        : score >= 70
        ? 'Personalized C/C++ Improvement Path: Memory Hygiene & Defensive Pointers'
        : 'Foundational C/C++ Recovery Path: Pointer Arithmetic & Zero-Leak Principles';

    const overallRecommendation =
      score >= 90
        ? 'Your score qualifies you as a Verified C/C++ Developer! To push your expertise toward compiler engineering and distributed systems, focus on cache locality and modern C++20 concurrency.'
        : score >= 70
        ? 'You have demonstrated solid algorithmic foundations. Reviewing the RAII destructor deallocation and boundary guard tutorials below will ensure 100% eligibility across all university project criteria.'
        : 'Review the high-priority tutorials on raw pointer arithmetic and dynamic memory allocation (`delete[]`/`free`). Practice the before/after code comparisons and retake the assessment to earn your verified badge.';

    return {
      headline,
      overallRecommendation,
      topics,
    };
  }

  public static calculateRoadmapProgress(
    profile: StudentProfile,
    submissions: AssessmentSubmission[],
    masteredTopicIds: string[] = [],
    applications: TeamApplication[] = []
  ): RoadmapProgress {
    const candidateSubmissions = submissions.filter((s) => s.candidateId === profile.id);
    const latestSubmission = candidateSubmissions.length > 0 ? candidateSubmissions[0] : null;
    const score = latestSubmission ? latestSubmission.score : profile.assessmentStatus?.score || 0;
    const cppVerified = Boolean(profile.assessmentStatus?.cppVerified);
    const cVerified = Boolean(profile.assessmentStatus?.cVerified);
    const verdict = profile.assessmentStatus?.verdict || 'PENDING';
    const cheatFlags = profile.assessmentStatus?.cheatFlags || 0;

    const hasCppSkill = profile.skills.some((s) =>
      ['c++', 'c', 'data structures', 'stl', 'systems programming'].includes(s.toLowerCase())
    );
    const hasSyntaxPassed = Boolean(latestSubmission?.syntaxValid) || score >= 50;
    const isPointersMastered = masteredTopicIds.includes('topic-pointers-nullsafety');
    const isBufferMastered = masteredTopicIds.includes('topic-buffer-safety');
    const isRaiiMastered = masteredTopicIds.includes('topic-raii-memory');
    const isGraphMastered = masteredTopicIds.includes('topic-graph-bfs');

    const hasZeroMemoryLeakAudit =
      score >= 75 &&
      (!latestSubmission ||
        !latestSubmission.memorySafetyNotes.some(
          (n) => n.toLowerCase().includes('leak') || n.toLowerCase().includes('missing')
        ));

    const hasOptimalComplexity =
      score >= 75 &&
      (!latestSubmission || !latestSubmission.timeComplexity.includes('O(N^2)'));

    const hasUploadedCredentials = Boolean(
      profile.resumeFileName || profile.githubUrl || (profile.certificates && profile.certificates.length > 0)
    );

    const candidateApps = applications.filter((a) => a.studentId === profile.id);
    const hasAppliedOrJoinedTeam = candidateApps.some(
      (a) => a.status === 'PENDING' || a.status === 'APPROVED'
    );
    const hasAcceptedTeam = candidateApps.some((a) => a.status === 'APPROVED');

    // Define raw stages with checkpoints
    const rawStages: Array<{
      id: string;
      stepNumber: number;
      title: string;
      shortTitle: string;
      tierLabel: string;
      subtitle: string;
      cppModuleRef: string;
      checkpoints: RoadmapCheckpoint[];
      keyConcepts: string[];
      nextActionText: string;
    }> = [
      {
        id: 'stage-1-foundations',
        stepNumber: 1,
        title: 'Beginner C/C++ Syntax & Pointer Foundations',
        shortTitle: '1. C/C++ Foundations',
        tierLabel: 'Tier 1 • Beginner Concepts',
        subtitle: 'Address-of mechanics, defensive nullptr guards, and bounded C buffer operations.',
        cppModuleRef: 'StudentModule.h / DataStorage.h',
        keyConcepts: ['Raw Pointers (*, &)', 'Null-Safety Guards', 'Bounded I/O (fgets/snprintf)', 'Compilation & Syntax'],
        nextActionText: 'Review defensive pointer mechanics and mark foundational C/C++ topics as mastered.',
        checkpoints: [
          {
            id: 'cp-1-skill-profile',
            label: 'C/C++ Core Language & Syntax Readiness',
            detail: 'Declare C/C++ proficiency in student profile or pass initial syntax compiler check.',
            completed: hasCppSkill || hasSyntaxPassed,
            actionType: 'TAKE_ASSESSMENT',
          },
          {
            id: 'cp-1-null-guards',
            label: 'Defensive Pointer Arithmetic & Null-Safety Guards',
            detail: 'Validate raw pointers before dereferencing (`ptr != nullptr`) in linked structures.',
            completed: isPointersMastered,
            linkedTopicId: 'topic-pointers-nullsafety',
            actionType: 'TOGGLE_TOPIC',
          },
          {
            id: 'cp-1-buffer-bounds',
            label: 'Secure Buffer Boundaries & String Safety',
            detail: 'Eliminate unbounded C array writes using `fgets`, `snprintf`, or `std::string_view`.',
            completed: isBufferMastered,
            linkedTopicId: 'topic-buffer-safety',
            actionType: 'TOGGLE_TOPIC',
          },
        ],
      },
      {
        id: 'stage-2-memory-raii',
        stepNumber: 2,
        title: 'Dynamic Memory Management & RAII Idioms',
        shortTitle: '2. Memory & RAII',
        tierLabel: 'Tier 2 • Core Systems C++',
        subtitle: 'Deterministic destructor cleanup, heap lifecycle discipline, and smart pointers.',
        cppModuleRef: 'StudentModule.cpp / ProjectModule.cpp',
        keyConcepts: ['RAII Destructors (~T)', 'new[] / delete[] Pairing', 'std::unique_ptr', 'Zero-Leak Valgrind Hygiene'],
        nextActionText: 'Master RAII destructor patterns and pass the static memory-leak verification audit.',
        checkpoints: [
          {
            id: 'cp-2-raii-topic',
            label: 'RAII & Deterministic Heap Deallocation',
            detail: 'Pair every dynamic allocation with scoped destructor cleanup or `std::unique_ptr`.',
            completed: isRaiiMastered,
            linkedTopicId: 'topic-raii-memory',
            actionType: 'TOGGLE_TOPIC',
          },
          {
            id: 'cp-2-zero-leak-audit',
            label: 'Static Memory Leak Verification Audit',
            detail: 'Submit C/C++ assessment code with zero unfreed heap blocks and score >= 75%.',
            completed: hasZeroMemoryLeakAudit,
            actionType: 'TAKE_ASSESSMENT',
          },
          {
            id: 'cp-2-c-verified',
            label: 'Low-Level C Pointer & Memory Verification',
            detail: 'Achieve verified status in C pointer manipulation and linked node reallocation.',
            completed: cVerified || score >= 80,
            actionType: 'TAKE_ASSESSMENT',
          },
        ],
      },
      {
        id: 'stage-3-dsa-graphs',
        stepNumber: 3,
        title: 'STL Data Structures & Graph Algorithms',
        shortTitle: '3. STL & Graph Algorithms',
        tierLabel: 'Tier 3 • Algorithmic Fluency',
        subtitle: 'Adjacency lists, O(V + E) Breadth-First Search, and priority-queue ranking.',
        cppModuleRef: 'GraphModule.cpp / MatchingModule.cpp',
        keyConcepts: ['std::vector & unordered_map', 'Bipartite Adjacency Graph', 'BFS Shortest-Path', 'O(V + E) Complexity'],
        nextActionText: 'Master O(V + E) graph traversal and eliminate quadratic O(N^2) nested loops.',
        checkpoints: [
          {
            id: 'cp-3-graph-topic',
            label: 'Adjacency List & O(V + E) BFS Traversal',
            detail: 'Implement `std::queue` Breadth-First Search for skill-to-project graph discovery.',
            completed: isGraphMastered,
            linkedTopicId: 'topic-graph-bfs',
            actionType: 'TOGGLE_TOPIC',
          },
          {
            id: 'cp-3-complexity-audit',
            label: 'Optimal Time & Space Complexity Benchmark',
            detail: 'Pass automated Big-O complexity check without O(N^2) performance bottlenecks.',
            completed: hasOptimalComplexity,
            actionType: 'TAKE_ASSESSMENT',
          },
          {
            id: 'cp-3-cpp-verified',
            label: 'Modern C++ STL Container Proficiency',
            detail: 'Earn the Verified C++ badge via dynamic vector or graph engine assessment.',
            completed: cppVerified,
            actionType: 'TAKE_ASSESSMENT',
          },
        ],
      },
      {
        id: 'stage-4-verification',
        stepNumber: 4,
        title: 'Multi-Module Architecture & Proctored Verification',
        shortTitle: '4. Proctored Verification',
        tierLabel: 'Tier 4 • Verified Developer',
        subtitle: 'Anti-cheat proctored certification, multi-file header modularity, and recruiter audit.',
        cppModuleRef: 'main.cpp / TeamModule.h',
        keyConcepts: ['Header Guards (#pragma once)', 'Proctored Anti-Cheat Integrity', 'Recruiter Code Audit', 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE Tier'],
        nextActionText: 'Complete the proctored C/C++ assessment with >= 75% score and zero anti-cheat violations.',
        checkpoints: [
          {
            id: 'cp-4-proctored-integrity',
            label: 'Anti-Cheat Proctoring Integrity Clearance',
            detail: 'Complete timed assessment with zero unauthorized tab switches or clipboard injections.',
            completed: (cppVerified || cVerified || score >= 70) && cheatFlags === 0,
            actionType: 'TAKE_ASSESSMENT',
          },
          {
            id: 'cp-4-eligibility-verdict',
            label: 'Verified Eligible Recruiter Audit Verdict',
            detail: 'Achieve `VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE` status on the automated C/C++ recruiting rubric.',
            completed: verdict === 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE',
            actionType: 'TAKE_ASSESSMENT',
          },
          {
            id: 'cp-4-dual-language',
            label: 'Full-Stack Systems Mastery (C & C++ Dual Badge)',
            detail: 'Hold dual verification across both C++ OOP/STL and C pointer systems.',
            completed: cppVerified && cVerified,
            actionType: 'TAKE_ASSESSMENT',
          },
        ],
      },
      {
        id: 'stage-5-collaboration',
        stepNumber: 5,
        title: 'Advanced Project Collaboration Readiness',
        shortTitle: '5. Collaboration Ready',
        tierLabel: 'Tier 5 • Production Team Ready',
        subtitle: 'Verified engineering dossier, algorithmic team matching, and active PBL collaboration.',
        cppModuleRef: 'TeamModule.cpp / MatchingModule.h',
        keyConcepts: ['PDF Dossier & GitHub Repo', 'Graph Compatibility Matching', 'Leader Pre-Acceptance Chat', 'Multi-Developer PBL'],
        nextActionText: 'Apply to high-compatibility university project teams and collaborate with team leaders.',
        checkpoints: [
          {
            id: 'cp-5-dossier',
            label: 'Technical Resume PDF & GitHub Portfolio Linked',
            detail: 'Upload verified PDF credentials and GitHub repository link for team leader inspection.',
            completed: hasUploadedCredentials,
          },
          {
            id: 'cp-5-eligibility-unlocked',
            label: 'University Team Recruitment Eligibility Unlocked',
            detail: 'Meet minimum skill assessment threshold (`ELIGIBLE_WITH_CONDITIONS` or `VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE`).',
            completed: verdict === 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE' || verdict === 'ELIGIBLE_WITH_CONDITIONS',
            actionType: 'TAKE_ASSESSMENT',
          },
          {
            id: 'cp-5-active-collaboration',
            label: 'Active Team Application & Leader Collaboration',
            detail: hasAcceptedTeam
              ? 'Accepted into an active university project team with full team chat access.'
              : 'Submit an application to a matching team and initiate real-time leader discussion.',
            completed: hasAppliedOrJoinedTeam,
            actionType: 'BROWSE_TEAMS',
          },
        ],
      },
    ];

    // Calculate status and progress per stage
    let previousStageMastered = true;
    let currentStageId = rawStages[0].id;
    let foundActiveStage = false;

    const stages: RoadmapStage[] = rawStages.map((raw) => {
      const totalCp = raw.checkpoints.length;
      const doneCp = raw.checkpoints.filter((c) => c.completed).length;
      const progressPercent = totalCp > 0 ? Math.round((doneCp / totalCp) * 100) : 0;

      let status: RoadmapStage['status'];
      if (progressPercent === 100) {
        status = 'MASTERED';
      } else if (progressPercent > 0 || previousStageMastered) {
        status = 'IN_PROGRESS';
        if (!foundActiveStage) {
          currentStageId = raw.id;
          foundActiveStage = true;
        }
      } else {
        status = 'UNLOCKED';
      }

      previousStageMastered = progressPercent === 100;

      return {
        ...raw,
        status,
        progressPercent,
      };
    });

    if (!foundActiveStage) {
      currentStageId = stages[stages.length - 1].id;
    }

    const totalCheckpoints = stages.reduce((acc, s) => acc + s.checkpoints.length, 0);
    const completedCheckpoints = stages.reduce(
      (acc, s) => acc + s.checkpoints.filter((c) => c.completed).length,
      0
    );
    const overallReadinessPercent =
      totalCheckpoints > 0 ? Math.round((completedCheckpoints / totalCheckpoints) * 100) : 0;
    const completedStagesCount = stages.filter((s) => s.status === 'MASTERED').length;

    let readinessTierTitle = 'Stage 1: Foundational C/C++ Learner';
    let readinessSummary =
      'Build core pointer safety and memory management habits to unlock university project team eligibility.';

    if (overallReadinessPercent >= 90) {
      readinessTierTitle = 'Stage 5: Advanced Project Collaboration Ready';
      readinessSummary =
        'You have mastered C/C++ memory safety, STL graph algorithms, and proctored verification—fully ready to lead or contribute to top university PBL teams.';
    } else if (overallReadinessPercent >= 70) {
      readinessTierTitle = 'Stage 4: Verified Systems & STL Practitioner';
      readinessSummary =
        'Strong command of C++ RAII, STL containers, and algorithmic complexity. Complete remaining verification or team checkpoints for 100% readiness.';
    } else if (overallReadinessPercent >= 45) {
      readinessTierTitle = 'Stage 3: Intermediate C++ & Data Structures Builder';
      readinessSummary =
        'Solid progress on foundational pointer safety. Focus on RAII heap cleanup and O(V + E) graph algorithms to earn full verification.';
    }

    return {
      overallReadinessPercent,
      readinessTierTitle,
      readinessSummary,
      completedStagesCount,
      totalStagesCount: stages.length,
      currentStageId,
      stages,
    };
  }
}

import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {
  verifyAdminCredentials,
  canViewUserAccountAndActivity,
  getVisibleActivitiesForViewer,
  matchTeamsBySkillAndInterest,
} from './portalAuthEngine';
import { ActivityLog, Team } from './types';

describe('portalAuthEngine', () => {
  it('verifies exact Admin credentials (adminsm$4146 / adminsrsad@414.) and rejects invalid ones', () => {
    assert.equal(verifyAdminCredentials('adminsm$4146', 'adminsrsad@414.'), true);
    assert.equal(verifyAdminCredentials('  adminsm$4146 ', ' adminsrsad@414. '), true);
    assert.equal(verifyAdminCredentials('adminsm$4146', 'adminsrsad@414'), true);
    assert.equal(verifyAdminCredentials('admin', 'adminsrsad@414.'), false);
    assert.equal(verifyAdminCredentials('adminsm$4146', 'wrongpass'), false);
  });

  it('enforces strict privacy visibility so data and activities are only visible to the creator and authenticated Admin', () => {
    // Creator can view their own data
    assert.equal(
      canViewUserAccountAndActivity({
        viewerRole: 'STUDENT',
        isAdminAuthenticated: false,
        viewerUserId: 'student-101',
        targetOwnerId: 'student-101',
      }),
      true
    );

    // Another student or team leader CANNOT view student-101's private data
    assert.equal(
      canViewUserAccountAndActivity({
        viewerRole: 'STUDENT',
        isAdminAuthenticated: false,
        viewerUserId: 'student-202',
        targetOwnerId: 'student-101',
      }),
      false
    );
    assert.equal(
      canViewUserAccountAndActivity({
        viewerRole: 'TEAM_LEADER',
        isAdminAuthenticated: false,
        viewerUserId: 'leader-303',
        targetOwnerId: 'student-101',
      }),
      false
    );

    // Unauthenticated ADMIN role cannot bypass privacy gate
    assert.equal(
      canViewUserAccountAndActivity({
        viewerRole: 'ADMIN',
        isAdminAuthenticated: false,
        viewerUserId: 'admin-system',
        targetOwnerId: 'student-101',
      }),
      false
    );

    // Authenticated Admin CAN view any user's data
    assert.equal(
      canViewUserAccountAndActivity({
        viewerRole: 'ADMIN',
        isAdminAuthenticated: true,
        viewerUserId: 'admin-system',
        targetOwnerId: 'student-101',
      }),
      true
    );
  });

  it('filters activity logs so non-admin users only see their own activities while Admin sees all', () => {
    const sampleLogs: ActivityLog[] = [
      {
        id: '1',
        type: 'REGISTRATION',
        description: 'Aarav registered as Team Member',
        actor: 'Aarav',
        actorId: 'user-aarav',
        timestamp: '10:00 AM',
      },
      {
        id: '2',
        type: 'PROJECT_CREATED',
        description: 'Priya created new project Quantum Net',
        actor: 'Priya',
        actorId: 'user-priya',
        timestamp: '10:05 AM',
      },
    ];

    const aaravLogs = getVisibleActivitiesForViewer({
      logs: sampleLogs,
      viewerRole: 'STUDENT',
      isAdminAuthenticated: false,
      viewerUserId: 'user-aarav',
      viewerName: 'Aarav',
    });
    assert.equal(aaravLogs.length, 1);
    assert.equal(aaravLogs[0].id, '1');

    const adminLogs = getVisibleActivitiesForViewer({
      logs: sampleLogs,
      viewerRole: 'ADMIN',
      isAdminAuthenticated: true,
      viewerUserId: 'admin-system',
    });
    assert.equal(adminLogs.length, 2);
  });

  it('matches teams by both student skills and interests', () => {
    const mockTeams: Team[] = [
      {
        id: 't-robotics',
        title: 'Autonomous Robotics C++ Kernel',
        department: 'Computer Science & Engineering',
        category: 'Robotics & Embedded',
        description: 'Building real-time ROS2 and C++ control loops',
        requiredSkills: ['C++', 'Algorithms'],
        teamSize: 4,
        leaderId: 'l1',
        leaderName: 'Lead 1',
        leaderEmail: 'l1@geu.ac.in',
        members: [],
        vacancyOpen: true,
        deadline: '2026-12-01',
        tags: ['Robotics', 'C++', 'Embedded'],
        bannerGradient: 'from-indigo-600 to-violet-600',
        createdAt: '2026-10-01',
      },
      {
        id: 't-web',
        title: 'Campus Portal UI',
        department: 'Information Technology',
        category: 'Web Development',
        description: 'Frontend dashboard in React',
        requiredSkills: ['React', 'CSS'],
        teamSize: 4,
        leaderId: 'l2',
        leaderName: 'Lead 2',
        leaderEmail: 'l2@geu.ac.in',
        members: [],
        vacancyOpen: true,
        deadline: '2026-12-01',
        tags: ['Frontend', 'UI/UX'],
        bannerGradient: 'from-blue-600 to-cyan-600',
        createdAt: '2026-10-01',
      },
    ];

    const results = matchTeamsBySkillAndInterest(
      {
        skills: ['C++', 'Algorithms'],
        interests: ['Robotics', 'Embedded Systems'],
        department: 'Computer Science & Engineering',
      },
      mockTeams
    );

    assert.equal(results.length, 2);
    assert.equal(results[0].team.id, 't-robotics');
    assert.ok(results[0].combinedScore >= 95);
    assert.ok(results[0].matchedInterests.includes('Robotics'));
  });

  it('populates complete login details and profiles for all leaders and members across all 105+ teams', async () => {
    const { INITIAL_STUDENTS, INITIAL_TEAMS, INITIAL_APPLICATIONS } = await import('./initialData');
    const { ensureAllTeamMembersInStudents, getTeamFullIntelligence, getUserTeamsMap } = await import('./portalAuthEngine');

    assert.ok(INITIAL_TEAMS.length >= 105, 'Should have at least 105 university teams');
    assert.ok(INITIAL_STUDENTS.length > 150, `Expected >150 full student profiles across all teams, got ${INITIAL_STUDENTS.length}`);

    const synced = ensureAllTeamMembersInStudents(INITIAL_STUDENTS, INITIAL_TEAMS);
    for (const person of synced) {
      assert.ok(person.email && person.email.includes('@'), `Person ${person.id} missing email`);
      assert.ok(person.studentIdNum && person.studentIdNum.length >= 6, `Person ${person.id} missing studentIdNum`);
      assert.ok(person.password && person.password.length >= 6, `Person ${person.id} missing login password`);
    }

    const firstTeamIntel = getTeamFullIntelligence(INITIAL_TEAMS[0], synced, INITIAL_APPLICATIONS);
    assert.equal(firstTeamIntel.team.id, 'team-sigma');
    assert.equal(firstTeamIntel.memberRecords.length, 3);
    assert.ok(firstTeamIntel.leaderProfile);
    assert.equal(firstTeamIntel.leaderProfile?.id, 'student-shreya');
    assert.ok(firstTeamIntel.memberRecords.every((r) => r.loginPassword && r.loginEmail && r.loginId));

    const userTeams = getUserTeamsMap(INITIAL_TEAMS);
    assert.equal(
      userTeams['student-shreya'].ledTeams.length,
      1,
      'Shreya Yadav must be leader of ONLY 1 team (Team Sigma)'
    );
    assert.equal(userTeams['student-shreya'].ledTeams[0].id, 'team-sigma');
    assert.equal(
      userTeams['student-shreya'].memberTeams.length,
      1,
      'Shreya Yadav must only be in Team Sigma'
    );
    assert.equal(userTeams['student-shreya'].memberTeams[0].id, 'team-sigma');
  });

  it('provides deep project descriptions, architecture modules, milestones, and unique leadership for every single team', async () => {
    const { INITIAL_TEAMS } = await import('./initialData');
    const { getTeamDeepProjectDetails } = await import('./portalAuthEngine');

    const leaderIds = new Set<string>();
    for (const team of INITIAL_TEAMS) {
      assert.ok(team.leaderId, `Team ${team.id} must have a leaderId`);
      if (team.id !== 'team-sigma') {
        assert.notEqual(
          team.leaderId,
          'student-shreya',
          `Team ${team.id} must not have Shreya Yadav as leader`
        );
        assert.ok(
          !team.leaderName.toLowerCase().includes('shreya yadav'),
          `Team ${team.id} must not list Shreya Yadav as leaderName`
        );
      }
      leaderIds.add(team.leaderId);

      const deep = getTeamDeepProjectDetails(team);
      assert.ok(deep.deepDescription.length >= 3, `Team ${team.id} missing deepDescription paragraphs`);
      assert.ok(deep.problemStatement.length > 20, `Team ${team.id} missing problemStatement`);
      assert.ok(deep.objectives.length >= 4, `Team ${team.id} missing objectives`);
      assert.ok(deep.architectureModules.length >= 4, `Team ${team.id} missing architectureModules`);
      assert.ok(deep.milestones.length >= 4, `Team ${team.id} missing milestones`);
      assert.ok(deep.techStackBreakdown.length >= 2, `Team ${team.id} missing techStackBreakdown`);
      assert.ok(deep.roleResponsibilities.length >= team.teamSize, `Team ${team.id} missing roleResponsibilities`);
      assert.ok(deep.expectedOutcomes.length >= 3, `Team ${team.id} missing expectedOutcomes`);
      assert.ok(deep.repositoryUrl.startsWith('https://'), `Team ${team.id} missing repositoryUrl`);
      assert.ok(deep.documentationUrl.startsWith('https://'), `Team ${team.id} missing documentationUrl`);
    }

    assert.equal(leaderIds.size, INITIAL_TEAMS.length, 'Every initial team should have a distinct leader');
  });
});

FILE: src/lib/portalAuthEngine.ts (597 lines)

import { ActivityLog, StudentProfile, Team, TeamApplication, TeamMember } from './types';
import { MatchingEngine, defaultSkillGraph } from './graphEngine';

export const ADMIN_USERNAME = 'adminsm$4146';
export const ADMIN_PASSWORD_EXACT = 'adminsrsad@414.';
export const ADMIN_PASSWORD_ALT = 'adminsrsad@414';

/**
 * Verifies the Supreme Admin credentials:
 * Username: adminsm$4146
 * Password: adminsrsad@414. (or adminsrsad@414)
 */
export function verifyAdminCredentials(username: string, password: string): boolean {
  const cleanUser = username.trim();
  const cleanPass = password.trim();
  return (
    cleanUser === ADMIN_USERNAME &&
    (cleanPass === ADMIN_PASSWORD_EXACT || cleanPass === ADMIN_PASSWORD_ALT)
  );
}

/**
 * Enforces strict privacy visibility rule:
 * When a user creates an account in any option (Team Member or Team Leader),
 * their stored data and every activity is ONLY visible to:
 * 1. The person who created that account (viewerUserId === targetOwnerId)
 * 2. The authenticated Admin (viewerRole === 'ADMIN' && isAdminAuthenticated)
 */
export function canViewUserAccountAndActivity(params: {
  viewerRole: 'STUDENT' | 'TEAM_LEADER' | 'ADMIN';
  isAdminAuthenticated: boolean;
  viewerUserId: string;
  targetOwnerId: string;
}): boolean {
  const { viewerRole, isAdminAuthenticated, viewerUserId, targetOwnerId } = params;
  if (viewerRole === 'ADMIN' && isAdminAuthenticated) {
    return true;
  }
  if (!viewerUserId || !targetOwnerId) {
    return false;
  }
  return viewerUserId === targetOwnerId;
}

/**
 * Filters platform activity logs so a user ONLY sees their own activities,
 * whereas an authenticated Admin sees every activity across the website.
 */
export function getVisibleActivitiesForViewer(params: {
  logs: ActivityLog[];
  viewerRole: 'STUDENT' | 'TEAM_LEADER' | 'ADMIN';
  isAdminAuthenticated: boolean;
  viewerUserId: string;
  viewerName?: string;
}): ActivityLog[] {
  const { logs, viewerRole, isAdminAuthenticated, viewerUserId, viewerName } = params;
  if (viewerRole === 'ADMIN' && isAdminAuthenticated) {
    return logs;
  }
  if (!viewerUserId) return [];
  return logs.filter((log) => {
    if (log.actorId) {
      return log.actorId === viewerUserId;
    }
    if (viewerName && log.actor) {
      return log.actor.toLowerCase() === viewerName.toLowerCase();
    }
    return false;
  });
}

export interface MatchedTeamResult {
  team: Team;
  skillScore: number;
  interestMatchCount: number;
  matchedInterests: string[];
  combinedScore: number;
  exactMatches: string[];
  relatedMatches: { required: string; candidateHas: string; weight: number }[];
  missingSkills: string[];
}

/**
 * Matches teams for a registered student based on BOTH their skills (C++ Graph Engine)
 * AND their interests / domain tags.
 */
export function matchTeamsBySkillAndInterest(
  student: Pick<StudentProfile, 'skills' | 'interests' | 'department'>,
  teams: Team[],
  onlyOpenVacancies = true
): MatchedTeamResult[] {
  const normalizedInterests = (student.interests || [])
    .map((i) => i.trim().toLowerCase())
    .filter(Boolean);
  const normalizedSkills = (student.skills || [])
    .map((s) => s.trim().toLowerCase())
    .filter(Boolean);

  return teams
    .filter((team) => !onlyOpenVacancies || (team.vacancyOpen && team.members.length < team.teamSize))
    .map((team) => {
      const detailed = MatchingEngine.calculateDetailedScore(
        student.skills || [],
        team.requiredSkills || [],
        defaultSkillGraph
      );

      const searchableTeamTokens = [
        team.category,
        team.department,
        team.title,
        team.description,
        ...(team.tags || []),
        ...(team.requiredSkills || []),
      ]
        .join(' ')
        .toLowerCase();

      const matchedInterests: string[] = [];
      for (const interest of student.interests || []) {
        const clean = interest.trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!clean) continue;
        // Check if any word or phrase of the interest appears in the team's category, tags, title, or description
        const words = clean.split(/[\s/&,-]+/).filter((w) => w.length >= 2);
        if (
          searchableTeamTokens.includes(clean) ||
          words.some((w) => searchableTeamTokens.includes(w))
        ) {
          matchedInterests.push(interest);
        }
      }

      // Also check if student skills align with team tags/category
      let tagSkillBonus = 0;
      for (const sk of normalizedSkills) {
        if ((team.tags || []).some((t) => t.toLowerCase().includes(sk))) {
          tagSkillBonus += 4;
        }
      }

      const interestBonus = Math.min(20, matchedInterests.length * 8);
      const deptBonus =
        student.department &&
        team.department.toLowerCase().includes(student.department.toLowerCase().split(' ')[0])
          ? 5
          : 0;

      const combinedScore = Math.min(
        100,
        Math.round(detailed.score * 0.8 + interestBonus + Math.min(10, tagSkillBonus) + deptBonus)
      );

      const exactMatches = detailed.breakdown
        .filter((b) => b.matchType === 'exact')
        .map((b) => b.required);
      const relatedMatches = detailed.breakdown
        .filter((b) => b.matchType === 'related')
        .map((b) => ({
          required: b.required,
          candidateHas: b.matchedSkill || '',
          weight: b.score / 100,
        }));
      const missingSkills = detailed.breakdown
        .filter((b) => b.matchType === 'none')
        .map((b) => b.required);

      return {
        team,
        skillScore: detailed.score,
        interestMatchCount: matchedInterests.length,
        matchedInterests,
        combinedScore: Math.max(detailed.score, combinedScore),
        exactMatches,
        relatedMatches,
        missingSkills,
      };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => {
      if (b.combinedScore !== a.combinedScore) {
        return b.combinedScore - a.combinedScore;
      }
      if (b.interestMatchCount !== a.interestMatchCount) {
        return b.interestMatchCount - a.interestMatchCount;
      }
      return b.skillScore - a.skillScore;
    });
}

/**
 * Guarantees every team leader and team member across all teams has a complete StudentProfile
 * with login credentials (email, studentIdNum, password), department, skills, and interests.
 */
export function ensureAllTeamMembersInStudents(
  students: StudentProfile[],
  teams: Team[]
): StudentProfile[] {
  const byId = new Map<string, StudentProfile>();
  let seq = 500;

  for (const stu of students) {
    const firstToken = stu.name.split(' ')[0].replace(/[^a-zA-Z]/g, '') || 'User';
    const fallbackPass = `${firstToken}@${stu.studentIdNum.slice(-4) || '2026'}#gehu`;
    byId.set(stu.id, {
      ...stu,
      password: stu.password || fallbackPass,
      accountType: stu.accountType || 'TEAM_MEMBER',
      createdAt: stu.createdAt || '2026-10-15 10:00',
    });
  }

  for (const team of teams) {
    for (const member of team.members) {
      if (byId.has(member.id)) continue;
      seq += 1;
      const isLeader = member.role === 'Leader' || member.id === team.leaderId;
      const studentIdNum = isLeader ? `2510110${seq}` : `2510190${seq}`;
      const firstToken = member.name.split(' ')[0].replace(/[^a-zA-Z]/g, '') || 'User';
      const password = `${firstToken}@${seq}#gehu`;
      const cleanSkills =
        member.skills && member.skills.length > 0
          ? member.skills.map((s) => s.split('|')[0])
          : ['C++', 'Algorithms'];

      byId.set(member.id, {
        id: member.id,
        studentIdNum,
        name: member.name,
        email: member.email,
        password,
        accountType: isLeader ? 'TEAM_LEADER' : 'TEAM_MEMBER',
        createdAt: '2026-10-18 11:00',
        phone: `+91 98100 ${String(10000 + seq).slice(-5)}`,
        department: team.department,
        year: isLeader ? '3rd Year (Project Lead)' : '3rd Year',
        avatar: member.avatar || '👨‍💻',
        bio: isLeader
          ? `Team Leader for ${team.title} (${team.department}).`
          : `Project Member in ${team.title} (${team.department}).`,
        skills: cleanSkills,
        interests: [team.category, ...(team.tags || []).slice(0, 2)],
        availability: '15 hrs/week',
        certificates: [],
        assessmentStatus: {
          cppVerified: true,
          cVerified: false,
          score: 85,
          verdict: 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE',
          cheatFlags: 0,
          completedAt: '2026-10-20 14:00',
        },
        blockedUsers: [],
        isBanned: false,
      });
    }
  }

  return Array.from(byId.values());
}

export interface TeamMemberFullRecord {
  member: TeamMember;
  profile: StudentProfile;
  isLeader: boolean;
  loginEmail: string;
  loginId: string;
  loginPassword: string;
}

export interface TeamFullIntelligence {
  team: Team;
  leaderProfile: StudentProfile | null;
  memberRecords: TeamMemberFullRecord[];
  teamApplications: TeamApplication[];
  capacityMetrics: {
    filled: number;
    total: number;
    openSpots: number;
    fillPercentage: number;
    isFull: boolean;
  };
}

/**
 * Resolves exhaustive team & member login intelligence for the Admin Team Inspector.
 */
export function getTeamFullIntelligence(
  team: Team,
  students: StudentProfile[],
  applications: TeamApplication[]
): TeamFullIntelligence {
  const studentMap = new Map<string, StudentProfile>();
  for (const s of students) {
    studentMap.set(s.id, s);
  }

  const memberRecords: TeamMemberFullRecord[] = team.members.map((m) => {
    const existing = studentMap.get(m.id);
    const firstToken = m.name.split(' ')[0].replace(/[^a-zA-Z]/g, '') || 'User';
    const fallbackProfile: StudentProfile = existing || {
      id: m.id,
      studentIdNum: `251019999`,
      name: m.name,
      email: m.email,
      password: `${firstToken}@2026#gehu`,
      accountType: m.role === 'Leader' ? 'TEAM_LEADER' : 'TEAM_MEMBER',
      createdAt: team.createdAt || '2026-10-18',
      department: team.department,
      year: '3rd Year',
      avatar: m.avatar || '👨‍💻',
      bio: `${m.role} of ${team.title}`,
      skills: m.skills || [],
      interests: [team.category],
      availability: '15 hrs/week',
      certificates: [],
      assessmentStatus: {
        cppVerified: true,
        cVerified: false,
        score: 85,
        verdict: 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE',
        cheatFlags: 0,
      },
    };
    const loginPassword = fallbackProfile.password || `${firstToken}@2026#gehu`;

    return {
      member: m,
      profile: fallbackProfile,
      isLeader: m.role === 'Leader' || m.id === team.leaderId,
      loginEmail: fallbackProfile.email,
      loginId: fallbackProfile.studentIdNum,
      loginPassword,
    };
  });

  const leaderProfile =
    studentMap.get(team.leaderId) ||
    memberRecords.find((r) => r.isLeader)?.profile ||
    null;

  const teamApplications = applications.filter((a) => a.teamId === team.id);
  const filled = team.members.length;
  const total = Math.max(1, team.teamSize);
  const openSpots = Math.max(0, total - filled);
  const fillPercentage = Math.min(100, Math.round((filled / total) * 100));

  return {
    team,
    leaderProfile,
    memberRecords,
    teamApplications,
    capacityMetrics: {
      filled,
      total,
      openSpots,
      fillPercentage,
      isFull: filled >= total,
    },
  };
}

/**
 * Maps every user ID to the teams they lead and the teams they are a member of.
 */
export function getUserTeamsMap(teams: Team[]): Record<
  string,
  { ledTeams: Team[]; memberTeams: Team[] }
> {
  const map: Record<string, { ledTeams: Team[]; memberTeams: Team[] }> = {};
  for (const team of teams) {
    if (team.leaderId) {
      if (!map[team.leaderId]) {
        map[team.leaderId] = { ledTeams: [], memberTeams: [] };
      }
      map[team.leaderId].ledTeams.push(team);
    }
    for (const m of team.members) {
      if (!map[m.id]) {
        map[m.id] = { ledTeams: [], memberTeams: [] };
      }
      if (!map[m.id].memberTeams.some((t) => t.id === team.id)) {
        map[m.id].memberTeams.push(team);
      }
    }
  }
  return map;
}

export interface ResolvedTeamDeepProjectDetails {
  deepDescription: string[];
  problemStatement: string;
  objectives: string[];
  architectureModules: {
    name: string;
    role: string;
    tech: string;
    description: string;
  }[];
  milestones: {
    phase: string;
    title: string;
    timeline: string;
    status: 'COMPLETED' | 'IN_PROGRESS' | 'UPCOMING';
    deliverable: string;
  }[];
  techStackBreakdown: {
    category: string;
    items: string[];
  }[];
  roleResponsibilities: {
    roleTitle: string;
    assignedTo: string;
    memberId?: string;
    status: 'FILLED' | 'OPEN_VACANCY';
    responsibilities: string;
    skills: string[];
  }[];
  expectedOutcomes: string[];
  repositoryUrl: string;
  documentationUrl: string;
  academicCourseCode: string;
}

/**
 * Resolves full, deep project documentation, architecture breakdown, milestones,
 * role allocations, and deliverables for ANY team on the platform (all 105+ initial teams
 * and any newly created team).
 */
export function getTeamDeepProjectDetails(team: Team): ResolvedTeamDeepProjectDetails {
  const cleanSkills = (team.requiredSkills || ['C++', 'Algorithms']).map((s) => s.split('|')[0]);
  const primarySkill = cleanSkills[0] || 'C++';
  const secondarySkill = cleanSkills[1] || 'Algorithms';
  const openSlotsCount = Math.max(0, team.teamSize - team.members.length);
  const courseCode =
    team.academicCourseCode ||
    `B.Tech ${(team.category || 'CSE').split(' ')[0].toUpperCase()}-2026-PBL`;

  const deepDescription =
    team.deepDescription && team.deepDescription.length > 0
      ? team.deepDescription
      : [
          `${team.title} is an official engineering and research project team operating under the Department of ${team.department} (${team.category}) at Graphic Era Deemed to be University. Led by ${team.leaderName}, the project focuses on: ${team.description}`,
          `The technical architecture is built around ${team.requiredSkills.join(', ')} with strict modular boundaries, deterministic memory and execution guarantees, and automated test verification. Each team member owns a dedicated subsystem while collaborating through shared Git workflows and weekly sprint reviews.`,
          `Candidates joining ${team.title} work directly with ${team.leaderName} and the core roster to design, implement, benchmark, and document the complete system ahead of the ${team.deadline} evaluation milestone.`,
        ];

  const problemStatement =
    team.problemStatement ||
    `Existing approaches in ${team.category} face performance, scalability, and integration bottlenecks when addressing ${team.description.toLowerCase().replace(/\.$/, '')}. ${team.title} solves this challenge through a purpose-built architecture leveraging ${team.requiredSkills.join(' and ')}.`;

  const objectives =
    team.objectives && team.objectives.length > 0
      ? team.objectives
      : [
          `Design and implement the core ${team.title} engine using ${primarySkill} and ${secondarySkill}.`,
          `Ensure end-to-end integration across ${team.requiredSkills.join(', ')} modules with high reliability.`,
          `Validate system throughput, memory safety, and edge-case correctness via automated test suites.`,
          `Deliver complete source code, technical documentation, and live demonstration by ${team.deadline}.`,
        ];

  const architectureModules =
    team.architectureModules && team.architectureModules.length > 0
      ? team.architectureModules
      : [
          {
            name: `Module 1: Core ${primarySkill} Engine & State Kernel`,
            role: `Led by ${team.leaderName}`,
            tech: `${primarySkill}, ${secondarySkill}`,
            description: `Implements the primary data structures, algorithmic kernel, and execution pipeline for ${team.title}.`,
          },
          {
            name: `Module 2: ${team.category} Processing & Integration Layer`,
            role: team.members[1] ? `Engineered by ${team.members[1].name}` : 'Core Team Roster',
            tech: team.requiredSkills.join(', '),
            description: `Manages subsystem communication, data transformation, and modular service orchestration.`,
          },
          {
            name: 'Module 3: Interface, Telemetry & Diagnostics Module',
            role: team.members[2] ? `Engineered by ${team.members[2].name}` : 'Collaborative Team Module',
            tech: `${cleanSkills[cleanSkills.length - 1] || primarySkill}, Real-time Telemetry`,
            description: `Provides interactive visualization, runtime metrics, and user/system interfacing for ${team.title}.`,
          },
          {
            name: 'Module 4: Verification, Benchmarking & QA Suite',
            role: openSlotsCount > 0 ? 'Open Vacancy / Sprint Collaboration' : 'Team QA & Verification',
            tech: `Automated Testing, Profiling, ${primarySkill}`,
            description: `Executes unit tests, stress benchmarks, and memory/correctness audits across all project modules.`,
          },
        ];

  const milestones =
    team.milestones && team.milestones.length > 0
      ? team.milestones
      : [
          {
            phase: 'Phase I',
            title: 'Problem Definition & System Architecture Blueprint',
            timeline: 'Completed',
            status: 'COMPLETED' as const,
            deliverable: `Approved technical specification and module interfaces for ${team.title}.`,
          },
          {
            phase: 'Phase II',
            title: `Core ${primarySkill} & ${secondarySkill} Implementation`,
            timeline: 'Completed',
            status: 'COMPLETED' as const,
            deliverable: `Working core engine and primary data structures verified on unit test suites.`,
          },
          {
            phase: 'Phase III',
            title: 'Full Module Integration & Performance Optimization',
            timeline: 'Active Sprint',
            status: 'IN_PROGRESS' as const,
            deliverable: `End-to-end integration of ${team.requiredSkills.join(', ')} components and stress profiling.`,
          },
          {
            phase: 'Phase IV',
            title: 'Final Evaluation, Documentation & Deployment',
            timeline: `Due ${team.deadline}`,
            status: 'UPCOMING' as const,
            deliverable: 'Final project defense, complete repository release, and technical report.',
          },
        ];

  const techStackBreakdown =
    team.techStackBreakdown && team.techStackBreakdown.length > 0
      ? team.techStackBreakdown
      : [
          {
            category: 'Required Technical Skills',
            items: team.requiredSkills.map((s) => s.replace('|', ' OR ')),
          },
          {
            category: 'Domain & Academic Track',
            items: [team.category, team.department, ...(team.tags || [])],
          },
          {
            category: 'Engineering & Collaboration Tools',
            items: ['Git / GitHub', 'Automated Test Suite', 'SkillMatch Team Workspace', 'Code Review Pipeline'],
          },
        ];

  const roleResponsibilities =
    team.roleResponsibilities && team.roleResponsibilities.length > 0
      ? team.roleResponsibilities
      : [
          ...team.members.map((m, idx) => ({
            roleTitle:
              m.role === 'Leader' || idx === 0
                ? `Team Leader & Lead ${primarySkill} Architect`
                : `Core ${m.skills[0] || primarySkill} Module Engineer`,
            assignedTo: m.name,
            memberId: m.id,
            status: 'FILLED' as const,
            responsibilities:
              m.role === 'Leader' || idx === 0
                ? `Leads overall system architecture, sprint planning, code reviews, and core ${primarySkill} integration for ${team.title}.`
                : `Implements and tests ${m.skills.join(' & ')} components and collaborates on milestone deliverables.`,
            skills: m.skills && m.skills.length > 0 ? m.skills : [primarySkill],
          })),
          ...Array.from({ length: openSlotsCount }).map((_, slotIdx) => ({
            roleTitle: `Open Team Member Slot #${slotIdx + 1} (${team.requiredSkills[slotIdx % team.requiredSkills.length] || primarySkill})`,
            assignedTo: 'Open Vacancy — Ready for Applicant',
            status: 'OPEN_VACANCY' as const,
            responsibilities: `Contribute to ${team.title} module development using ${team.requiredSkills.join(', ')}, participate in team sprints, and co-author final deliverables.`,
            skills: team.requiredSkills,
          })),
        ];

  const expectedOutcomes =
    team.expectedOutcomes && team.expectedOutcomes.length > 0
      ? team.expectedOutcomes
      : [
          `Fully functional, benchmarked prototype for ${team.title}.`,
          `Modular, well-documented codebase utilizing ${team.requiredSkills.join(', ')}.`,
          `Comprehensive technical report and live presentation for ${team.department}.`,
        ];

  return {
    deepDescription,
    problemStatement,
    objectives,
    architectureModules,
    milestones,
    techStackBreakdown,
    roleResponsibilities,
    expectedOutcomes,
    repositoryUrl:
      team.repositoryUrl ||
      `https://github.com/gehu-projects-2026/${team.id}-${team.title.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')}`,
    documentationUrl:
      team.documentationUrl ||
      `https://gehu.ac.in/projects/2026/${team.id}/documentation`,
    academicCourseCode: courseCode,
  };
}

import { test, describe } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert';
import { StreakEngine, formatToDateKey } from './streakEngine';
import { AssessmentSubmission } from './types';

describe('StreakEngine', () => {
  const refDate = new Date('2026-11-01T12:00:00Z');
  const todayKey = formatToDateKey(refDate);

  const yesterday = new Date(refDate);
  yesterday.setDate(yesterday.getDate() - 1);
  const yesterdayKey = formatToDateKey(yesterday);

  const dayBefore = new Date(refDate);
  dayBefore.setDate(dayBefore.getDate() - 2);
  const dayBeforeKey = formatToDateKey(dayBefore);

  test('returns 0 streak when candidate has no assessment activity', () => {
    const data = StreakEngine.getStreakData('student-1', [], [], refDate);
    assert.strictEqual(data.currentStreak, 0);
    assert.strictEqual(data.hasPracticedToday, false);
    assert.strictEqual(data.status, 'INACTIVE');
    assert.strictEqual(data.totalActiveDays, 0);
  });

  test('detects active streak when practiced today', () => {
    const mockSubmission: AssessmentSubmission = {
      id: 'sub-101',
      candidateId: 'student-1',
      candidateName: 'Test Student',
      candidateEmail: 'test@gehu.ac.in',
      language: 'cpp',
      challengeId: 'cpp-smart-vector',
      challengeTitle: 'Vector Challenge',
      submittedCode: 'int main(){}',
      syntaxValid: true,
      testCasesPassed: 4,
      totalTestCases: 4,
      score: 100,
      timeSpentSeconds: 300,
      tabSwitchCount: 0,
      copyPasteCount: 0,
      cheatSuspicion: 'LOW',
      timeComplexity: 'O(1)',
      spaceComplexity: 'O(N)',
      memorySafetyNotes: [],
      verdict: 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE',
      personalizedFeedback: 'Great work',
      submittedAt: `${todayKey} 10:30`,
    };

    const data = StreakEngine.getStreakData('student-1', [mockSubmission], [], refDate);
    assert.strictEqual(data.currentStreak, 1);
    assert.strictEqual(data.hasPracticedToday, true);
    assert.strictEqual(data.status, 'ACTIVE_TODAY');
    assert.strictEqual(data.totalActiveDays, 1);
  });

  test('identifies AT_RISK status when practiced yesterday but not today', () => {
    const mockSubmission: AssessmentSubmission = {
      id: 'sub-102',
      candidateId: 'student-1',
      candidateName: 'Test Student',
      candidateEmail: 'test@gehu.ac.in',
      language: 'c',
      challengeId: 'c-pointer-string-reverse',
      challengeTitle: 'Pointer Reversal',
      submittedCode: 'void rev(){}',
      syntaxValid: true,
      testCasesPassed: 4,
      totalTestCases: 4,
      score: 90,
      timeSpentSeconds: 250,
      tabSwitchCount: 0,
      copyPasteCount: 0,
      cheatSuspicion: 'LOW',
      timeComplexity: 'O(N)',
      spaceComplexity: 'O(1)',
      memorySafetyNotes: [],
      verdict: 'VERIFIED_ELIGIBLE',
      personalizedFeedback: 'Good pointers',
      submittedAt: `${yesterdayKey} 18:00`,
    };

    const data = StreakEngine.getStreakData('student-1', [mockSubmission], [], refDate);
    assert.strictEqual(data.currentStreak, 1);
    assert.strictEqual(data.hasPracticedToday, false);
    assert.strictEqual(data.status, 'AT_RISK');
  });

  test('calculates 3-day consecutive streak across yesterday and day before', () => {
    const dates = [dayBeforeKey, yesterdayKey, todayKey];
    const data = StreakEngine.getStreakData('student-1', [], dates, refDate);

    assert.strictEqual(data.currentStreak, 3);
    assert.strictEqual(data.longestStreak, 3);
    assert.strictEqual(data.status, 'ACTIVE_TODAY');
    assert.strictEqual(data.milestones[0].unlocked, true); // 3-day Consistency Bronze unlocked
  });

  test('generates correct 7-day strip and 28-day monthly heatmap', () => {
    const dates = [yesterdayKey, todayKey];
    const data = StreakEngine.getStreakData('student-1', [], dates, refDate);

    assert.strictEqual(data.weeklyStrip.length, 7);
    assert.strictEqual(data.monthlyHeatmap.length, 28);
    assert.strictEqual(data.weeklyStrip[6].isToday, true);
    assert.strictEqual(data.weeklyStrip[6].completed, true);
    assert.strictEqual(data.weeklyStrip[5].completed, true);
  });
});

/**
 * Streak Calculation Engine for C/C++ Daily Coding Practice
 * Computes consecutive days of assessment activity, activity calendar,
 * milestones, and streak status.
 */

import { AssessmentSubmission } from './types';

export interface DayActivity {
  date: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
  dayLabel: string; // e.g. "Mon", "Tue"
  dayShort: string; // e.g. "M", "T"
  displayDate: string; // e.g. "Nov 1"
  isToday: boolean;
  completed: boolean;
  submissionCount: number;
  languages: ('cpp' | 'c')[];
  bestScore?: number;
}

export interface StreakMilestone {
  days: number;
  title: string;
  badge: string;
  description: string;
  unlocked: boolean;
}

export interface StreakData {
  currentStreak: number;
  longestStreak: number;
  totalActiveDays: number;
  hasPracticedToday: boolean;
  status: 'ACTIVE_TODAY' | 'AT_RISK' | 'INACTIVE';
  streakFreezesAvailable: number;
  weeklyStrip: DayActivity[];
  monthlyHeatmap: DayActivity[];
  milestones: StreakMilestone[];
  nextMilestone: StreakMilestone | null;
  dailyChallengeRecommendation: {
    id: string;
    title: string;
    language: 'cpp' | 'c';
    difficulty: 'Beginner' | 'Intermediate' | 'Advanced';
    timeLimitMinutes: number;
  };
}

/**
 * Format a Date object to YYYY-MM-DD in local time
 */
export function formatToDateKey(date: Date): string {
  const year = date.getFullYear();
  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
}

/**
 * Parse various date formats (ISO, YYYY-MM-DD, or localized strings)
 */
export function parseDateToKey(dateStr: string): string | null {
  if (!dateStr) return null;
  
  // If already YYYY-MM-DD at start
  const isoMatch = dateStr.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
  if (isoMatch) {
    return `${isoMatch[1]}-${isoMatch[2]}-${isoMatch[3]}`;
  }

  // Handle local date string like "10/31/2026, 11:15 AM" or "10/31/2026"
  const parsed = new Date(dateStr);
  if (!isNaN(parsed.getTime())) {
    return formatToDateKey(parsed);
  }

  return null;
}

export class StreakEngine {
  /**
   * Defined milestone ladder for C/C++ coding consistency
   */
  public static readonly MILESTONES: StreakMilestone[] = [
    {
      days: 3,
      title: 'Consistency Bronze',
      badge: '🥉',
      description: '3 consecutive days of C/C++ problem solving.',
      unlocked: false,
    },
    {
      days: 7,
      title: '7-Day C++ Sprint',
      badge: '⚡',
      description: '1 full week of daily memory safety & algorithms practice.',
      unlocked: false,
    },
    {
      days: 14,
      title: 'Algorithmic Master',
      badge: '🏆',
      description: '2 weeks of continuous dynamic programming and graph traversals.',
      unlocked: false,
    },
    {
      days: 30,
      title: 'Kernel Architect',
      badge: '🛡️',
      description: '1 month of flawless daily C/C++ systems engineering.',
      unlocked: false,
    },
  ];

  /**
   * Extract distinct active dates and metadata for a specific candidate
   */
  public static extractActiveDateMap(
    candidateId: string,
    submissions: AssessmentSubmission[],
    additionalPracticeDates: string[] = []
  ): Map<string, { count: number; languages: Set<'cpp' | 'c'>; bestScore: number }> {
    const map = new Map<string, { count: number; languages: Set<'cpp' | 'c'>; bestScore: number }>();

    // Filter submissions for this candidate
    const candidateSubmissions = submissions.filter((s) => s.candidateId === candidateId);

    for (const sub of candidateSubmissions) {
      const dateKey = parseDateToKey(sub.submittedAt);
      if (!dateKey) continue;

      const entry = map.get(dateKey) || {
        count: 0,
        languages: new Set<'cpp' | 'c'>(),
        bestScore: 0,
      };

      entry.count += 1;
      if (sub.language) entry.languages.add(sub.language);
      if (sub.score > entry.bestScore) entry.bestScore = sub.score;

      map.set(dateKey, entry);
    }

    // Add additional practice dates (from localStorage or seeds)
    for (const dateStr of additionalPracticeDates) {
      const dateKey = parseDateToKey(dateStr);
      if (!dateKey) continue;

      if (!map.has(dateKey)) {
        map.set(dateKey, {
          count: 1,
          languages: new Set(['cpp']),
          bestScore: 90,
        });
      }
    }

    return map;
  }

  /**
   * Calculate consecutive streak days ending today or yesterday
   */
  public static calculateStreakDays(
    activeDates: Set<string>,
    referenceDate: Date = new Date()
  ): {
    currentStreak: number;
    hasPracticedToday: boolean;
    status: 'ACTIVE_TODAY' | 'AT_RISK' | 'INACTIVE';
  } {
    const todayKey = formatToDateKey(referenceDate);

    // Yesterday's date
    const yesterday = new Date(referenceDate);
    yesterday.setDate(yesterday.getDate() - 1);
    const yesterdayKey = formatToDateKey(yesterday);

    const hasPracticedToday = activeDates.has(todayKey);
    const hasPracticedYesterday = activeDates.has(yesterdayKey);

    let currentStreak = 0;
    let status: 'ACTIVE_TODAY' | 'AT_RISK' | 'INACTIVE' = 'INACTIVE';

    if (hasPracticedToday) {
      status = 'ACTIVE_TODAY';
      currentStreak = 1;
      // Walk back from yesterday
      let checkDate = new Date(yesterday);
      while (activeDates.has(formatToDateKey(checkDate))) {
        currentStreak++;
        checkDate.setDate(checkDate.getDate() - 1);
      }
    } else if (hasPracticedYesterday) {
      status = 'AT_RISK';
      currentStreak = 1;
      // Walk back from 2 days ago
      let checkDate = new Date(yesterday);
      checkDate.setDate(checkDate.getDate() - 1);
      while (activeDates.has(formatToDateKey(checkDate))) {
        currentStreak++;
        checkDate.setDate(checkDate.getDate() - 1);
      }
    } else {
      status = 'INACTIVE';
      currentStreak = 0;
    }

    return {
      currentStreak,
      hasPracticedToday,
      status,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Calculate longest streak across all recorded history
   */
  public static calculateLongestStreak(activeDates: Set<string>): number {
    if (activeDates.size === 0) return 0;

    const sortedDates = Array.from(activeDates).sort();
    let maxStreak = 1;
    let current = 1;

    for (let i = 1; i < sortedDates.length; i++) {
      const prevDate = new Date(sortedDates[i - 1]);
      const currDate = new Date(sortedDates[i]);
      const diffMs = currDate.getTime() - prevDate.getTime();
      const diffDays = Math.round(diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));

      if (diffDays === 1) {
        current++;
        if (current > maxStreak) maxStreak = current;
      } else if (diffDays > 1) {
        current = 1;
      }
    }

    return maxStreak;
  }

  /**
   * Generate 7-day rolling window
   */
  public static generateWeeklyStrip(
    activeMap: Map<string, { count: number; languages: Set<'cpp' | 'c'>; bestScore: number }>,
    referenceDate: Date = new Date()
  ): DayActivity[] {
    const days: DayActivity[] = [];
    const dayNames = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'];
    const shortNames = ['S', 'M', 'T', 'W', 'T', 'F', 'S'];

    for (let i = 6; i >= 0; i--) {
      const d = new Date(referenceDate);
      d.setDate(d.getDate() - i);

      const dateKey = formatToDateKey(d);
      const isToday = i === 0;
      const entry = activeMap.get(dateKey);

      days.push({
        date: dateKey,
        dayLabel: dayNames[d.getDay()],
        dayShort: shortNames[d.getDay()],
        displayDate: d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }),
        isToday,
        completed: !!entry && entry.count > 0,
        submissionCount: entry ? entry.count : 0,
        languages: entry ? Array.from(entry.languages) : [],
        bestScore: entry?.bestScore,
      });
    }

    return days;
  }

  /**
   * Generate 28-day monthly activity heatmap (4 rolling weeks)
   */
  public static generateMonthlyHeatmap(
    activeMap: Map<string, { count: number; languages: Set<'cpp' | 'c'>; bestScore: number }>,
    referenceDate: Date = new Date()
  ): DayActivity[] {
    const days: DayActivity[] = [];
    const dayNames = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'];
    const shortNames = ['S', 'M', 'T', 'W', 'T', 'F', 'S'];

    for (let i = 27; i >= 0; i--) {
      const d = new Date(referenceDate);
      d.setDate(d.getDate() - i);

      const dateKey = formatToDateKey(d);
      const isToday = i === 0;
      const entry = activeMap.get(dateKey);

      days.push({
        date: dateKey,
        dayLabel: dayNames[d.getDay()],
        dayShort: shortNames[d.getDay()],
        displayDate: d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }),
        isToday,
        completed: !!entry && entry.count > 0,
        submissionCount: entry ? entry.count : 0,
        languages: entry ? Array.from(entry.languages) : [],
        bestScore: entry?.bestScore,
      });
    }

    return days;
  }

  /**
   * Main computation producing all streak data for a candidate
   */
  public static getStreakData(
    candidateId: string,
    submissions: AssessmentSubmission[],
    additionalPracticeDates: string[] = [],
    referenceDate: Date = new Date()
  ): StreakData {
    const activeMap = this.extractActiveDateMap(candidateId, submissions, additionalPracticeDates);
    const dateSet = new Set(activeMap.keys());

    const { currentStreak, hasPracticedToday, status } = this.calculateStreakDays(dateSet, referenceDate);
    const longestStreak = Math.max(currentStreak, this.calculateLongestStreak(dateSet));
    const totalActiveDays = dateSet.size;

    const weeklyStrip = this.generateWeeklyStrip(activeMap, referenceDate);
    const monthlyHeatmap = this.generateMonthlyHeatmap(activeMap, referenceDate);

    // Compute unlocked milestones
    const milestones = this.MILESTONES.map((m) => ({
      ...m,
      unlocked: currentStreak >= m.days || longestStreak >= m.days,
    }));

    const nextMilestone = milestones.find((m) => !m.unlocked) || null;

    // Daily featured challenge recommendation
    const dailyChallengeRecommendation = {
      id: 'c-pointer-string-reverse',
      title: 'In-Place String & Pointer Arithmetic',
      language: 'c' as const,
      difficulty: 'Intermediate' as const,
      timeLimitMinutes: 15,
    };

    return {
      currentStreak,
      longestStreak,
      totalActiveDays,
      hasPracticedToday,
      status,
      streakFreezesAvailable: 1, // 1 shield token available for exam preparation
      weeklyStrip,
      monthlyHeatmap,
      milestones,
      nextMilestone,
      dailyChallengeRecommendation,
    };
  }
}
